





|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau

tikanipātapāḷi


1. paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. bālavaggau

1. bhayasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai bhaddantai (ka॰)’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yāni kānici, bhikkhavai, bhayāni uppaj‍janti sabbāni tāni bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau  paṇḍitatau| yai kaici upaddavā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau| yai kaici upasaggā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, naḷāgārā vā tiṇāgārā vā naḷāgāraṃ vā tiṇāgāraṃ vā (sī॰) aggi muttau aggimuk‍kau (sī॰), aggi muk‍kau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kūṭāgārānipi ḍahati ul‍littāvalittāni nivātāni phusitaggaḷāni pihitavātapānāni; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici bhayāni uppaj‍janti sabbāni tāni bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau| yai kaici upaddavā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau| yai kaici upasaggā uppaj‍janti sabbai tai bālatau uppaj‍janti, nau paṇḍitatau|

‘‘iti  khau, bhikkhavai, sappaṭibhayau  bālau, appaṭibhayau paṇḍitau| saupaddavau bālau, anupaddavau paṇḍitau| saupasaggau bālau, anupasaggau paṇḍitau| natthi, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitatau bhayaṃ, natthi paṇḍitatau upaddavau, natthi paṇḍitatau upasaggau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaihi tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau tai tayau dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā, yaihi tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau tai tayau dhammai samādāya vattissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. lakkhaṇasuttaṃ

2. ‘‘kammalakkhaṇau, bhikkhavai, bālau, kammalakkhaṇau paṇḍitau, apadānasaubhanī apadānai saubhati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pañ‍ñāti pañ‍ñatti (?)| tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? kāyaduc‍caritaina, vacīduc‍caritaina, manauduc‍caritaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? kāyasucaritaina, vacīsucaritaina, manausucaritaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaihi tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau  tai tayau dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā, yaihi tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau tai tayau dhammai samādāya vattissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. cintīsuttaṃ

3. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, bālassa bālalakkhaṇāni bālanimittāni bālāpadānāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, bālau duc‍cintitacintī ca hauti dubbhāsitabhāsī ca duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca| nau caidaṃ nau caitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, bālau duc‍cintitacintī ca abhavissa dubbhāsitabhāsī ca duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca, kaina naṃ paṇḍitā jānaiyyuṃ taina naṃ paṇḍitā na jānaiyyuṃ (ka॰), na naṃ paṇḍitā jānaiyyuṃ (?) – ‘bālau ayaṃ bhavaṃ asappurisau’ti? yasmā  ca khau, bhikkhavai, bālau duc‍cintitacintī ca hauti dubbhāsitabhāsī ca duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca tasmā naṃ paṇḍitā jānanti – ‘bālau ayaṃ bhavaṃ asappurisau’ti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi bālassa bālalakkhaṇāni bālanimittāni bālāpadānāni|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa paṇḍitalakkhaṇāni paṇḍitanimittāni paṇḍitāpadānāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sucintitacintī ca hauti subhāsitabhāsī ca sukatakammakārī ca| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sucintitacintī ca abhavissa subhāsitabhāsī ca sukatakammakārī ca, kaina naṃ paṇḍitā jānaiyyuṃ – ‘paṇḍitau ayaṃ bhavaṃ sappurisau’ti? yasmā  ca khau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau sucintitacintī ca hauti subhāsitabhāsī ca sukatakammakārī ca tasmā naṃ paṇḍitā jānanti – ‘paṇḍitau ayaṃ bhavaṃ sappurisau’ti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi paṇḍitassa paṇḍitalakkhaṇāni paṇḍitanimittāni paṇḍitāpadānāni| tasmātiha…| tatiyaṃ|

4. ac‍cayasuttaṃ

4. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau na passati, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ nappaṭikarauti, parassa khau  pana ac‍cayaṃ daisaintassa yathādhammaṃ nappaṭiggaṇhāti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau passati, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, parassa khau pana ac‍cayaṃ daisaintassa yathādhammaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau| tasmātiha…| catutthaṃ|

5. ayaunisausuttaṃ

5. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? ayaunisau pañhaṃ kattā hauti, ayaunisau pañhaṃ vissaj‍jaitā hauti, parassa khau pana yaunisau pañhaṃ vissaj‍jitaṃ parimaṇḍalaihi  padabyañ‍janaihi siliṭṭhaihi upagataihi nābbhanumauditā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? yaunisau pañhaṃ kattā hauti, yaunisau pañhaṃ vissaj‍jaitā hauti, parassa khau pana yaunisau pañhaṃ vissaj‍jitaṃ parimaṇḍalaihi padabyañ‍janaihi siliṭṭhaihi upagataihi abbhanumauditā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau| tasmātiha…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. akusalasuttaṃ

6. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau| katamaihi  tīhi? akusalaina  kāyakammaina, akusalaina vacīkammaina, akusalaina manaukammaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? kusalaina kāyakammaina, kusalaina vacīkammaina, kusalaina manaukammaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau| tasmātiha…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sāvaj‍jasuttaṃ

7. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? sāvaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, sāvaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, sāvaj‍jaina manaukammaina…pai॰… anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. sabyābajjhasuttaṃ

8. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? sabyābajjhaina kāyakammaina, sabyābajjhaina vacīkammaina, sabyābajjhaina manaukammaina…pai॰… abyābajjhaina kāyakammaina, abyābajjhaina vacīkammaina, abyābajjhaina manaukammaina| imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ  sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaihi tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau vaiditabbau tai tayau dhammai abhinivaj‍jaitvā, yaihi tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau vaiditabbau tai tayau dhammai samādāya vattissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. khatasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi tīhi? kāyaduc‍caritaina, vacīduc‍caritaina, manauduc‍caritaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi tīhi? kāyasucaritaina, vacīsucaritaina, manausucaritaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. malasuttaṃ

10. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau tayau malai appahāya yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi tīhi? dussīlau ca hauti, dussīlyamalañ‍cassa appahīnaṃ  hauti; issukī ca hauti, issāmalañ‍cassa appahīnaṃ hauti; maccharī ca hauti, macchairamalañ‍cassa appahīnaṃ hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau imai tayau malai appahāya yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau tayau malai pahāya yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi tīhi? sīlavā ca hauti, dussīlyamalañ‍cassa pahīnaṃ hauti; anissukī ca hauti, issāmalañ‍cassa pahīnaṃ hauti; amaccharī ca hauti, macchairamalañ‍cassa pahīnaṃ hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau imai tayau malai pahāya yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

bālavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

bhayaṃ lakkhaṇacintī ca, ac‍cayañ‍ca ayaunisau|

akusalañ‍ca sāvaj‍jaṃ, sabyābajjhakhataṃ malanti||


2. rathakāravaggau

1. ñātasuttaṃ

11. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ñātau bhikkhu bahujanaahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanadukkhāya, bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? ananulaumikai kāyakammai samādapaiti, ananulaumikai vacīkammai samādapaiti, ananulaumikaisu dhammaisu samādapaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ñātau bhikkhu bahujanaahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanadukkhāya, bahunau janassa  anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ñātau bhikkhu bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanasukhāya, bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? anulaumikai kāyakammai samādapaiti, anulaumikai vacīkammai samādapaiti, anulaumikaisu dhammaisu samādapaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ñātau bhikkhu bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanasukhāya, bahunau janassa atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sāraṇīyasuttaṃ

12. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyāni saraṇīyāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavanti| katamāni tīṇi? yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, padaisai rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau jātau hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ padaisai rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ padaisai rājā khattiyau muddhāvasittau saṅgāmaṃ abhivijinitvā vijitasaṅgāmau tamaiva saṅgāmasīsaṃ ajjhāvasati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ hauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyāni bhavanti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇimāni bhikkhussa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyāni bhavanti| katamāni tīṇi? yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, padaisai bhikkhu kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajitau hauti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ bhikkhussa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ padaisai bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ bhikkhussa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ padaisai bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ bhikkhussa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ hauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi bhikkhussa yāvajīvaṃ sāraṇīyāni bhavantī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. āsaṃsasuttaṃ

13. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? nirāsau, āsaṃsau, vigatāsau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai puggalau nirāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau hauti, caṇḍālakulai vā vainakulai vaiṇakulai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā naisādakulai vā  rathakārakulai vā puk‍kusakulai vā daliddai appan‍napānabhaujanai kasiravuttikai, yattha kasiraina ghāsacchādau labbhati| sau ca hauti dubbaṇṇau duddasikau aukauṭimakau bavhābādhau bahvābādhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) kāṇau vā kuṇī vā khañ‍jau vā pakkhahatau vā, na lābhī an‍nassa pānassa vatthassa yānassa mālāgandhavilaipanassa saiyyāvasathapadīpaiyyassa| sau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau kira khattiyau khattiyaihi khattiyābhisaikaina abhisittau’ti| tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘kudāssu nāma mampi  khattiyā khattiyābhisaikaina abhisiñ‍cissantī’ti! ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau nirāsau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau āsaṃsau? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa jaiṭṭhau puttau hauti ābhisaikau anabhisittau acalappattau macalappattau (sī॰ pī॰)| sau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau kira khattiyau khattiyaihi khattiyābhisaikaina abhisittau’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kudāssu nāma mampi khattiyā khattiyābhisaikaina abhisiñ‍cissantī’ti! ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau āsaṃsau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau vigatāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, rājā hauti khattiyau muddhāvasittau| sau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau kira khattiyau khattiyaihi khattiyābhisaikaina abhisittau’ti| tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘kudāssu nāma mampi khattiyā khattiyābhisaikaina abhisiñ‍cissantī’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? yā hissa, bhikkhavai, pubbai anabhisittassa abhisaikāsā sā sāssa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭippassaddhā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau vigatāsau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā bhikkhūsu| katamai tayau? nirāsau, āsaṃsau, vigatāsau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau nirāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau asuci saṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti avassutau kasambujātau| sau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau kira bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘kudāssu nāma ahampi āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissāmī’ti! ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau nirāsau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau āsaṃsau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti kalyāṇadhammau| sau suṇāti  āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘kudāssu nāma ahampi āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissāmī’ti! ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau āsaṃsau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau vigatāsau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ hauti khīṇāsavau| sau suṇāti – ‘itthan‍nāmau kira bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘kudāssu nāma ahampi āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissāmī’ti! taṃ kissa haitu? yā hissa, bhikkhavai, pubbai avimuttassa vimuttāsā sā paṭippassaddhā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau vigatāsau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā bhikkhūsū’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. cak‍kavattisuttaṃ

14. ‘‘yaupi  sau, bhikkhavai, rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā saupi na arājakaṃ cak‍kaṃ vattaitī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘kau pana, bhantai, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa dhammikassa dhammarañ‍ñau rājā’’ti cak‍kanti (ka॰)? ‘‘dhammau, bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā dhammaṃyaiva nissāya garukarauntau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati antaujanasmiṃ’’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā dhammaṃyaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ  rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati khattiyaisu, anuyantaisu anuyuttaisu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), balakāyasmiṃ, brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu, naigamajānapadaisu, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu, migapakkhīsu| sa khau sau bhikkhu rājā cak‍kavattī dhammikau dhammarājā dhammaṃyaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā antaujanasmiṃ, dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā khattiyaisu, anuyantaisu, balakāyasmiṃ, brāhmaṇagahapatikaisu, naigamajānapadaisu, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu, migapakkhīsu, dhammainaiva cak‍kaṃ vattaiti| taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ kainaci manussabhūtaina pac‍catthikaina pāṇinā|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhu bhikkhavai (ka॰), tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammikau dhammarājā dhammaṃyaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau  dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati kāyakammasmiṃ – ‘aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ kāyakammaṃ na saivitabba’’’nti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhu, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammikau dhammarājā dhammaṃyaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahati vacīkammasmiṃ – ‘aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ vacīkammaṃ na saivitabba’nti…pai॰… manaukammasmiṃ – ‘aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ saivitabbaṃ, aivarūpaṃ manaukammaṃ na saivitabba’’’nti|

‘‘sa  khau sau, bhikkhu, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammikau dhammarājā dhammaṃyaiva nissāya dhammaṃ sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaddhajau dhammakaitu dhammādhipataiyyau dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā kāyakammasmiṃ, dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā vacīkammasmiṃ, dhammikaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiṃ saṃvidahitvā manaukammasmiṃ, dhammainaiva anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaiti| taṃ hauti cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmi’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. sacaitanasuttaṃ

15. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti  tai  bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā ahausi sacaitanau pacaitanau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nāma| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā sacaitanau rathakāraṃ āmantaisi – ‘itau mai, samma rathakāra, chan‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina saṅgāmau bhavissati| sakkhissasi sakkhasi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mai, samma rathakāra, navaṃ cak‍kayugaṃ kātu’nti? ‘sak‍kaumi daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, rathakārau rañ‍ñau sacaitanassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rathakārau chahi māsaihi chārattūnaihi aikaṃ cak‍kaṃ niṭṭhāpaisi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, rājā sacaitanau rathakāraṃ āmantaisi – ‘itau mai, samma rathakāra, chan‍naṃ divasānaṃ ac‍cayaina saṅgāmau bhavissati, niṭṭhitaṃ navaṃ cak‍kayuga’nti? ‘imaihi khau, daiva, chahi māsaihi chārattūnaihi aikaṃ cak‍kaṃ niṭṭhita’nti| ‘sakkhissasi pana mai, samma rathakāra, imaihi chahi divasaihi dutiyaṃ cak‍kaṃ niṭṭhāpaitu’nti? ‘sak‍kaumi daivā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, rathakārau chahi divasaihi dutiyaṃ cak‍kaṃ niṭṭhāpaitvā navaṃ cak‍kayugaṃ ādāya yaina rājā sacaitanau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ sacaitanaṃ aitadavauca – ‘idaṃ tai, daiva, navaṃ cak‍kayugaṃ niṭṭhita’nti| ‘yañ‍ca tai idaṃ, samma rathakāra, cak‍kaṃ chahi māsaihi niṭṭhitaṃ chārattūnaihi yañ‍ca tai idaṃ cak‍kaṃ chahi divasaihi niṭṭhitaṃ, imaisaṃ kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ? naisāhaṃ kiñ‍ci nānākaraṇaṃ passāmī’ti| ‘atthaisaṃ, daiva, nānākaraṇaṃ| passatu daivau nānākaraṇa’’’nti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, rathakārau yaṃ taṃ cak‍kaṃ chahi divasaihi niṭṭhitaṃ taṃ pavattaisi| taṃ pavattitaṃ samānaṃ yāvatikā abhisaṅkhārassa gati tāvatikaṃ gantvā ciṅgulāyitvā  bhūmiyaṃ papati| yaṃ pana taṃ cak‍kaṃ chahi māsaihi niṭṭhitaṃ  chārattūnaihi taṃ pavattaisi| taṃ pavattitaṃ samānaṃ yāvatikā abhisaṅkhārassa gati tāvatikaṃ gantvā akkhāhataṃ mañ‍ñai aṭṭhāsi|

‘‘‘kau nu khau, samma rathakāra, haitu kau pac‍cayau yamidaṃ yadidaṃ (ka॰) cak‍kaṃ chahi divasaihi niṭṭhitaṃ taṃ pavattitaṃ samānaṃ yāvatikā abhisaṅkhārassa gati tāvatikaṃ gantvā ciṅgulāyitvā bhūmiyaṃ  papati? kau pana, samma rathakāra, haitu kau pac‍cayau yamidaṃ cak‍kaṃ chahi māsaihi niṭṭhitaṃ chārattūnaihi taṃ pavattitaṃ samānaṃ yāvatikā abhisaṅkhārassa gati tāvatikaṃ gantvā akkhāhataṃ mañ‍ñai aṭṭhāsī’ti? ‘yamidaṃ, daiva, cak‍kaṃ chahi divasaihi niṭṭhitaṃ tassa naimipi savaṅkā sadausā sakasāvā, arāpi savaṅkā sadausā sakasāvā, nābhipi savaṅkā sadausā sakasāvā| taṃ naimiyāpi savaṅkattā sadausattā sakasāvattā, arānampi savaṅkattā sadausattā sakasāvattā, nābhiyāpi savaṅkattā sadausattā sakasāvattā pavattitaṃ samānaṃ yāvatikā abhisaṅkhārassa gati tāvatikaṃ gantvā ciṅgulāyitvā bhūmiyaṃ papati| yaṃ pana taṃ, daiva, cak‍kaṃ chahi māsaihi niṭṭhitaṃ chārattūnaihi tassa naimipi avaṅkā adausā akasāvā, arāpi avaṅkā adausā akasāvā, nābhipi avaṅkā adausā akasāvā| taṃ naimiyāpi avaṅkattā adausattā akasāvattā, arānampi avaṅkattā adausattā akasāvattā, nābhiyāpi avaṅkattā adausattā akasāvattā pavattitaṃ samānaṃ yāvatikā abhisaṅkhārassa gati tāvatikaṃ gantvā akkhāhataṃ mañ‍ñai aṭṭhāsī’’’ti|

‘‘siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘añ‍ñau nūna taina samayaina sau  rathakārau ahausī’ti! na khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| ahaṃ taina samayaina sau rathakārau ahausiṃ| tadāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kusalau dāruvaṅkānaṃ dārudausānaṃ dārukasāvānaṃ| aitarahi khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kusalau kāyavaṅkānaṃ kāyadausānaṃ  kāyakasāvānaṃ, kusalau vacīvaṅkānaṃ vacīdausānaṃ vacīkasāvānaṃ, kusalau manauvaṅkānaṃ manaudausānaṃ manaukasāvānaṃ| yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā kāyavaṅkau appahīnau kāyadausau kāyakasāvau, vacīvaṅkau appahīnau vacīdausau vacīkasāvau, manauvaṅkau appahīnau manaudausau manaukasāvau, aivaṃ papatitā tai, bhikkhavai, imasmā dhammavinayā, saiyyathāpi taṃ cak‍kaṃ chahi divasaihi niṭṭhitaṃ|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā kāyavaṅkau pahīnau kāyadausau kāyakasāvau, vacīvaṅkau pahīnau vacīdausau vacīkasāvau, manauvaṅkau pahīnau manaudausau manaukasāvau, aivaṃ patiṭṭhitā tai, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, saiyyathāpi taṃ cak‍kaṃ chahi māsaihi niṭṭhitaṃ chārattūnaihi|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kāyavaṅkaṃ pajahissāma kāyadausaṃ kāyakasāvaṃ, vacīvaṅkaṃ pajahissāma vacīdausaṃ vacīkasāvaṃ, manauvaṅkaṃ pajahissāma manaudausaṃ manaukasāva’nti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. apaṇṇakasuttaṃ

16. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu apaṇṇakapaṭipadaṃ apaṇṇakataṃ paṭipadaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) ṭīkāya pana samaiti paṭipan‍nau hauti, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāya| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ yatvādhikaraṇamaitaṃ (sī॰) cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ  manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu indriyaisu guttadvārau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti, rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina  nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti, rattiyā  majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā, rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu jāgariyaṃ anuyuttau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu apaṇṇakapaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, yauni cassa āraddhā hauti āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. attabyābādhasuttaṃ

17. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti| katamai tayau? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau dhammā attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, dhammā naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti| katamai tayau? kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau  dhammā naivattabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, na parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattanti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattantī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. daivalaukasuttaṃ

18. ‘‘sacai  vau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘daivalaukūpapattiyā, āvusau, samaṇai gautamai brahmacariyaṃ vussathā’ti? nanu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ puṭṭhā aṭṭīyaiyyātha harāyaiyyātha jigucchaiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, dibbaina āyunā aṭṭīyatha harāyatha jigucchatha, dibbaina vaṇṇaina dibbaina sukhaina  dibbaina yasaina dibbainādhipataiyyaina aṭṭīyatha harāyatha jigucchatha; pagaiva khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhaihi kāyaduc‍caritaina aṭṭīyitabbaṃ harāyitabbaṃ jigucchitabbaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaina… manauduc‍caritaina aṭṭīyitabbaṃ harāyitabbaṃ jigucchitabba’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamapāpaṇikasuttaṃ

19. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpaṇikau abhabbau anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ majjhantikasamayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) na sak‍kac‍caṃ kammantaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, majjhanhikasamayaṃ na sak‍kac‍caṃ kammantaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, sāyanhasamayaṃ na sak‍kac‍caṃ kammantaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpaṇikau abhabbau anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ phātikattuṃ (sī॰), phātikātuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ na sak‍kac‍caṃ samādhinimittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, majjhanhikasamayaṃ na sak‍kac‍caṃ samādhinimittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, sāyanhasamayaṃ na sak‍kac‍caṃ samādhinimittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpaṇikau bhabbau anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ kammantaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, majjhanhikasamayaṃ…pai॰… sāyanhasamayaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ kammantaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpaṇikau bhabbau anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ samādhinimittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, majjhanhikasamayaṃ…pai॰… sāyanhasamayaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ samādhinimittaṃ adhiṭṭhāti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anadhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ adhigantuṃ, adhigataṃ vā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ phātiṃ kātu’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyapāpaṇikasuttaṃ

20. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpaṇikau nacirassaiva mahattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ mahantattaṃ vā vaipul‍lattaṃ vā (pī॰ ka॰) pāpuṇāti bhaugaisu| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau cakkhumā ca hauti vidhurau ca nissayasampan‍nau ca| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau cakkhumā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau paṇiyaṃ jānāti – ‘idaṃ paṇiyaṃ aivaṃ kītaṃ, aivaṃ vik‍kayamānaṃ vik‍kīyamānaṃ (?), aittakaṃ mūlaṃ bhavissati, aittakau udayau’ti uddayauti (sī॰)| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau cakkhumā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau vidhurau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau kusalau hauti paṇiyaṃ kaituñ‍ca vik‍kaituñ‍ca| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau vidhurau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau nissayasampan‍nau hauti? idha  bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikaṃ yai tai gahapatī vā gahapatiputtā vā aḍḍhā mahaddhanā mahābhaugā tai aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ pāpaṇikau cakkhumā vidhurau  ca paṭibalau puttadārañ‍ca pausaituṃ, amhākañ‍ca kālaina kālaṃ anuppadātu’nti| tai naṃ bhaugaihi nipatanti – ‘itau, samma pāpaṇika, bhaugai karitvā haritvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) puttadārañ‍ca pausaihi, amhākañ‍ca kālaina kālaṃ anuppadaihī’ti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpaṇikau nissayasampan‍nau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpaṇikau nacirassaiva mahattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti bhaugaisu|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva mahattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti kusalaisu dhammaisu| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhumā ca hauti vidhurau ca nissayasampan‍nau ca| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhumā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhumā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vidhurau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati  akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vidhurau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nissayasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati  – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ, imassa kau atthau’ti? tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttānīkaraunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nissayasampan‍nau hauti| imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nacirassaiva mahattaṃ vaipul‍lattaṃ pāpuṇāti kusalaisu dhammaisū’’ti| dasamaṃ|

rathakāravaggau dutiyau|


paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ñātau  ñātakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sāraṇīyau bhikkhu, cak‍kavattī sacaitanau|

apaṇṇakattā daivau ca, duvai pāpaṇikaina cāti||


3. puggalavaggau

1. samiddhasuttaṃ

21. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ca samiddhau saviṭṭhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau mahākauṭṭhitau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ samiddhaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘tayaumai, āvusau samiddha, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau diṭṭhappattau (ka॰), saddhāvimuttau| imai khau, āvusau, tayau puggalā santau  saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, āvusau, tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ katamau tai puggalau khamati abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘tayaumai, āvusau sāriputta, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau| imai khau, āvusau, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, āvusau, tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ yvāyaṃ yauyaṃ (ka॰) puggalau saddhāvimuttau, ayaṃ mai puggalau khamati imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? imassa, āvusau, puggalassa saddhindriyaṃ adhimatta’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tayaumai, āvusau kauṭṭhika, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau| imai khau, āvusau, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, āvusau, tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ katamau tai puggalau khamati abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘tayaumai, āvusau sāriputta, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau| imai khau, āvusau, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, āvusau, tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ yvāyaṃ puggalau kāyasakkhī, ayaṃ mai puggalau khamati imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? imassa, āvusau, puggalassa samādhindriyaṃ adhimatta’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tayaumai, āvusau sāriputta, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau| imai khau, āvusau, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, āvusau, tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ katamau tai puggalau khamati abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘tayaumai, āvusau kauṭṭhika, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? kāyasakkhī, diṭṭhippattau, saddhāvimuttau| imai khau, āvusau, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ, āvusau, tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ yvāyaṃ puggalau diṭṭhippattau, ayaṃ mai puggalau khamati imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau ca| taṃ kissa haitu? imassa, āvusau, puggalassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ adhimatta’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantañ‍ca samiddhaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau, amhaihi sabbaihaiva yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| āyāmāvusau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaissāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā ca samiddhau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmatau sāriputtassa  pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca samiddhau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau yāvatakau ahausi āyasmatā ca samiddhaina āyasmatā ca mahākauṭṭhikaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ  bhagavatau āraucaisi|

‘‘na  khvaittha, sāriputta, sukaraṃ aikaṃsaina byākātuṃ – ‘ayaṃ imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’ti| ṭhānañhaitaṃ, sāriputta, vij‍jati yvāyaṃ puggalau saddhāvimuttau svāssa svāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sauyaṃ (ka॰) arahattāya paṭipan‍nau, yvāyaṃ puggalau kāyasakkhī svāssa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā, yau cāyaṃ puggalau diṭṭhippattau saupassa sauyaṃ (ka॰) sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā|

‘‘na khvaittha, sāriputta, sukaraṃ aikaṃsaina byākātuṃ – ‘ayaṃ imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’ti| ṭhānañhaitaṃ, sāriputta, vij‍jati yvāyaṃ puggalau kāyasakkhī svāssa arahattāya paṭipan‍nau, yvāyaṃ puggalau saddhāvimuttau svāssa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā, yau cāyaṃ puggalau diṭṭhippattau saupassa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā|

‘‘na khvaittha, sāriputta, sukaraṃ aikaṃsaina byākātuṃ – ‘ayaṃ imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’ti| ṭhānañhaitaṃ, sāriputta, vij‍jati yvāyaṃ puggalau diṭṭhippattau svāssa arahattāya paṭipan‍nau, yvāyaṃ puggalau saddhāvimuttau svāssa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā, yau cāyaṃ puggalau kāyasakkhī saupassa sakadāgāmī vā anāgāmī vā|

‘‘na khvaittha, sāriputta, sukaraṃ aikaṃsaina byākātuṃ – ‘ayaṃ imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ abhik‍kantatarau ca paṇītatarau cā’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. gilānasuttaṃ

22. pu॰ pa॰ 94 ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, gilānā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau gilānau labhantau vā sappāyāni bhaujanāni alabhantau vā sappāyāni bhaujanāni, labhantau vā sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni  alabhantau vā sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, labhantau vā  patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ alabhantau vā patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ naiva vuṭṭhāti tamhā ābādhā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau gilānau labhantau vā sappāyāni bhaujanāni alabhantau vā sappāyāni bhaujanāni, labhantau vā sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni alabhantau vā sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni, labhantau vā patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ alabhantau vā patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ vuṭṭhāti tamhā ābādhā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau gilānau labhantauva sappāyāni bhaujanāni nau alabhantau, labhantauva sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni nau alabhantau, labhantauva patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ nau alabhantau vuṭṭhāti tamhā ābādhā|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ gilānau labhantauva sappāyāni bhaujanāni nau alabhantau, labhantauva sappāyāni bhaisaj‍jāni nau alabhantau, labhantauva patirūpaṃ upaṭṭhākaṃ nau alabhantau vuṭṭhāti tamhā ābādhā, imaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, gilānaṃ paṭic‍ca gilānabhattaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ gilānabhaisaj‍jaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ gilānupaṭṭhākau anuñ‍ñātau| imañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, gilānaṃ paṭic‍ca añ‍ñaipi gilānā upaṭṭhātabbā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau gilānā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tayaumai gilānūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau labhantau vā tathāgataṃ dassanāya alabhantau vā tathāgataṃ dassanāya, labhantau vā tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ savanāya alabhantau vā tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ  savanāya naiva auk‍kamati niyāmaṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘idha, pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau labhantau vā tathāgataṃ dassanāya alabhantau vā tathāgataṃ dassanāya, labhantau vā tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ savanāya alabhantau vā tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ savanāya auk‍kamati niyāmaṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau labhantauva tathāgataṃ dassanāya nau alabhantau, labhantauva tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ savanāya nau alabhantau auk‍kamati niyāmaṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ puggalau labhantauva tathāgataṃ dassanāya nau alabhantau, labhantauva  tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ savanāya nau alabhantau auk‍kamati niyāmaṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ, imaṃ khau bhikkhavai, puggalaṃ paṭic‍ca dhammadaisanā anuñ‍ñātā| imañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, puggalaṃ paṭic‍ca añ‍ñaisampi dhammau daisaitabbau| ‘‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau gilānūpamā puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saṅkhārasuttaṃ

23. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sabyābajjhaṃ sabyāpajjhaṃ (sabbattha) aivamuparipi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ  upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayati aikantadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi sattā nairayikā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau abyābajjhaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, abyābajjhaṃ manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ abyābajjhaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ abyābajjhā phassā phusanti| sau abyābajjhaihi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau abyābajjhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayati aikantasukhaṃ, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharauti| sau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi kāyasaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vacīsaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā, sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi  manausaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkharitvā sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi laukaṃ  upapan‍naṃ samānaṃ sabyābajjhāpi abyābajjhāpi phassā  phusanti| sau sabyābajjhaihipi abyābajjhaihipi phassaihi phuṭṭhau samānau sabyābajjhampi abyābajjhampi vaidanaṃ vaidayati vaukiṇṇasukhadukkhaṃ, saiyyathāpi manussā aikac‍cai ca daivā aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bahukārasuttaṃ

24. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā puggalassa bahukārā| katamai tayau? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalaṃ āgamma puggalau buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalau imassa puggalassa bahukārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaṃ puggalaṃ āgamma puggalau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalau imassa puggalassa bahukārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaṃ puggalaṃ āgamma puggalau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, puggalau imassa puggalassa bahukārau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā puggalassa bahukārā|

‘‘imaihi ca pana, bhikkhavai, tīhi puggalaihi imassa puggalassa natthañ‍ñau puggalau bahukārauti vadāmi| imaisaṃ pana, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ iminā puggalaina na suppatikāraṃ vadāmi, yadidaṃ abhivādanapac‍cuṭṭhānaañ‍jalikammasāmīcikammacīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsana-gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānuppadānainā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. vajirūpamasuttaṃ

25. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? arukūpamacittau puggalau, vij‍jūpamacittau puggalau, vajirūpamacittau puggalau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, arukūpamacittau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kaudhanau hauti upāyāsabahulau appampi  vuttau  samānau abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, duṭṭhārukau duṭṭhārukā (sī॰) kaṭṭhaina vā kaṭhalāya kathalāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), kaṭhalaina-kathalaina (aṭṭhakathā) vā ghaṭṭitau ghaṭṭitā (sī॰) bhiyyausaumattāya āsavaṃ daiti assavanauti (sī॰); aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau kaudhanau hauti upāyāsabahulau appampi vuttau samānau abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, arukūpamacittau puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, vij‍jūpamacittau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| saiyyathāpi bhikkhavai, cakkhumā purisau rattandhakāratimisāyaṃ vij‍jantarikāya rūpāni passaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vij‍jūpamacittau puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, vajirūpamacittau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vajirassa natthi kiñ‍ci abhaij‍jaṃ maṇi vā pāsāṇau vā; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vajirūpamacittau puggalau| ‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā  laukasmi’’’nti pu॰ pa॰ 102| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. saivitabbasuttaṃ

26. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau| atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau  sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau  na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau hīnau hauti sīlaina samādhinā pañ‍ñāya| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, puggalau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau añ‍ñatra anuddayā añ‍ñatra anukampā|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sadisau hauti sīlaina samādhinā pañ‍ñāya| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? sīlasāmañ‍ñagatānaṃ sataṃ sīlakathā ca nau bhavissati, sā ca nau pavattinī pavattanī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pu॰ pa॰ 122 passitabbaṃ bhavissati, sā ca nau phāsu bhavissati| samādhisāmañ‍ñagatānaṃ sataṃ samādhikathā ca nau bhavissati, sā ca nau pavattinī bhavissati, sā ca nau phāsu bhavissati| pañ‍ñāsāmañ‍ñagatānaṃ sataṃ pañ‍ñākathā ca nau bhavissati, sā ca nau pavattinī bhavissati, sā ca nau phāsu bhavissatīti| tasmā aivarūpau puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau adhikau hauti sīlaina samādhinā pañ‍ñāya| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, puggalau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? iti aparipūraṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraissāmi, paripūraṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmi; aparipūraṃ vā samādhikkhandhaṃ paripūraissāmi, paripūraṃ vā samādhikkhandhaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmi; aparipūraṃ vā pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ paripūraissāmi, paripūraṃ vā pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ tattha tattha pañ‍ñāya anuggahaissāmīti| tasmā aivarūpau puggalau sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘nihīyati  purisau nihīnasaivī,

na ca hāyaitha kadāci tulyasaivī|

saiṭṭhamupanamaṃ udaiti khippaṃ,

tasmā attanau uttariṃ bhajaithā’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. jigucchitabbasuttaṃ

27. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau jigucchitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau| atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau ajjhupaikkhitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau| atthi, bhikkhavai, puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau jigucchitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau dussīlau hauti pāpadhammau asuci saṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau, assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau, abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau, antaupūti avassutau kasambujātau| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, puggalau jigucchitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? kiñ‍cāpi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpassa puggalassa na diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati, atha khau naṃ pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati – ‘pāpamittau purisapuggalau pāpasahāyau pāpasampavaṅkau’ti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ahi gūthagatau kiñ‍cāpi na daṃsati ḍaṃsati (sī॰ syā॰), ḍassati (pī॰), atha khau naṃ makkhaiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kiñ‍cāpi aivarūpassa puggalassa na diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati, atha khau naṃ pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati – ‘pāpamittau purisapuggalau pāpasahāyau pāpasampavaṅkau’ti| tasmā aivarūpau puggalau jigucchitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau ajjhupaikkhitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau kaudhanau hauti upāyāsabahulau, appampi vuttau samānau abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, duṭṭhārukau kaṭṭhaina vā kaṭhalāya vā ghaṭṭitau bhiyyausaumattāya āsavaṃ daiti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tindukālātaṃ  kaṭṭhaina  vā kaṭhalāya vā ghaṭṭitaṃ bhiyyausaumattāya cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati; aivamaivaṃ khau bhikkhavai…pai॰… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gūthakūpau kaṭṭhaina vā kaṭhalāya vā ghaṭṭitau bhiyyausaumattāya duggandhau hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau  kaudhanau hauti upāyāsabahulau, appampi vuttau samānau abhisaj‍jati kuppati byāpaj‍jati patitthīyati, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, puggalau ajjhupaikkhitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? ak‍kausaiyyapi maṃ paribhāsaiyyapi maṃ anatthampi maṃ karaiyyāti| tasmā aivarūpau puggalau ajjhupaikkhitabbau na saivitabbau na bhajitabbau na payirupāsitabbau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlavā hauti kalyāṇadhammau| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? kiñ‍cāpi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpassa puggalassa na diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati, atha khau naṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati – ‘kalyāṇamittau purisapuggalau kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau’ti| tasmā aivarūpau puggalau saivitabbau bhajitabbau payirupāsitabbau| ‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’’nti|

‘‘nihīyati  purisau nihīnasaivī,

na ca hāyaitha kadāci tulyasaivī|

saiṭṭhamupanamaṃ udaiti khippaṃ,

tasmā attanau uttariṃ bhajaithā’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. gūthabhāṇīsuttaṃ

28. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? gūthabhāṇī, pupphabhāṇī, madhubhāṇī| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau gūthabhāṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sabhaggatau vā parisaggatau vā sabhāgatau vā parisāgatau vā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ñātimajjhagatau vā pūgamajjhagatau vā rājakulamajjhagatau vā abhinītau sakkhipuṭṭhau – ‘aihambhau purisa, yaṃ jānāsi taṃ vadaihī’ti| sau  ajānaṃ vā āha ‘jānāmī’ti, jānaṃ vā āha ‘na jānāmī’ti, apassaṃ vā āha ‘passāmī’ti, passaṃ vā āha ‘na  passāmī’ti ma॰ ni॰ 1.440; pu॰ pa॰ 91; iti attahaitu vā parahaitu vā āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau gūthabhāṇī|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau pupphabhāṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sabhaggatau vā parisaggatau vā ñātimajjhagatau vā pūgamajjhagatau vā rājakulamajjhagatau vā abhinītau sakkhipuṭṭhau – ‘aihambhau purisa, yaṃ pajānāsi taṃ vadaihī’ti, sau ajānaṃ vā āha ‘na jānāmī’ti, jānaṃ vā āha ‘jānāmī’ti, apassaṃ vā āha ‘na passāmī’ti, passaṃ vā āha ‘passāmī’ti; iti attahaitu vā parahaitu vā āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu vā na sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau  pupphabhāṇī|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau madhubhāṇī? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti; yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau madhubhāṇī| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. andhasuttaṃ

29. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? andhau, aikacakkhu, dvicakkhu| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau andhau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tathārūpaṃ cakkhu na  hauti yathārūpaina cakkhunā anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ adhigacchaiyya adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ karaiyya phātikaraiyya (sī॰); tathārūpampissa cakkhu na hauti yathārūpaina cakkhunā kusalākusalai dhammai jānaiyya, sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai dhammai jānaiyya, hīnappaṇītai dhammai jānaiyya, kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammai jānaiyya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau andhau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau aikacakkhu? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tathārūpaṃ  cakkhu hauti yathārūpaina cakkhunā anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ  adhigacchaiyya adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ karaiyya; tathārūpaṃ panassa tathārūpampissa (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) cakkhu na hauti yathārūpaina cakkhunā kusalākusalai dhammai jānaiyya, sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai dhammai jānaiyya, hīnappaṇītai dhammai jānaiyya, kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai  dhammai jānaiyya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau aikacakkhu|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau dvicakkhu? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tathārūpaṃ cakkhu hauti yathārūpaina cakkhunā anadhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ adhigacchaiyya, adhigataṃ vā bhaugaṃ phātiṃ karaiyya; tathārūpampissa cakkhu hauti yathārūpaina cakkhunā kusalākusalai dhammai jānaiyya; sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai dhammai jānaiyya, hīnappaṇītai dhammai jānaiyya, kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammai jānaiyya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau dvicakkhu| ‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’’nti|

‘‘na caiva bhaugā tathārūpā, na ca puñ‍ñāni kubbati|

ubhayattha kaliggāhau, andhassa hatacakkhunau||

‘‘athāparāyaṃ akkhātau, aikacakkhu ca puggalau|

dhammādhammaina saṭhausau saṃsaṭṭhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), saṭhauti (ka॰), bhaugāni pariyaisati||

‘‘thaiyyaina kūṭakammaina, musāvādaina cūbhayaṃ|

kusalau hauti saṅghātuṃ saṃhātuṃ (syā॰), kāmabhaugī ca mānavau|

itau sau nirayaṃ gantvā, aikacakkhu vihañ‍ñati||

‘‘dvicakkhu pana akkhātau, saiṭṭhau purisapuggalau|

dhammaladdhaihi bhaugaihi, uṭṭhānādhigataṃ dhanaṃ||

‘‘dadāti  saiṭṭhasaṅkappau, abyaggamānasau narau|

upaiti bhaddakaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yattha gantvā na saucati||

‘‘andhañ‍ca  aikacakkhuñ‍ca, ārakā parivaj‍jayai|

dvicakkhuṃ  pana saivaitha, saiṭṭhaṃ purisapuggala’’nti|| navamaṃ|

10. avakuj‍jasuttaṃ

30. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai pu॰ pa॰ 107-108, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? avakuj‍japañ‍ñau puggalau, ucchaṅgapañ‍ñau puggalau, puthupañ‍ñau puggalau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, avakuj‍japañ‍ñau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ārāmaṃ gantā hauti abhikkhaṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dhammassavanāya| tassa bhikkhū dhammaṃ daisainti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsainti| sau tasmiṃ āsanai nisin‍nau tassā kathāya naiva ādiṃ manasi karauti, na majjhaṃ manasi karauti, na pariyausānaṃ manasi karauti; vuṭṭhitaupi tamhā āsanā tassā kathāya naiva ādiṃ manasi karauti, na majjhaṃ manasi karauti, na pariyausānaṃ manasi karauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kumbhau nik‍kuj‍jau nik‍kuj‍jau (sī॰ pī॰) tatra udakaṃ āsittaṃ vivaṭṭati, nau saṇṭhāti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau ārāmaṃ gantā hauti abhikkhaṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dhammassavanāya| tassa bhikkhū dhammaṃ daisainti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsainti| sau tasmiṃ āsanai nisin‍nau tassā kathāya naiva ādiṃ manasi karauti, na majjhaṃ manasi karauti, na pariyausānaṃ manasi karauti; vuṭṭhitaupi tamhā āsanā tassā kathāya naivādiṃ manasi karauti, na majjhaṃ manasi karauti, na pariyausānaṃ manasi karauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, avakuj‍japañ‍ñau  puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, ucchaṅgapañ‍ñau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ārāmaṃ gantā hauti abhikkhaṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dhammassavanāya| tassa bhikkhū dhammaṃ daisainti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsainti| sau tasmiṃ āsanai nisin‍nau tassā kathāya ādimpi manasi karauti, majjhampi manasi karauti, pariyausānampi manasi karauti; vuṭṭhitau ca khau tamhā āsanā tassā kathāya naivādiṃ manasi karauti, na majjhaṃ manasi karauti, na pariyausānaṃ manasi karauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisassa ucchaṅgai nānākhaj‍jakāni ākiṇṇāni – tilā  taṇḍulā maudakā badarā| sau tamhā āsanā vuṭṭhahantau  satisammausā pakiraiyya| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau ārāmaṃ gantā hauti abhikkhaṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dhammassavanāya| tassa bhikkhū dhammaṃ daisainti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsainti| sau tasmiṃ āsanai nisin‍nau tassā kathāya ādimpi manasi karauti, majjhampi manasi karauti, pariyausānampi manasi karauti; vuṭṭhitau ca khau tamhā āsanā tassā kathāya naiva ādiṃ manasi karauti, na majjhaṃ manasi karauti, na pariyausānaṃ manasi karauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ucchaṅgapañ‍ñau puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puthupañ‍ñau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau ārāmaṃ gantā hauti abhikkhaṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  santikai dhammassavanāya| tassa bhikkhū dhammaṃ daisainti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsainti| sau tasmiṃ āsanai nisin‍nau tassā kathāya ādimpi manasi karauti, majjhampi manasi karauti, pariyausānampi manasi karauti; vuṭṭhitaupi tamhā āsanā tassā kathāya ādimpi manasi karauti, majjhampi manasi karauti, pariyausānampi manasi karauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kumbhau uk‍kuj‍jau tatra udakaṃ āsittaṃ saṇṭhāti nau vivaṭṭati; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau ārāmaṃ gantā hauti abhikkhaṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dhammassavanāya| tassa bhikkhū dhammaṃ daisainti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsainti| sau tasmiṃ āsanai nisin‍nau tassā kathāya ādimpi manasi karauti, majjhampi manasi karauti, pariyausānampi manasi karauti; vuṭṭhitaupi tamhā āsanā tassā kathāya ādimpi manasi karauti, majjhampi manasi karauti, pariyausānampi manasi karauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puthupañ‍ñau puggalau| ‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’’nti|

‘‘avakuj‍japañ‍ñau purisau, dummaidhau avicakkhaṇau|

abhikkhaṇampi cai hauti, gantā bhikkhūna santikai||

‘‘ādiṃ  kathāya majjhañ‍ca, pariyausānañ‍ca tādisau|

uggahaituṃ na sak‍kauti, pañ‍ñā hissa na vij‍jati||

‘‘ucchaṅgapañ‍ñau  purisau, saiyyau aitaina vuc‍cati|

abhikkhaṇampi cai hauti, gantā bhikkhūna santikai||

‘‘ādiṃ kathāya majjhañ‍ca, pariyausānañ‍ca tādisau|

nisin‍nau āsanai tasmiṃ, uggahaitvāna byañ‍janaṃ|

vuṭṭhitau nappajānāti, gahitaṃ hissa gahitampissa (ka॰) mussati||

‘‘puthupañ‍ñau ca purisau, saiyyau aitaihi aitaina (ka॰) vuc‍cati|

abhikkhaṇampi cai hauti, gantā bhikkhūna santikai||

‘‘ādiṃ kathāya majjhañ‍ca, pariyausānañ‍ca tādisau|

nisin‍nau āsanai tasmiṃ, uggahaitvāna byañ‍janaṃ||

‘‘dhāraiti saiṭṭhasaṅkappau, abyaggamānasau narau|

dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau, dukkhassantakarau siyā’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

puggalavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

samiddha kāyasakkhi (sī॰), saviṭṭha (syā॰ kaṃ॰), saiṭṭha (ka॰) -gilāna-saṅkhārā, bahukārā vajiraina ca|

saivi-jiguccha-gūthabhāṇī, andhau ca avakuj‍jatāti||


4. daivadūtavaggau

1.sabrahmakasuttaṃ

31. ‘‘sabrahmakāni, bhikkhavai, tāni kulāni yaisaṃ puttānaṃ mātāpitarau ajjhāgārai pūjitā haunti| sapubbācariyakāni, bhikkhavai, tāni kulāni yaisaṃ puttānaṃ mātāpitarau ajjhāgārai pūjitā haunti| sāhunaiyyāni, bhikkhavai, tāni kulāni yaisaṃ puttānaṃ mātāpitarau ajjhāgārai pūjitā haunti| ‘brahmā’ti, bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘pubbācariyā’ti, bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘āhunaiyyā’ti, bhikkhavai, mātāpitūnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? bahukārā, bhikkhavai, mātāpitarau puttānaṃ, āpādakā pausakā, imassa laukassa dassaitārauti|

‘‘brahmāti mātāpitarau, pubbācariyāti vuc‍carai|

āhunaiyyā ca puttānaṃ, pajāya anukampakā||

‘‘tasmā hi nai namassaiyya, sak‍karaiyya ca paṇḍitau|

an‍naina atha pānaina, vatthaina sayanaina ca|

ucchādanaina nhāpanaina nahāpanaina (sī॰), pādānaṃ dhauvanaina ca||

‘‘tāya naṃ pāricariyāya, mātāpitūsu paṇḍitā|

idhaiva idha caiva (sī॰) naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti saggai ca maudatīti (sī॰) itivu॰ 106 itivuttakai|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. ānandasuttaṃ

32. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘siyā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā nāssu, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā nāssu; yañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatau ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, ānanda, bhikkhunau  tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā nāssu, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā nāssu; yañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatau ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, siyā bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā nāssu, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā  nāssu; yañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatau ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’’ti?

‘‘idhānanda, bhikkhunau aivaṃ hauti – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, siyā bhikkhunau tathārūpau samādhipaṭilābhau yathā imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā nāssu, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā nāssu; yañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatau ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘idañ‍ca pana maitaṃ, ānanda, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ pārāyanai puṇṇakapañhai –

‘‘saṅkhāya laukasmiṃ parauparāni parauvarāni (sī॰ pī॰) su॰ ni॰ 1054; cūḷani॰ puṇṇakamāṇavapucchā 73,

yassiñ‍jitaṃ natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai|

santau  vidhūmau anīghau anighau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), anaghau (?) nirāsau,

atāri sau jātijaranti brūmī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. sāriputtasuttaṃ

33. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saṃkhittainapi khau ahaṃ, sāriputta, dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; vitthārainapi khau ahaṃ, sāriputta, dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; saṃkhittavitthārainapi khau ahaṃ, sāriputta, dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; añ‍ñātārau ca dul‍labhā’’ti| ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau, aitassa, sugata, kālau yaṃ bhagavā saṃkhittainapi dhammaṃ daisaiyya, vitthārainapi dhammaṃ daisaiyya, saṃkhittavitthārainapi dhammaṃ daisaiyya| bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, sāriputta, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na bhavissanti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na bhavissanti, yañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharatau ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi khau, sāriputta, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘yatau ca khau, sāriputta, bhikkhunau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai  kāyai ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti, yañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharatau ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na haunti tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, sāriputta – ‘bhikkhu acchaicchi acchaij‍ji (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) taṇhaṃ, vivattayi vāvattayi (sī॰ pī॰) saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassa’| idañ‍ca pana maitaṃ, sāriputta, sandhāya bhāsitaṃ pārāyanai pārāyaṇai (sī॰) udayapañhai –

‘‘pahānaṃ kāmasañ‍ñānaṃ, daumanassāna cūbhayaṃ|

thinassa ca panūdanaṃ, kuk‍kuc‍cānaṃ nivāraṇaṃ||

‘‘upaikkhāsatisaṃsuddhaṃ, dhammatak‍kapuraijavaṃ|

añ‍ñāvimaukkhaṃ pabrūmi, avij‍jāya pabhaidana’’nti su॰ ni॰ 1112; cūḷani॰ udayamāṇavapucchā 131|| tatiyaṃ|

4. nidānasuttaṃ

34. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? laubhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, dausau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, mauhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, laubhapakataṃ kammaṃ laubhajaṃ laubhanidānaṃ laubhasamudayaṃ, yatthassa attabhāvau nibbattati tattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati| yattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati tattha tassa kammassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍ja vā upapaj‍jai vā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) upapaj‍jitvāti ma॰ ni॰ 3.303 pāḷiyā saṃvaṇṇanā aparai vā aparāparai vā (ka॰) pariyāyai|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dausapakataṃ kammaṃ dausajaṃ dausanidānaṃ dausasamudayaṃ, yatthassa attabhāvau nibbattati tattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati| yattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati tattha tassa kammassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍ja vā aparai vā pariyāyai|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mauhapakataṃ kammaṃ mauhajaṃ mauhanidānaṃ mauhasamudayaṃ, yatthassa attabhāvau nibbattati tattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati| yattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati tattha  tassa kammassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍ja  vā aparai vā pariyāyai|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, bījāni akhaṇḍāni apūtīni avātātapahatāni sārādāni sukhasayitāni sukhaittai suparikammakatāya bhūmiyā nikkhittāni| daivau ca sammādhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| aivassu tāni, bhikkhavai, bījāni vuddhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ laubhapakataṃ kammaṃ laubhajaṃ laubhanidānaṃ laubhasamudayaṃ, yatthassa attabhāvau nibbattati tattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati| yattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati tattha tassa kammassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍ja vā aparai vā pariyāyai|

‘‘yaṃ  dausapakataṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… yaṃ mauhapakataṃ kammaṃ mauhajaṃ mauhanidānaṃ mauhasamudayaṃ, yatthassa attabhāvau nibbattati tattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati| yattha taṃ kammaṃ vipac‍cati tattha tassa kammassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍ja vā aparai vā pariyāyai| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? alaubhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, adausau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, amauhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, alaubhapakataṃ kammaṃ alaubhajaṃ alaubhanidānaṃ alaubhasamudayaṃ, laubhai vigatai aivaṃ taṃ kammaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, adausapakataṃ kammaṃ adausajaṃ adausanidānaṃ adausasamudayaṃ, dausai vigatai aivaṃ taṃ kammaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, amauhapakataṃ kammaṃ amauhajaṃ amauhanidānaṃ amauhasamudayaṃ, mauhai vigatai aivaṃ taṃ kammaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, bījāni akhaṇḍāni apūtīni avātātapahatāni  sārādāni sukhasayitāni| tāni purisau agginā ḍahaiyya| agginā ḍahitvā masiṃ karaiyya| masiṃ karitvā mahāvātai vā auphuṇaiyya aupunaiyya (sī॰ pī॰) nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya| aivassu tāni, bhikkhavai, bījāni ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṅkatāni anabhāvakatāni (sī॰ pī॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāni| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ alaubhapakataṃ kammaṃ alaubhajaṃ alaubhanidānaṃ alaubhasamudayaṃ, laubhai vigatai aivaṃ taṃ kammaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṅkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ|

‘‘yaṃ  adausapakataṃ kammaṃ…pai॰… yaṃ amauhapakataṃ kammaṃ amauhajaṃ amauhanidānaṃ amauhasamudayaṃ, mauhai vigatai aivaṃ taṃ kammaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti…pai॰… āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāyā’’ti|

‘‘laubhajaṃ dausajañ‍caiva dausajaṃ kammaṃ (ka॰), mauhajañ‍cāpaviddasu|

yaṃ taina pakataṃ kammaṃ, appaṃ vā yadi vā bahuṃ|

idhaiva taṃ vaidaniyaṃ, vatthu añ‍ñaṃ na vij‍jati||

‘‘tasmā laubhañ‍ca dausañ‍ca, mauhajañ‍cāpi viddasu|

vij‍jaṃ uppādayaṃ bhikkhu, sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. hatthakasuttaṃ

35. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati gaumaggai siṃsapāvanai paṇṇasantharai| atha khau hatthakau āḷavakau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau addasa bhagavantaṃ gaumaggai siṃsapāvanai paṇṇasantharai nisin‍naṃ| disvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau hatthakau āḷavakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhantai, bhagavā sukhamasayitthā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, kumāra, sukhamasayitthaṃ| yai ca pana laukai sukhaṃ sainti, ahaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau’’ti|

‘‘sītā, bhantai, haimantikā ratti, antaraṭṭhakau himapātasamayau, kharā gaukaṇṭakahatā bhūmi, tanukau paṇṇasantharau, viraḷāni rukkhassa  pattāni, sītāni kāsāyāni vatthāni, sītau ca vairambhau vātau vāyati| atha ca pana bhagavā aivamāha – ‘aivaṃ, kumāra, sukhamasayitthaṃ| yai ca pana laukai sukhaṃ sainti, ahaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau’’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, kumāra, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kumāra, idhassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā kūṭāgāraṃ ul‍littāvalittaṃ nivātaṃ phusitaggaḷaṃ pihitavātapānaṃ| tatrassa pal‍laṅkau gaunakatthatau paṭikatthatau paṭalikatthatau  kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇau kādalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇau (sī॰) sauttaracchadau ubhatau lauhitakūpadhānau; tailappadīpau caittha jhāyaiyya jālaiyya (ka॰); catassau ca tassaiva (ka॰) pajāpatiyau manāpāmanāpaina pac‍cupaṭṭhitā assu| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kumāra, sukhaṃ vā sau sayaiyya nau vā? kathaṃ vā tai aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘sukhaṃ sau, bhantai, sayaiyya| yai ca pana laukai sukhaṃ sainti, sau taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kumāra, api nu tassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ rāgajā pariḷāhā kāyikā vā caitasikā vā yaihi sau rāgajaihi pariḷāhaihi pariḍayhamānau dukkhaṃ sayaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘yaihi  khau sau, kumāra, gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā rāgajaihi pariḷāhaihi pariḍayhamānau dukkhaṃ sayaiyya, sau rāgau tathāgatassa pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| tasmāhaṃ sukhamasayitthaṃ|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, kumāra, api nu tassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ dausajā pariḷāhā…pai॰… mauhajā pariḷāhā kāyikā vā caitasikā vā yaihi sau mauhajaihi pariḷāhaihi pariḍayhamānau dukkhaṃ sayaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘yai  hi khau sau, kumāra, gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā mauhajaihi pariḷāhaihi pariḍayhamānau dukkhaṃ sayaiyya, sau mauhau tathāgatassa pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau  āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| tasmāhaṃ sukhamasayittha’’nti|

cūḷava॰ 305; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.242 ‘‘sabbadā vai sukhaṃ saiti, brāhmaṇau parinibbutau|

yau na limpati lippati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) kāmaisu, sītibhūtau nirūpadhi||

‘‘sabbā āsattiyau chaitvā, vinaiyya hadayai daraṃ|

upasantau sukhaṃ saiti, santiṃ pappuyya caitasau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. daivadūtasuttaṃ

36. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, daivadūtāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā nānābāhāsu gahaitvā yamassa rañ‍ñau dassainti – ‘ayaṃ, daiva, purisau amattaiyyau  apaittaiyyau asāmañ‍ñau abrahmañ‍ñau, na kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyī| imassa daivau daṇḍaṃ paṇaitū’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā paṭhamaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau, purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu paṭhamaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā āsītikaṃ vā nāvutikaṃ vā vassasatikaṃ vā passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.263 jātiyā jiṇṇaṃ gaupānasivaṅkaṃ bhauggaṃ daṇḍaparāyaṇaṃ daṇḍaparāyanaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pavaidhamānaṃ gacchantaṃ āturaṃ gatayaubbanaṃ khaṇḍadantaṃ palitakaisaṃ vilūnaṃ khal‍litasiraṃ khalitaṃ sirau (sī॰ pī॰), khalitasiraṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.263 valitaṃ tilakāhatagatta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau, purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – ahampi khaumhi jarādhammau jaraṃ anatītau, handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi, kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau  aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai| pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau, purisa, pamādatāya pamādavatāya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.262 na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ taṃ (ka॰), ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ tai (ka॰) pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ taṃ khau panaitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pāpakammaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ (sī॰) naiva mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā kataṃ, na bhātarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ, na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ, na daivatāhi kataṃ, na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ; atha khau tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā paṭhamaṃ daivadūtaṃ  samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā, dutiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu dutiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’ti| ‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā ābādhikaṃ dukkhitaṃ bāḷhagilānaṃ, sakai muttakarīsai palipan‍naṃ saimānaṃ, añ‍ñaihi vuṭṭhāpiyamānaṃ, añ‍ñaihi saṃvaisiyamāna’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – ahampi khaumhi byādhidhammau byādhiṃ anatītau, handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai| pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, pamādatāya na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ pāpakammaṃ naiva mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā kataṃ, na bhātarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na mittāmac‍caihi  kataṃ, na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ, na daivatāhi kataṃ, na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ; atha khau tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ| tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā dutiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā, tatiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jati samanugāhati samanubhāsati – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu  tatiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ pātubhūta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāddasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, na tvaṃ addasa manussaisu itthiṃ  vā purisaṃ vā aikāhamataṃ vā dvīhamataṃ vā tīhamataṃ vā uddhumātakaṃ vinīlakaṃ vipubbakajāta’nti? sau aivamāha – ‘addasaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, tassa tai viñ‍ñussa satau mahal‍lakassa na aitadahausi – ahampi khaumhi maraṇadhammau maraṇaṃ anatītau, handāhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ karaumi kāyaina vācāya manasā’ti? sau aivamāha – ‘nāsakkhissaṃ, bhantai| pamādassaṃ, bhantai’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā aivamāha – ‘ambhau purisa, pamādatāya na kalyāṇamakāsi kāyaina vācāya manasā| taggha tvaṃ, ambhau purisa, tathā karissanti yathā taṃ pamattaṃ| taṃ khau pana tai aitaṃ pāpakammaṃ naiva mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā kataṃ, na bhātarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na mittāmac‍caihi kataṃ, na ñātisālauhitaihi kataṃ, na daivatāhi kataṃ, na samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi kataṃ; atha khau tayāvaitaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ| tvañ‍ñaivaitassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidissasī’’’ti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamau rājā tatiyaṃ daivadūtaṃ samanuyuñ‍jitvā samanugāhitvā samanubhāsitvā tuṇhī hauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā pañ‍cavidhabandhanaṃ nāma kāraṇaṃ karaunti| tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ hatthai gamainti| tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyasmiṃ hatthai gamainti| tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ pādai gamainti| tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyasmiṃ pādai gamainti| tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ majjhai urasmiṃ gamainti| sau tattha dukkhā  tibbā tippā (sī॰) kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyati, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā saṃvaisaitvā saṃkaḍḍhitvā (ka॰) kudhārīhi tacchanti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyati, na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti|

‘‘tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā vāsīhi tacchanti…pai॰… tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā rathai yaujaitvā ādittāya bhūmiyā sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya sañ‍jautibhūtāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sāraintipi pac‍cāsāraintipi…pai॰… tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā mahantaṃ aṅgārapabbataṃ ādittaṃ  sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āraupaintipi auraupaintipi…pai॰… tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā tattāya lauhakumbhiyā pakkhipanti, ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍camānau sakimpi uddhaṃ gacchati, sakimpi adhau gacchati, sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidiyati, na ca tāva kālaṃ karauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, nirayapālā mahānirayai pakkhipanti| sau khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahānirayau –

‘‘catuk‍kaṇṇau catudvārau, vibhattau bhāgasau mitau|

ayaupākārapariyantau, ayasā paṭikuj‍jitau||

‘‘tassa  ayaumayā bhūmi, jalitā taijasā yutā|

samantā  yaujanasataṃ, pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā’’ti pai॰ va॰ 70-71, 240-241||

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, yamassa rañ‍ñau aitadahausi – ‘yai kira, bhau, laukai pāpakāni kammāni karaunti tai aivarūpā vividhā kammakāraṇā karīyanti| ahau vatāhaṃ manussattaṃ labhaiyyaṃ, tathāgatau ca laukai uppaj‍jaiyya arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, tañ‍cāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsaiyyaṃ| sau ca mai bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiyya, tassa cāhaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ ājānaiyya’nti| taṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, na añ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā sutvā aivaṃ vadāmi, api ca khau, bhikkhavai, yadaiva mai sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ tadaivāhaṃ vadāmī’’ti|

‘‘cauditā  daivadūtaihi, yai pamaj‍janti māṇavā|

tai dīgharattaṃ saucanti, hīnakāyūpagā narā||

‘‘yai ca khau daivadūtaihi, santau sappurisā idha|

cauditā nappamaj‍janti, ariyadhammai kudācanaṃ||

‘‘upādānai  bhayaṃ disvā, jātimaraṇasambhavai|

anupādā vimuc‍canti, jātimaraṇasaṅkhayai||

‘‘tai appamattā tai khauppamattā (sī॰), tai khaimappattā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.271 sukhinau sukhitā (sī॰ syā॰), diṭṭhadhammābhinibbutā|

sabbavairabhayātītā, sabbadukkhaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. catumahārājasuttaṃ

37. ‘‘aṭṭhamiyaṃ, bhikkhavai, pakkhassa catun‍naṃ mahārājānaṃ amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā imaṃ laukaṃ anuvicaranti – ‘kac‍ci bahū manussā manussaisu mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau upausathaṃ  upavasanti paṭijāgaraunti puñ‍ñāni karauntī’ti| cātuddasiṃ, bhikkhavai, pakkhassa catun‍naṃ mahārājānaṃ puttā imaṃ laukaṃ anuvicaranti – ‘kac‍ci bahū manussā manussaisu mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau  upausathaṃ upavasanti paṭijāgaraunti puñ‍ñāni karauntī’ti| tadahu, bhikkhavai, upausathai pan‍narasai cattārau mahārājānau sāmañ‍ñaiva imaṃ laukaṃ anuvicaranti – ‘kac‍ci bahū manussā manussaisu mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau upausathaṃ upavasanti paṭijāgaraunti puñ‍ñāni karauntī’’’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, appakā haunti manussā manussaisu mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau upausathaṃ upavasanti paṭijāgaraunti puñ‍ñāni karaunti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, cattārau mahārājānau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sudhammāya sabhāya san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ āraucainti – ‘appakā khau, mārisā, manussā manussaisu mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau upausathaṃ upavasanti paṭijāgaraunti puñ‍ñāni karauntī’ti| taina khau, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā anattamanā haunti – ‘dibbā vata, bhau, kāyā parihāyissanti, paripūrissanti asurakāyā’’’ti|
