‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, bahū haunti manussā manussaisu mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau upausathaṃ upavasanti paṭijāgaraunti puñ‍ñāni karaunti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, cattārau mahārājānau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sudhammāya sabhāya san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ āraucainti – ‘bahū khau, mārisā, manussā manussaisu mattaiyyā paittaiyyā sāmañ‍ñā brahmañ‍ñā kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinau upausathaṃ upavasanti paṭijāgaraunti puñ‍ñāni karauntī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā attamanā haunti – ‘dibbā vata, bhau, kāyā paripūrissanti, parihāyissanti asurakāyā’’’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau daivai tāvatiṃsai anunayamānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘cātuddasiṃ  pañ‍cadasiṃ, yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī|

pāṭihāriyapakkhañ‍ca, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ|

upausathaṃ upavasaiyya, yaupissa yaupassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mādisau narau’’ti||

‘‘sā khau panaisā, bhikkhavai, sak‍kaina daivānamindaina gāthā duggītā na sugītā dubbhāsitā na subhāsitā| taṃ kissa haitu? sak‍kau hi, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau avītarāgau avītadausau avītamauhau|

‘‘yau ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā brahmacariyau katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau, tassa khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau tassa khau aitaṃ bhikkhunau (sī॰ syā॰), tassa khau aivaṃ bhikkhavai bhikkhunau (ka॰) kal‍laṃ vacanāya –

‘‘cātuddasiṃ pañ‍cadasiṃ, yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī|

pāṭihāriyapakkhañ‍ca, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ|

upausathaṃ upavasaiyya, yaupissa mādisau narau’’ti||

‘‘taṃ  kissa haitu? sau hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vītarāgau vītadausau vītamauhau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyacatumahārājasuttaṃ

38. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau daivai tāvatiṃsai anunayamānau tāyaṃ  vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘cātuddasiṃ pañ‍cadasiṃ, yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī|

pāṭihāriyapakkhañ‍ca, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ|

upausathaṃ upavasaiyya, yaupissa mādisau narau’’ti||

‘‘sā khau panaisā, bhikkhavai, sak‍kaina daivānamindaina gāthā duggītā na sugītā dubbhāsitā na subhāsitā| taṃ kissa haitu? sak‍kau hi, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau aparimuttau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, aparimuttau dukkhasmāti vadāmi|

‘‘yau ca khau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau, tassa khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vacanāya –

‘‘cātuddasiṃ pañ‍cadasiṃ, yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī|

pāṭihāriyapakkhañ‍ca, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ|

upausathaṃ upavasaiyya, yaupissa mādisau narau’’ti||

‘‘taṃ  kissa haitu? sau hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parimuttau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuttau dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sukhumālasuttaṃ

39. ‘‘sukhumālau  ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, paramasukhumālau ac‍cantasukhumālau| mama sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, pitu nivaisanai paukkharaṇiyau kāritā haunti| aikattha sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, uppalaṃ vappati pupphati (sī॰ pī॰), aikattha padumaṃ, aikattha puṇḍarīkaṃ, yāvadaiva mamatthāya| na khau panassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, akāsikaṃ candanaṃ dhāraimi kāsikaṃ candanaṃ dhāraimi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), akāsikaṃ dhāraimi (?)| kāsikaṃ, bhikkhavai, su mai taṃ vaiṭhanaṃ hauti, kāsikā kañ‍cukā, kāsikaṃ nivāsanaṃ, kāsikau uttarāsaṅgau| rattindivaṃ rattidivaṃ (ka॰) khau pana mai su taṃ, bhikkhavai, saitacchattaṃ dhārīyati – ‘mā naṃ phusi sītaṃ vā uṇhaṃ vā tiṇaṃ vā rajau vā ussāvau vā’’’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, tayau pāsādā ahaisuṃ – aikau haimantikau, aikau gimhikau, aikau vassikau| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, vassikai pāsādai vassikai cattārau māsai nippurisaihi tūriyaihi paricārayamānau paricāriyamānau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) na haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ aurauhāmi| yathā khau pana, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaisaṃ nivaisanai dāsakammakarapaurisassa kaṇājakaṃ bhaujanaṃ dīyati bilaṅgadutiyaṃ, aivamaivassu mai, bhikkhavai, pitu nivaisanai dāsakammakarapaurisassa sālimaṃsaudanau dīyati|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivarūpāya iddhiyā saman‍nāgatassa aivarūpaina ca sukhumālaina aitadahausi – ‘assutavā khau puthuj‍janau attanā jarādhammau samānau jaraṃ anatītau paraṃ jiṇṇaṃ disvā aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati attānaṃyaiva atisitvā, ahampi khaumhi jarādhammau jaraṃ anatītau| ahañ‍caiva ahañ‍cai (?) khau pana jarādhammau samānau jaraṃ anatītau paraṃ jiṇṇaṃ disvā aṭṭīyaiyyaṃ harāyaiyyaṃ jigucchaiyyaṃ  na maitaṃ assa patirūpa’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhatau yau yaubbanai yaubbanamadau sau sabbasau pahīyi|

‘‘assutavā khau puthuj‍janau attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhiṃ anatītau paraṃ byādhitaṃ disvā  aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati attānaṃyaiva atisitvā – ‘ahampi khaumhi byādhidhammau byādhiṃ anatītau, ahañ‍caiva khau pana byādhidhammau samānau byādhiṃ anatītau paraṃ byādhikaṃ disvā aṭṭīyaiyyaṃ harāyaiyyaṃ jigucchaiyyaṃ, na maitaṃ assa patirūpa’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhatau yau āraugyai āraugyamadau sau sabbasau pahīyi|

‘‘assutavā  khau puthuj‍janau attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇaṃ anatītau paraṃ mataṃ disvā aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati attānaṃyaiva atisitvā – ‘ahampi khaumhi maraṇadhammau, maraṇaṃ anatītau, ahaṃ caiva khau pana  maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇaṃ anatītau paraṃ mataṃ disvā aṭṭīyaiyyaṃ harāyaiyyaṃ jigucchaiyyaṃ, na maitaṃ assa patirūpa’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, iti paṭisañ‍cikkhatau yau jīvitai jīvitamadau sau sabbasau pahīyī’’ti|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, madā| katamai tayau? yaubbanamadau, āraugyamadau, jīvitamadau| yaubbanamadamattau vā, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| āraugyamadamattau vā, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau…pai॰… jīvitamadamattau vā, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ  carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| sau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati|

‘‘yaubbanamadamattau vā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattati| āraugyamadamattau vā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu…pai॰… jīvitamadamattau vā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattatī’’ti|

‘‘byādhidhammā jarādhammā, athau maraṇadhamminau|

yathādhammā byādhidhammau jarādhammau, athau maraṇadhammikau| yathā dhammau (ka॰) tathāsantā, jigucchanti puthuj‍janā||

‘‘ahañ‍cai taṃ jigucchaiyyaṃ, aivaṃdhammaisu pāṇisu|

na maitaṃ patirūpassa, mama aivaṃ vihārinau||

‘‘sauhaṃ aivaṃ viharantau, ñatvā dhammaṃ nirūpadhiṃ|

āraugyai yaubbanasmiñ‍ca, jīvitasmiñ‍ca yai madā||

‘‘sabbai  madai abhibhausmi atītausmi (ka॰), naikkhammai daṭṭhu khaimataṃ|

tassa mai ahu ussāhau, nibbānaṃ abhipassatau||

‘‘nāhaṃ bhabbau aitarahi, kāmāni paṭisaivituṃ|

anivatti bhavissāmi, brahmacariyaparāyaṇau’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. ādhipataiyyasuttaṃ

40. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, ādhipataiyyāni| katamāni tīṇi? attādhipataiyyaṃ, laukādhipataiyyaṃ, dhammādhipataiyyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, attādhipataiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau panāhaṃ cīvarahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| na piṇḍapātahaitu, na saināsanahaitu, na  itibhavābhavahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| api ca khaumhi autiṇṇau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau| appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithāti| ahañ‍caiva khau pana yādisakai  yādisakai vā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) kāmai auhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau tādisakai vā ca (ka॰) kāmai pariyaisaiyyaṃ tatau vā ca (ka॰) pāpiṭṭhatarai, na maitaṃ patirūpa’nti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘āraddhaṃ khau pana mai vīriyaṃ bhavissati asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikagga’nti| sau attānaṃyaiva adhipatiṃ karitvā akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti, sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti, suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, attādhipataiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, laukādhipataiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau panāhaṃ cīvarahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| na piṇḍapātahaitu, na saināsanahaitu, na itibhavābhavahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| api ca khaumhi autiṇṇau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau| appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| ahañ‍caiva khau pana aivaṃ pabbajitau  samānau kāmavitak‍kaṃ vā vitak‍kaiyyaṃ, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ vā vitak‍kaiyyaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ vā vitak‍kaiyyaṃ, mahā khau panāyaṃ  laukasan‍nivāsau| mahantasmiṃ khau pana laukasan‍nivāsai santi samaṇabrāhmaṇā iddhimantau dibbacakkhukā paracittavidunau| tai dūrataupi passanti, āsan‍nāpi na dissanti, caitasāpi cittaṃ pajānanti jānanti (ka॰)| taipi maṃ aivaṃ jānaiyyuṃ – ‘passatha, bhau, imaṃ kulaputtaṃ  saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau vaukiṇṇau viharati pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihī’ti| daivatāpi khau santi iddhimantiniyau dibbacakkhukā paracittaviduniyau| tā dūrataupi passanti, āsan‍nāpi na dissanti, caitasāpi cittaṃ jānanti| tāpi maṃ aivaṃ jānaiyyuṃ – ‘passatha, bhau, imaṃ kulaputtaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau samānau vaukiṇṇau viharati pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihī’ti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘āraddhaṃ khau pana mai vīriyaṃ bhavissati  asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikagga’nti| sau laukaṃyaiva adhipatiṃ karitvā akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti, sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti, suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, laukādhipataiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammādhipataiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘na khau panāhaṃ cīvarahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| na piṇḍapātahaitu, na saināsanahaitu, na itibhavābhavahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| api ca khaumhi autiṇṇau jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau  dukkhaparaitau| appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithāti| svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti| santi khau pana mai sabrahmacārī jānaṃ passaṃ viharanti| ahañ‍caiva khau pana aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitau samānau kusītau viharaiyyaṃ pamattau, na maitaṃ assa patirūpa’nti| sau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘āraddhaṃ khau pana mai vīriyaṃ bhavissati asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikagga’nti| sau dhammaṃyaiva adhipatiṃ karitvā akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti, sāvaj‍jaṃ pajahati, anavaj‍jaṃ bhāvaiti, suddhaṃ attānaṃ pariharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammādhipataiyyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi ādhipataiyyānī’’ti|

‘‘natthi  laukai rahau nāma, pāpakammaṃ pakubbatau|

attā tai purisa jānāti, sac‍caṃ vā yadi vā musā||

‘‘kalyāṇaṃ  vata bhau sakkhi, attānaṃ atimañ‍ñasi|

yau santaṃ attani pāpaṃ, attānaṃ parigūhasi||

‘‘passanti  daivā ca tathāgatā ca,

laukasmiṃ bālaṃ visamaṃ carantaṃ|

tasmā hi attādhipataiyyakau ca attādhipakau sakau carai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰),

laukādhipau ca nipakau ca jhāyī||

‘‘dhammādhipau ca anudhammacārī,

na  hīyati sac‍caparak‍kamau muni|

pasayha māraṃ abhibhuyya antakaṃ,

yau ca phusī jātikkhayaṃ padhānavā|

sau tādisau laukavidū sumaidhau,

sabbaisu dhammaisu atammayau munī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

daivadūtavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

brahma ānanda sāriputtau, nidānaṃ hatthakaina ca|

dūtā duvai ca rājānau, sukhumālādhipataiyyaina cāti||


5. cūḷavaggau

1. sammukhībhāvasuttaṃ

41. ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ? saddhāya, bhikkhavai, sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| daiyyadhammassa, bhikkhavai, sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| dakkhiṇaiyyānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. tiṭhānasuttaṃ

42. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi saddhau pasan‍nau vaiditabbau| katamaihi tīhi? sīlavantānaṃ dassanakāmau hauti, saddhammaṃ sautukāmau hauti, vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi ṭhānaihi saddhau pasan‍nau vaiditabbau’’|

‘‘dassanakāmau sīlavataṃ, saddhammaṃ sautumicchati|

vinayai macchairamalaṃ, sa vai saddhauti vuc‍catī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. atthavasasuttaṃ

43. ‘‘tayau, bhikkhavai, atthavasai sampassamānaina alamaiva paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaituṃ| katamai tayau? yau dhammaṃ daisaiti sau atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| yau dhammaṃ suṇāti sau atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| yau caiva dhammaṃ daisaiti yau ca dhammaṃ  suṇāti ubhau atthappaṭisaṃvaidinau ca haunti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidinau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau atthavasai sampassamānaina alamaiva paraisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaitu’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. kathāpavattisuttaṃ

44. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi kathā pavattinī hauti| katamaihi tīhi? yau dhammaṃ daisaiti sau atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| yau dhammaṃ suṇāti sau atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| yau caiva dhammaṃ daisaiti yau ca dhammaṃ suṇāti ubhau atthappaṭisaṃvaidinau ca haunti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidinau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi ṭhānaihi kathā pavattinī hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṇḍitasuttaṃ

45. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitapañ‍ñattāni sappurisapañ‍ñattāni| katamāni tīṇi? dānaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitapañ‍ñattaṃ sappurisapañ‍ñattaṃ| pabbaj‍jā, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitapañ‍ñattā sappurisapañ‍ñattā| mātāpitūnaṃ, bhikkhavai, upaṭṭhānaṃ paṇḍitapañ‍ñattaṃ sappurisapañ‍ñattaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi paṇḍitapañ‍ñattāni sappurisapañ‍ñattānī’’ti|

‘‘sabbhi dānaṃ upañ‍ñattaṃ, ahiṃsā saṃyamau damau|

mātāpitu upaṭṭhānaṃ, santānaṃ brahmacārinaṃ||

‘‘sataṃ aitāni ṭhānāni, yāni saivaitha paṇḍitau|

ariyau dassanasampan‍nau, sa laukaṃ bhajatai siva’’nti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sīlavantasuttaṃ

46. ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavantau pabbajitā gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharanti| tattha manussā tīhi ṭhānaihi bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| katamaihi tīhi? kāyaina, vācāya, manasā| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, sīlavantau pabbajitā gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharanti| tattha manussā imaihi tīhi ṭhānaihi bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saṅkhatalakkhaṇasuttaṃ

47. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, saṅkhatassa saṅkhatalakkhaṇāni| katamāni tīṇi? uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi saṅkhatassa saṅkhatalakkhaṇānī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. asaṅkhatalakkhaṇasuttaṃ

48. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, asaṅkhatassa asaṅkhatalakkhaṇāni| katamāni tīṇi? na uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, na vayau pañ‍ñāyati, na ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi asaṅkhatassa asaṅkhatalakkhaṇānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pabbatarājasuttaṃ

49. ‘‘himavantaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbatarājaṃ nissāya mahāsālā tīhi  vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhanti| katamāhi tīhi? sākhāpattapalāsaina vaḍḍhanti, tacapapaṭikāya vaḍḍhanti, phaiggusāraina vaḍḍhanti| himavantaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabbatarājaṃ nissāya mahāsālā imāhi tīhi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhanti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saddhaṃ kulapatiṃ nissāya antau janau tīhi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhati| katamāhi tīhi? saddhāya vaḍḍhati, sīlaina vaḍḍhati, pañ‍ñāya vaḍḍhati| saddhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulapatiṃ nissāya antau janau imāhi tīhi vaḍḍhīhi vaḍḍhatī’’ti|

‘‘yathāpi pabbatau sailau, arañ‍ñasmiṃ brahāvanai|

taṃ rukkhā upanissāya, vaḍḍhantai tai vanappatī||

‘‘tathaiva  sīlasampan‍naṃ, saddhaṃ kulapatiṃ idha|

upanissāya vaḍḍhanti, puttadārā ca bandhavā|

amac‍cā ñātisaṅghā ca, yai cassa anujīvinau||

‘‘tyāssa  sīlavatau sīlaṃ, cāgaṃ sucaritāni ca|

passamānānukubbanti, attamatthaṃ yai bhavanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vicakkhaṇā||

‘‘idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, maggaṃ sugatigāminaṃ|

nandinau daivalaukasmiṃ, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. ātappakaraṇīyasuttaṃ

50. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi ātappaṃ karaṇīyaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya ātappaṃ karaṇīyaṃ, anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya ātappaṃ karaṇīyaṃ, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsanāya ātappaṃ karaṇīyaṃ| imaihi tīhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi ātappaṃ karaṇīyaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya ātappaṃ karauti, anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya ātappaṃ karauti, uppan‍nānaṃ sārīrikānaṃ vaidanānaṃ dukkhānaṃ tibbānaṃ kharānaṃ kaṭukānaṃ asātānaṃ amanāpānaṃ pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsanāya ātappaṃ  karauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ātāpī nipakau satau sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. mahācaurasuttaṃ

51. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau  visamanissitau ca hauti, gahananissitau ca hauti, balavanissitau ca hauti| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau visamanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau nadīviduggaṃ vā nissitau hauti pabbatavisamaṃ vā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau visamanissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau gahananissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau tiṇagahanaṃ vā nissitau hauti, rukkhagahanaṃ  vā raudhaṃ gaidhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vā mahāvanasaṇḍaṃ vā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau gahananissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau balavanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau rājānaṃ vā rājamahāmattānaṃ vā nissitau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai maṃ kauci kiñ‍ci vakkhati, imai mai rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇissantī’ti| sacai naṃ kauci kiñ‍ci āha, tyāssa rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, mahācaurau balavanissitau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mahācaurau sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpabhikkhu khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamanissitau ca hauti gahananissitau ca balavanissitau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamaina kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, visamaina vacīkammaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti, visamaina manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu visamanissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu gahananissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu micchādiṭṭhikau  hauti, antaggāhikāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu gahananissitau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu balavanissitau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu rājānaṃ vā rājamahāmattānaṃ vā nissitau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sacai maṃ kauci kiñ‍ci vakkhati, imai mai rājānau  vā rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇissantī’ti| sacai naṃ kauci kiñ‍ci āha, tyāssa rājānau vā rājamahāmattā vā pariyaudhāya atthaṃ bhaṇanti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pāpabhikkhu balavanissitau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau pāpabhikkhu khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

cūḷavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sammukhī ṭhānatthavasaṃ, pavatti paṇḍita sīlavaṃ|

saṅkhataṃ pabbatātappaṃ, mahācaurainaikādasāti mahācauraina tai dasāti (ka॰)||

paṭhamau paṇṇāsakau samattau|



2. dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(6) 1. brāhmaṇavaggau

1. paṭhamadvaibrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

52. atha  khau dvai brāhmaṇā jiṇṇā vuddhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā vīsavassasatikā jātiyā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai brāhmaṇā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayamassu, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā jiṇṇā vuddhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā vīsavassasatikā jātiyā; tai camhā akatakalyāṇā akatakusalā akatabhīruttāṇā| auvadatu nau bhavaṃ gautamau, anusāsatu nau bhavaṃ gautamau yaṃ amhākaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘taggha tumhai, brāhmaṇā, jiṇṇā vuddhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā vīsavassasatikā jātiyā; tai cattha akatakalyāṇā akatakusalā akatabhīruttāṇā| upanīyati khau ayaṃ, brāhmaṇā, laukau jarāya byādhinā maraṇaina| aivaṃ upanīyamānai khau, brāhmaṇā, laukai jarāya byādhinā maraṇaina, yau idha kāyaina saṃyamau vācāya saṃyamau manasā saṃyamau, taṃ tassa paitassa tāṇañ‍ca laiṇañ‍ca dīpañ‍ca saraṇañ‍ca parāyaṇañ‍cā’’ti|

‘‘upanīyati  jīvitamappamāyu,

jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau,

puñ‍ñāni kayirātha sukhāvahāni||

‘‘yaudha  kāyaina saṃyamau, vācāya uda caitasā|

taṃ tassa paitassa sukhāya hauti,

yaṃ jīvamānau pakarauti puñ‍ña’’nti|| saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.100 paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyadvaibrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

53. atha  khau dvai brāhmaṇā jiṇṇā vuddhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā vīsavassasatikā jātiyā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai brāhmaṇā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayamassu, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā jiṇṇā vuddhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā vīsavassasatikā jātiyā; tai camhā akatakalyāṇā akatakusalā akatabhīruttāṇā| auvadatu nau bhavaṃ gautamau, anusāsatu nau bhavaṃ gautamau yaṃ amhākaṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘taggha tumhai, brāhmaṇā, jiṇṇā vuddhā mahal‍lakā addhagatā vayauanuppattā vīsavassasatikā jātiyā; tai cattha akatakalyāṇā akatakusalā akatabhīruttāṇā| ādittau khau ayaṃ, brāhmaṇā, laukau jarāya byādhinā maraṇaina| aivaṃ ādittai khau, brāhmaṇā, laukai jarāya byādhinā maraṇaina, yau idha kāyaina saṃyamau vācāya saṃyamau manasā saṃyamau, taṃ  tassa paitassa tāṇañ‍ca laiṇañ‍ca dīpañ‍ca saraṇañ‍ca parāyaṇañ‍cā’’ti|

‘‘ādittasmiṃ agārasmiṃ, yaṃ nīharati bhājanaṃ|

taṃ tassa hauti atthāya, nau ca yaṃ tattha ḍayhati||

‘‘aivaṃ ādittau khau aivaṃ ādīvitau (sī॰ pī॰), aivaṃ ādittakau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.41 laukau, jarāya maraṇaina ca|

nīharaithaiva dānaina, din‍naṃ hauti sunīhataṃ sunibbhataṃ (ka॰)||

‘‘yaudha kāyaina saṃyamau, vācāya uda caitasā|

taṃ tassa paitassa sukhāya hauti,

yaṃ jīvamānau pakarauti puñ‍ña’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. añ‍ñatarabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

54. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikau dhammau sandiṭṭhikau dhammau’ti, bhau gautama, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhau gautama, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau aupanayikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pañ‍camasuttassa ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti?

‘‘rattau khau, brāhmaṇa, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attabyābādhāyapi  caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| rāgai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| (rattau khau…pai॰… kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| rāgai pahīnā naiva kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, na vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, na manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| rattau khau…pai॰… attatthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, paratthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ubhayatthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| rāgai pahīnai attatthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, paratthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ubhayatthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu na dissati, idhapi duṭṭhamūḷhavāraisu aivampi khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘duṭṭhau khau, brāhmaṇa, dausaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi  caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| dausai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na caitasikampi na caitasikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivampi khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘mūḷhau khau, brāhmaṇa, mauhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| mauhai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi  caitaiti, na caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. paribbājakasuttaṃ

55. atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇaparibbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇaparibbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikau dhammau sandiṭṭhikau dhammau’ti, bhau gautama, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhau gautama, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti?

‘‘rattau khau, brāhmaṇa, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| rāgai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāyapi  caitaiti, na parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘rattau  khau, brāhmaṇa, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| rāgai pahīnai naiva kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, na vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, na manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati|

‘‘rattau  khau, brāhmaṇa, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attatthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, paratthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ubhayatthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| rāgai pahīnai attatthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, paratthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ubhayatthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivampi khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti…pai॰…|

‘‘duṭṭhau khau, brāhmaṇa, dausaina…pai॰… mūḷhau khau, brāhmaṇa, mauhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| mauhai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘mūḷhau khau, brāhmaṇa, mauhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau, kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati| mauhai pahīnai naiva kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, na vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, na manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati|

‘‘mūḷhau khau, brāhmaṇa, mauhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attatthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, paratthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, ubhayatthampi yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| mauhai pahīnai attatthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, paratthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ubhayatthampi yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hauti akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. nibbutasuttaṃ

56. atha khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbāna’nti, bhau gautama, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā  nu khau, bhau gautama, sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ hauti akālikaṃ aihipassikaṃ aupanaiyyikaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ viñ‍ñūhī’’ti?

‘‘rattau  khau, brāhmaṇa, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| rāgai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivampi khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ hauti|

‘‘duṭṭhau khau, brāhmaṇa…pai॰… mūḷhau khau, brāhmaṇa, mauhaina abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| mauhai pahīnai naivattabyābādhāyapi  caitaiti, na parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, na caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivampi khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ hauti|

‘‘yatau khau ayaṃ, brāhmaṇa yatau ca khau ayaṃ brāhmaṇa (sī॰), yatau khau brāhmaṇa akālikaṃ aihipassikaṃ aupanaiyyikaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ (ka॰), anavasaisaṃ rāgakkhayaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, anavasaisaṃ dausakkhayaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, anavasaisaṃ mauhakkhayaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ hauti akālikaṃ aihipassikaṃ aupanaiyyikaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ viñ‍ñūhī’’ti| ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. palaukasuttaṃ

57. atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇamahāsālau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau brāhmaṇamahāsālau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama, pubbakānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vuddhānaṃ mahal‍lakānaṃ ācariyapācariyānaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ – ‘pubbai sudaṃ pubbassudaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ayaṃ laukau avīci mañ‍ñai phuṭau ahausi manussaihi, kuk‍kuṭasaṃpātikā gāmanigamarājadhāniyau’ti| kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu kau pac‍cayau yainaitarahi manussānaṃ khayau hauti, tanuttaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, gāmāpi  agāmā haunti, nigamāpi anigamā haunti, nagarāpi anagarā haunti, janapadāpi ajanapadā hauntī’’ti?

‘‘aitarahi, brāhmaṇa, manussā adhammarāgarattā visamalaubhābhibhūtā micchādhammaparaitā| tai adhammarāgarattā visamalaubhābhibhūtā micchādhammaparaitā tiṇhāni satthāni gahaitvā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa (sabbattha) jīvitā vauraupainti, taina bahū manussā kālaṃ karaunti| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainaitarahi manussānaṃ khayau hauti, tanuttaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, gāmāpi agāmā haunti, nigamāpi anigamā haunti, nagarāpi anagarā haunti, janapadāpi ajanapadā haunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aitarahi manussā adhammarāgarattā visamalaubhābhibhūtā micchādhammaparaitā| taisaṃ adhammarāgarattānaṃ visamalaubhābhibhūtānaṃ micchādhammaparaitānaṃ daivau na sammādhāraṃ anuppavaicchati| taina dubbhikkhaṃ hauti dussassaṃ saitaṭṭhikaṃ salākāvuttaṃ| taina bahū manussā kālaṃ karaunti| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainaitarahi manussānaṃ khayau hauti, tanuttaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, gāmāpi agāmā haunti, nigamāpi anigamā haunti, nagarāpi anagarā haunti, janapadāpi ajanapadā haunti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aitarahi manussā adhammarāgarattā visamalaubhābhibhūtā micchādhammaparaitā| taisaṃ adhammarāgarattānaṃ visamalaubhābhibhūtānaṃ micchādhammaparaitānaṃ yakkhā vāḷai amanussai aussaj‍janti aussajanti (sī॰), taina bahū manussā kālaṃ karaunti| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau yainaitarahi manussānaṃ khayau hauti, tanuttaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, gāmāpi agāmā haunti, nigamāpi anigamā haunti, nagarāpi anagarā haunti, janapadāpi ajanapadā hauntī’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vacchagauttasuttaṃ

58. atha  khau vacchagauttau vacchaputtau (ka॰) paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā  saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhau gautama, samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – ‘mayhamaiva dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ; mayhamaiva din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ; mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphala’nti| yai tai, bhau gautama, aivamāhaṃsu ‘samaṇau gautamau aivamāha mayhamaiva dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ| mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ dānaṃ dātabbaṃ| mayhamaiva din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ| mayhamaiva sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, nāñ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphala’nti| kac‍ci tai bhautau gautamassa vuttavādinau ca bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraunti, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānupātau vādānuvādau (ka॰) gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati? anabbhakkhātukāmā hi mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautama’’nti|

‘‘yai tai, vaccha, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘samaṇau gautamau aivamāha – mayhamaiva dānaṃ dātabbaṃ…pai॰… nāñ‍ñaisaṃ sāvakānaṃ din‍naṃ mahapphala’nti na mai tai vuttavādinau| abbhācikkhanti ca pana maṃ ca pana maṃ tai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) asatā abhūtaina| yau khau, vaccha, paraṃ dānaṃ dadantaṃ vāraiti sau tiṇṇaṃ antarāyakarau hauti, tiṇṇaṃ pāripanthikau| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ? dāyakassa puñ‍ñantarāyakarau  hauti, paṭiggāhakānaṃ lābhantarāyakarau hauti, pubbaiva khau panassa attā khatau ca hauti upahatau ca| yau khau, vaccha, paraṃ dānaṃ dadantaṃ vāraiti sau imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ antarāyakarau hauti, tiṇṇaṃ pāripanthikau|

‘‘ahaṃ khau pana, vaccha, aivaṃ vadāmi – yai hi tai candanikāya vā auligal‍lai vā pāṇā, tatrapi yau thālidhauvanaṃ thālakadhauvanaṃ (ka॰) vā sarāvadhauvanaṃ vā chaḍḍaiti – yai  tattha pāṇā tai taina yāpaintūti, tatau nidānampāhaṃ, vaccha, puñ‍ñassa āgamaṃ vadāmi| kau pana vādau manussabhūtai! api cāhaṃ, vaccha, sīlavatau din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ vadāmi, nau tathā dussīlassa, sau ca hauti pañ‍caṅgavippahīnau pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍caṅgāni pahīnāni haunti? kāmacchandau pahīnau hauti, byāpādau pahīnau hauti, thinamiddhaṃ  pahīnaṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, vicikicchā pahīnā hauti| imāni pañ‍caṅgāni vippahīnāni haunti|

‘‘katamaihi pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti; imaihi pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti| iti pañ‍caṅgavippahīnai pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatai din‍naṃ mahapphalanti vadāmī’’ti|

‘‘iti kaṇhāsu saitāsu, rauhiṇīsu harīsu vā|

kammāsāsu sarūpāsu, gausu pāraivatāsu vā||

‘‘yāsu  kāsuci aitāsu, dantau jāyati puṅgavau|

dhaurayhau balasampan‍nau, kalyāṇajavanik‍kamau|

tamaiva bhārai yuñ‍janti, nāssa vaṇṇaṃ parikkharai||

‘‘aivamaivaṃ manussaisu, yasmiṃ kasmiñ‍ci jātiyai|

khattiyai brāhmaṇai vaissai, suddai caṇḍālapuk‍kusai||

‘‘yāsu kāsuci aitāsu, dantau jāyati subbatau|

dhammaṭṭhau sīlasampan‍nau, sac‍cavādī hirīmanau||

‘‘pahīnajātimaraṇau, brahmacariyassa kaivalī|

pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau, katakic‍cau anāsavau||

‘‘pāragū sabbadhammānaṃ, anupādāya nibbutau|

tasmiṃyaiva tasmiṃ vai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) virajai khaittai, vipulā hauti dakkhiṇā||

‘‘bālā  ca avijānantā, dummaidhā assutāvinau|

bahiddhā dainti dānāni, na hi santai upāsarai||

‘‘yai ca santai upāsanti, sappañ‍ñai dhīrasammatai|

saddhā ca naisaṃ sugatai, mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā||

‘‘daivalaukañ‍ca tai yanti, kulai vā idha jāyarai|

anupubbaina nibbānaṃ, adhigacchanti paṇḍitā’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. tikaṇṇasuttaṃ

59. atha  khau tikaṇṇau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau tikaṇṇau  brāhmaṇau bhagavatau sammukhā taivij‍jānaṃ sudaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – ‘‘aivampi taivij‍jā brāhmaṇā, itipi taivij‍jā brāhmaṇā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ taivij‍jaṃ pañ‍ñāpaintī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca, saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā, akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, ajjhāyakau, mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayauti| aivaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā taivij‍jaṃ pañ‍ñāpaintī’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñathā khau, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ taivij‍jaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hautī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hauti? sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hautī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau tikaṇṇau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pītiyā  ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā  ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ  upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattārīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi, anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ| tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| ayamassa paṭhamā vij‍jā adhigatā hauti; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ  cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā…pai॰… manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ  vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā, manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā  ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā  sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| ayamassa dutiyā vij‍jā adhigatā hauti; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘imai āsavā’ti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayamassa tatiyā vij‍jā adhigatā hauti; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau’’ti|

‘‘anuc‍cāvacasīlassa, nipakassa ca jhāyinau|

cittaṃ yassa vasībhūtaṃ, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

‘‘taṃ vai tamaunudaṃ dhīraṃ, taivij‍jaṃ mac‍cuhāyinaṃ|

hitaṃ daivamanussānaṃ, āhu sabbappahāyinaṃ||

‘‘tīhi vij‍jāhi sampan‍naṃ, asammūḷhavihārinaṃ|

buddhaṃ antimadaihinaṃ antimasārīraṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), taṃ namassanti gautamaṃ||

dha॰ pa॰ 423; itivu॰ 99 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ  yau vaidī, saggāpāyañ‍ca passati|

athau jātikkhayaṃ pattau, abhiñ‍ñāvausitau muni||

‘‘aitāhi tīhi vij‍jāhi, taivij‍jau hauti brāhmaṇau|

tamahaṃ vadāmi taivij‍jaṃ, nāñ‍ñaṃ lapitalāpana’’nti||

‘‘aivaṃ  khau, brāhmaṇa, ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hautī’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñathā, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ taivij‍jau, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hauti| imassa ca pana, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jassa brāhmaṇānaṃ taivij‍jau kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ’’|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. jāṇussauṇisuttaṃ

60. atha khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yassassu, bhau gautama, yañ‍ñau vā saddhaṃ vā thālipākau vā daiyyadhammaṃ vā, taivij‍jaisu brāhmaṇaisu dānaṃ dadaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇā taivij‍jaṃ pañ‍ñapaintī’’ti? ‘‘idha khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau ubhatau sujātau hauti mātitau ca pititau ca saṃsuddhagahaṇikau yāva sattamā pitāmahayugā akkhittau anupak‍kuṭṭhau jātivādaina, ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū sanighaṇḍukaiṭubhānaṃ sākkharappabhaidānaṃ itihāsapañ‍camānaṃ, padakau, vaiyyākaraṇau, laukāyatamahāpurisalakkhaṇaisu anavayauti| aivaṃ khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā taivij‍jaṃ pañ‍ñapaintī’’ti|

‘‘añ‍ñathā khau, brāhmaṇa, brāhmaṇā brāhmaṇaṃ taivij‍jaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hautī’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hauti? sādhu mai bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hautī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai  mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| ayamassa paṭhamā vij‍jā adhigatā hauti; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| ayamassa dutiyā vij‍jā adhigatā hauti; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau|

‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰…  ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau  aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati; vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayamassa tatiyā vij‍jā adhigatā hauti; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau’’ti|

‘‘yau sīlabbatasampan‍nau, pahitattau samāhitau|

cittaṃ yassa vasībhūtaṃ, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

dha॰ pa॰ 423; itivu॰ 99 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ yau vaidī, saggāpāyañ‍ca passati|

athau jātikkhayaṃ pattau, abhiñ‍ñāvausitau muni||

‘‘aitāhi  tīhi vij‍jāhi, taivij‍jau hauti brāhmaṇau|

tamahaṃ vadāmi taivij‍jaṃ, nāñ‍ñaṃ lapitalāpana’’nti||

‘‘aivaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hautī’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñathā, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇānaṃ taivij‍jau, añ‍ñathā ca pana ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jau hauti| imassa ca, bhau gautama, ariyassa vinayai taivij‍jassa brāhmaṇānaṃ taivij‍jau kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ’’|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. saṅgāravasuttaṃ

61. atha  khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayamassu, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā nāma| yañ‍ñaṃ yajāmapi yajāpaimapi| tatra, bhau gautama, yau caiva yajati yau caiva yañ‍ñaṃ yajati (syā॰ kaṃ॰) yau ca yajāpaiti sabbai tai anaikasārīrikaṃ puñ‍ñappaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nā haunti, yadidaṃ yañ‍ñādhikaraṇaṃ| yau panāyaṃ, bhau gautama, yassa vā tassa vā kulā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau aikamattānaṃ damaiti, aikamattānaṃ samaiti, aikamattānaṃ parinibbāpaiti, aivamassāyaṃ aikasārīrikaṃ puñ‍ñappaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, yadidaṃ pabbaj‍jādhikaraṇa’’nti|

‘‘taina hi, brāhmaṇa, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi| yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, idha tathāgatau laukai  uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau aivamāha – ‘aithāyaṃ maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā yathāpaṭipan‍nau ahaṃ anuttaraṃ brahmacariyaugadhaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaimi; aitha aitaṃ (ka॰), tumhaipi tathā paṭipaj‍jatha, yathāpaṭipan‍nā tumhaipi anuttaraṃ brahmacariyaugadhaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti| iti ayañ‍caiva sayaṃ caiva (ka॰) satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti, parai ca tathatthāya  paṭipaj‍janti, tāni khau pana haunti anaikānipi satāni anaikānipi sahassāni anaikānipi satasahassāni|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, ic‍cāyaṃ aivaṃ santai aikasārīrikā vā puñ‍ñappaṭipadā hauti anaikasārīrikā vā, yadidaṃ pabbaj‍jādhikaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘ic‍cāyampi  ic‍cāyantai (ka॰), bhau gautama, aivaṃ santai anaikasārīrikā puñ‍ñappaṭipadā hauti, yadidaṃ pabbaj‍jādhikaraṇa’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau saṅgāravaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imāsaṃ tai, brāhmaṇa, dvin‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ katamā paṭipadā khamati appatthatarā ca appasamārambhatarā ca mahapphalatarā ca  mahānisaṃsatarā cā’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi bhavaṃ gautamau bhavaṃ cānandau| aitai mai puj‍jā, aitai mai pāsaṃsā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau saṅgāravaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃ pucchāmi – ‘kai vā tai puj‍jā kai vā tai pāsaṃsā’ti? aivaṃ khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, pucchāmi – ‘imāsaṃ tai, brāhmaṇa, dvin‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ katamā paṭipadā khamati appatthatarā ca appasamārambhatarā ca mahapphalatarā ca mahānisaṃsatarā cā’’’ti? dutiyampi khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi bhavaṃ gautamau bhavaṃ cānandau| aitai mai puj‍jā, aitai mai pāsaṃsā’’ti|

tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau saṅgāravaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃ pucchāmi – ‘kai vā tai puj‍jā kai vā tai pāsaṃsā’ti? aivaṃ khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, pucchāmi – ‘imāsaṃ tai, brāhmaṇa, dvin‍naṃ paṭipadānaṃ katamā paṭipadā khamati appatthatarā ca appasamārambhatarā ca mahapphalatarā  ca mahānisaṃsatarā cā’’’ti? tatiyampi khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi bhavaṃ gautamau bhavaṃ cānandau| aitai mai puj‍jā, aitai mai  pāsaṃsā’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘yāva tatiyampi khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau ānandaina sahadhammikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau saṃsādaiti ma॰ ni॰ 1.337 nau vissaj‍jaiti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ parimaucaiyya’’nti| atha khau bhagavā saṅgāravaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kā nvaj‍ja, brāhmaṇa, rājantaipurai rājapurisānaṃ rājaparisāyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ antarākathā udapādī’’ti? ‘‘ayaṃ khvaj‍ja, bhau gautama, rājantaipurai rājapurisānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ antarākathā udapādi – ‘pubbai sudaṃ appatarā caiva bhikkhū ahaisuṃ bahutarā ca uttari manussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaisuṃ; aitarahi pana bahutarā caiva bhikkhū appatarā ca uttari manussadhammā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaintī’ti| ayaṃ khvaj‍ja, bhau gautama, rājantaipurai rājapurisānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ antarākathā udapādī’’ti|

paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.30; dī॰ ni॰ 1.483 ‘‘tīṇi khau imāni, brāhmaṇa, pāṭihāriyāni| katamāni tīṇi? iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ, ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaṃ, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti – ‘aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hauti, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau hauti; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchati, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauti, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchati, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamati, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasati parāmasati (dī॰ ni॰ 1.484; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.102 parimaj‍jati, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti’| idaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaṃ? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau nimittaina ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti| sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā|

‘‘idha  pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau na haiva khau nimittaina ādisati, api ca khau manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti| sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā|

‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau na haiva khau nimittaina ādisati napi manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati, api ca khau vitak‍kayatau vicārayatau vitak‍kavipphārasaddaṃ sutvā ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti| sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā|

‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau na haiva khau nimittaina ādisati, napi manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati, napi vitak‍kayatau vicārayatau vitak‍kavipphārasaddaṃ sutvā ādisati, api ca khau avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nassa caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – ‘yathā imassa bhautau manausaṅkhārā paṇihitā imassa  cittassa anantarā amuṃ nāma vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaissatī’ti| sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, brāhmaṇa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ? idha, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau aivamanusāsati – ‘aivaṃ vitak‍kaitha, mā aivaṃ vitak‍kayittha; aivaṃ manasi karautha, mā aivaṃ manasākattha; idaṃ pajahatha, idaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaṃ| imāni khau, brāhmaṇa, tīṇi pāṭihāriyāni| imaisaṃ tai, brāhmaṇa, tiṇṇaṃ pāṭihāriyānaṃ katamaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ khamati abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍cā’’ti?

‘‘tatra, bhau gautama, yadidaṃ yamidaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pāṭihāriyaṃ idhaikac‍cau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti…pai॰… yāva brāhmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti, idaṃ, bhau gautama, pāṭihāriyaṃ yauva yau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) naṃ karauti sauva sauca ca (syā॰ kaṃ pī॰ ka॰) naṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yauva yau ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) naṃ karauti tassaiva tassamaiva (sī॰ ka॰), tassa caiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) taṃ hauti| idaṃ mai, bhau gautama, pāṭihāriyaṃ māyāsahadhammarūpaṃ viya khāyati|

‘‘yampidaṃ, bhau gautama, pāṭihāriyaṃ idhaikac‍cau nimittaina ādisati – ‘aivampi tai manau, itthampi tai manau, itipi tai citta’nti, sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| idha pana, bhau gautama, aikac‍cau na haiva khau nimittaina ādisati, api ca khau manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati…pai॰… napi manussānaṃ vā amanussānaṃ vā daivatānaṃ vā saddaṃ sutvā ādisati, api ca khau vitak‍kayatau vicārayatau vitak‍kavipphārasaddaṃ sutvā ādisati…pai॰… napi vitak‍kayatau vicārayatau vitak‍kavipphārasaddaṃ sutvā ādisati, api ca khau avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nassa caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – ‘yathā  imassa bhautau manausaṅkhārā paṇihitā imassa cittassa anantarā amhaṃ nāma vitak‍kaṃ  vitak‍kaissatī’ti, sau bahuṃ caipi ādisati tathaiva taṃ hauti, nau añ‍ñathā| idampi, bhau gautama, pāṭihāriyaṃ yauva naṃ karauti sauva naṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yauva naṃ karauti tassaiva taṃ hauti| idampi mai, bhau gautama, pāṭihāriyaṃ māyāsahadhammarūpaṃ viya khāyati|

‘‘yañ‍ca khau idaṃ, bhau gautama, pāṭihāriyaṃ idhaikac‍cau aivaṃ anusāsati – ‘aivaṃ vitak‍kaitha, mā aivaṃ vitak‍kayittha; aivaṃ manasi karautha, mā aivaṃ manasākattha; idaṃ pajahatha, idaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’ti| idamaiva, bhau gautama, pāṭihāriyaṃ khamati imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ pāṭihāriyānaṃ abhik‍kantatarañ‍ca paṇītatarañ‍ca|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāvasubhāsitamidaṃ bhautā gautamaina imaihi ca mayaṃ tīhi pāṭihāriyaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ dhāraima| bhavañhi gautamau anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti, bhavañhi gautamau avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nassa caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti – ‘yathā imassa bhautau manausaṅkhārā paṇihitā imassa cittassa anantarā amuṃ nāma vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaissatī’ti| bhavañhi gautamau aivamanusāsati – ‘aivaṃ vitak‍kaitha, mā aivaṃ vitak‍kayittha; aivaṃ manasi karautha, mā aivaṃ manasākattha; idaṃ pajahatha, idaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’’’ti|

‘‘addhā  khau tyāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, āsaj‍ja upanīya vācā bhāsitā; api ca tyāhaṃ byākarissāmi| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaumi…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaimi| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhiṃ samāpan‍nassa caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi – ‘yathā imassa bhautau manausaṅkhārā paṇihitā, imassa cittassa anantarā amuṃ nāma vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaissatī’ti| ahañhi, brāhmaṇa, aivamanusāsāmi – ‘aivaṃ vitak‍kaitha, mā aivaṃ vitak‍kayittha; aivaṃ manasi karautha, mā aivaṃ manasākattha; idaṃ pajahatha, idaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharathā’’’ti|

‘‘atthi pana, bhau gautama, añ‍ñau aikabhikkhupi yau imaihi tīhi pāṭihāriyaihi saman‍nāgatau, añ‍ñatra bhautā gautamainā’’ti? ‘‘na khau, brāhmaṇa, aikaṃyaiva sataṃ na dvai satāni na tīṇi satāni na cattāri satāni na pañ‍ca satāni, atha khau bhiyyauva, yai tai (ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 2.195 bhikkhū imaihi tīhi pāṭihāriyaihi saman‍nāgatā’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ pana, bhau gautama, aitarahi tai bhikkhū viharantī’’ti? ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva  khau, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhusaṅghai’’ti|

‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai  vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – ‘cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantī’ti, aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti| dasamaṃ|

brāhmaṇavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai  brāhmaṇā cañ‍ñatarau, paribbājakaina nibbutaṃ|

palaukavacchau tikaṇṇau, sauṇi saṅgāravaina cāti||


(7) 2. mahāvaggau

1. titthāyatanādisuttaṃ

62. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, titthāyatanāni yāni paṇḍitaihi samanuyuñ‍jiyamānāni samanuggāhiyamānāni (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) samanugāhiyamānāni samanubhāsiyamānāni parampi gantvā akiriyāya saṇṭhahanti| katamāni tīṇi? santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitū’ti| santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ issaranimmānahaitū’ti| santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ ahaituappac‍cayā’’’ti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitū’ti, tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ pubbaikatahaitū’ti? tai ca mai tai cai mai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmā’ti āmauti (sī॰) paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ  aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘tainahāyasmantau pāṇātipātinau bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, adin‍nādāyinau bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, abrahmacārinau bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, musāvādinau bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, pisuṇavācā bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, pharusavācā bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, samphappalāpinau bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, abhijjhālunau bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, byāpan‍nacittā bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu, micchādiṭṭhikā bhavissanti pubbaikatahaitu’’’|

‘‘pubbaikataṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, sāratau pac‍cāgacchataṃ na hauti chandau vā vāyāmau vā idaṃ vā karaṇīyaṃ idaṃ vā akaraṇīyanti| iti karaṇīyākaraṇīyai khau pana sac‍catau thaitatau  anupalabbhiyamānai muṭṭhassatīnaṃ anārakkhānaṃ viharataṃ na hauti pac‍cattaṃ sahadhammikau samaṇavādau| ayaṃ khau mai, bhikkhavai, taisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu aivaṃvādīsu aivaṃdiṭṭhīsu paṭhamau sahadhammikau niggahau hauti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ issaranimmānahaitū’ti, tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃvādinau aivadiṭṭhinau – yaṃ kiñ‍cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ issaranimmānahaitū’ti? tai ca mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmā’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘tainahāyasmantau pāṇātipātinau bhavissanti  issaranimmānahaitu, adin‍nādāyinau bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, abrahmacārinau bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, musāvādinau bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, pisuṇavācā bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, pharusavācā bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, samphappalāpinau bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, abhijjhālunau bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, byāpan‍nacittā bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu, micchādiṭṭhikā bhavissanti issaranimmānahaitu’’’|

‘‘issaranimmānaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, sāratau pac‍cāgacchataṃ na hauti chandau vā vāyāmau vā idaṃ vā karaṇīyaṃ idaṃ vā akaraṇīyanti| iti karaṇīyākaraṇīyai khau pana sac‍catau thaitatau anupalabbhiyamānai muṭṭhassatīnaṃ anārakkhānaṃ viharataṃ na hauti pac‍cattaṃ sahadhammikau samaṇavādau| ayaṃ khau mai, bhikkhavai, taisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu aivaṃvādīsu aivaṃdiṭṭhīsu dutiyau sahadhammikau niggahau hauti|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘yaṃ kiṃ cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ ahaituappac‍cayā’ti, tyāhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai āyasmantau aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – yaṃ kiṃ cāyaṃ purisapuggalau paṭisaṃvaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā sabbaṃ taṃ ahaituappac‍cayā’ti? tai ca mai aivaṃ puṭṭhā ‘āmā’ti paṭijānanti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘tainahāyasmantau pāṇātipātinau bhavissanti ahaituappac‍cayā…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhikā bhavissanti ahaituappac‍cayā’’’|

‘‘ahaituappac‍cayaṃ  ahaituṃ (sī॰), ahaitu (syā॰ kaṃ॰), ahaituappac‍cayā (pī॰), ahaituṃ appac‍cayaṃ (ka॰) khau pana, bhikkhavai, sāratau pac‍cāgacchataṃ na hauti chandau vā vāyāmau vā idaṃ vā karaṇīyaṃ idaṃ vā akaraṇīyanti| iti karaṇīyākaraṇīyai khau pana sac‍catau thaitatau anupalabbhiyamānai muṭṭhassatīnaṃ anārakkhānaṃ viharataṃ na hauti pac‍cattaṃ sahadhammikau samaṇavādau| ayaṃ khau mai, bhikkhavai, taisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu aivaṃvādīsu aivaṃdiṭṭhīsu tatiyau  sahadhammikau niggahau hauti|

‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi titthāyatanāni yāni paṇḍitaihi samanuyuñ‍jiyamānāni samanugāhiyamānāni samanubhāsiyamānāni parampi gantvā akiriyāya saṇṭhahanti|

‘‘ayaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi? imā cha dhātuyauti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| imāni cha phassāyatanānīti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| imai aṭṭhārasa manaupavicārāti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cānīti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi|

‘‘imā cha dhātuyauti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? chayimā, bhikkhavai, dhātuyau – pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu| imā  cha dhātuyauti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau  anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘imāni  cha phassāyatanānīti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? chayimāni, bhikkhavai, phassāyatanāni – cakkhu phassāyatanaṃ, sautaṃ phassāyatanaṃ, ghānaṃ phassāyatanaṃ, jivhā phassāyatanaṃ, kāyau phassāyatanaṃ, manau phassāyatanaṃ| imāni cha phassāyatanānīti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘imai aṭṭhārasa manaupavicārāti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā saumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati daumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati upaikkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ rūpaṃ upavicarati, sautaina saddaṃ sutvā… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya saumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati daumanassaṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati upaikkhāṭṭhāniyaṃ dhammaṃ upavicarati| imai aṭṭhārasa manaupavicārāti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti  yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cānīti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? chan‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, dhātūnaṃ upādāya gabbhassāvak‍kanti hauti; auk‍kantiyā sati nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā| vaidiyamānassa khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, idaṃ dukkhanti pañ‍ñapaimi, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayauti pañ‍ñapaimi, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhauti pañ‍ñapaimi, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti pañ‍ñapaimi|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, ( ) (byādhipi dukkhau) (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) aṭṭhakathāya saṃsandaitabbaṃ visuddhi॰ 2.537 maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā, (appiyaihi  sampayaugau dukkhau, piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau,) (natthi katthaci) yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ| saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ? avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ariyasac‍caṃ? avij‍jāya tvaiva  asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ| ‘imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cānī’ti, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau daisitau aniggahitau asaṃkiliṭṭhau anupavaj‍jau appaṭikuṭṭhau samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhīti| iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

63. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, amātāputtikāni bhayānīti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati| katamāni tīṇi? hauti sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ  mahāaggiḍāhau vuṭṭhāti| mahāaggiḍāhai  khau pana, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhitai taina gāmāpi ḍayhanti nigamāpi ḍayhanti nagarāpi ḍayhanti| gāmaisupi ḍayhamānaisu nigamaisupi ḍayhamānaisu nagaraisupi ḍayhamānaisu tattha mātāpi puttaṃ nappaṭilabhati, puttaupi mātaraṃ nappaṭilabhati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ amātāputtikaṃ bhayanti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti sau samayau yaṃ mahāmaighau vuṭṭhāti| mahāmaighai khau pana, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhitai mahāudakavāhakau sañ‍jāyati| mahāudakavāhakai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sañ‍jāyantai taina gāmāpi vuyhanti nigamāpi vuyhanti nagarāpi vuyhanti| gāmaisupi vuyhamānaisu nigamaisupi vuyhamānaisu nagaraisupi vuyhamānaisu tattha mātāpi puttaṃ nappaṭilabhati, puttaupi mātaraṃ nappaṭilabhati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ amātāputtikaṃ bhayanti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti sau samayau yaṃ bhayaṃ hauti aṭavisaṅkaupau, cak‍kasamāruḷhā jānapadā pariyāyanti| bhayai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sati aṭavisaṅkaupai cak‍kasamāruḷhaisu jānapadaisu pariyāyantaisu tattha mātāpi puttaṃ nappaṭilabhati, puttaupi mātaraṃ nappaṭilabhati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ amātāputtikaṃ bhayanti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi amātāputtikāni bhayānīti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati|

‘‘tāni khau panimāni imāni khau (sī॰), imāni khau pana (ka॰), bhikkhavai, tīṇi samātāputtikāniyaiva bhayāni amātāputtikāni bhayānīti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati| katamāni tīṇi? hauti sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ mahāaggiḍāhau vuṭṭhāti| mahāaggiḍāhai khau pana, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhitai taina gāmāpi ḍayhanti nigamāpi ḍayhanti nagarāpi ḍayhanti| gāmaisupi ḍayhamānaisu nigamaisupi ḍayhamānaisu nagaraisupi ḍayhamānaisu hauti sau samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci mātāpi puttaṃ  paṭilabhati, puttaupi mātaraṃ paṭilabhati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ samātāputtikaṃyaiva bhayaṃ amātāputtikaṃ bhayanti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti sau samayau yaṃ mahāmaighau vuṭṭhāti| mahāmaighai khau pana, bhikkhavai, vuṭṭhitai mahāudakavāhakau sañ‍jāyati| mahāudakavāhakai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sañ‍jātai  taina gāmāpi vuyhanti nigamāpi vuyhanti nagarāpi vuyhanti| gāmaisupi vuyhamānaisu nigamaisupi vuyhamānaisu nagaraisupi vuyhamānaisu hauti sau samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci mātāpi puttaṃ paṭilabhati, puttaupi mātaraṃ paṭilabhati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyaṃ samātāputtikaṃyaiva bhayaṃ amātāputtikaṃ bhayanti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti sau samayau yaṃ bhayaṃ hauti aṭavisaṅkaupau, cak‍kasamāruḷhā jānapadā pariyāyanti| bhayai khau pana, bhikkhavai, sati aṭavisaṅkaupai cak‍kasamāruḷhaisu jānapadaisu pariyāyantaisu hauti sau samayau yaṃ kadāci karahaci mātāpi puttaṃ paṭilabhati, puttaupi mātaraṃ paṭilabhati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyaṃ samātāputtikaṃyaiva bhayaṃ amātāputtikaṃ bhayanti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati| ‘‘imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi samātāputtikāniyaiva bhayāni amātāputtikāni bhayānīti assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati’’|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, amātāputtikāni bhayāni| katamāni tīṇi? jarābhayaṃ, byādhibhayaṃ, maraṇabhayanti| na, bhikkhavai, mātā puttaṃ jīramānaṃ aivaṃ labhati – ‘ahaṃ jīrāmi, mā mai puttau jīrī’ti; puttau vā pana mātaraṃ jīramānaṃ na aivaṃ labhati – ‘ahaṃ jīrāmi, mā mai  mātā jīrī’’’ti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mātā puttaṃ byādhiyamānaṃ aivaṃ labhati – ‘ahaṃ byādhiyāmi, mā mai puttau byādhiyī’ti; puttau vā pana mātaraṃ byādhiyamānaṃ na aivaṃ labhati – ‘ahaṃ byādhiyāmi, mā mai mātā byādhiyī’’’ti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mātā puttaṃ mīyamānaṃ aivaṃ labhati – ‘ahaṃ mīyāmi, mā mai puttau mīyī’ti; puttau vā pana mātaraṃ mīyamānaṃ na aivaṃ labhati – ‘ahaṃ mīyāmi, mā mai mātā mīyī’ti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi amātāputtikāni bhayānī’’ti|

‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, maggau atthi paṭipadā imaisañ‍ca tiṇṇaṃ samātāputtikānaṃ bhayānaṃ imaisañ‍ca tiṇṇaṃ amātāputtikānaṃ bhayānaṃ pahānāya samatik‍kamāya saṃvattati| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, maggau katamā ca paṭipadā  imaisañ‍ca tiṇṇaṃ samātāputtikānaṃ bhayānaṃ imaisañ‍ca tiṇṇaṃ  amātāputtikānaṃ bhayānaṃ pahānāya samatik‍kamāya saṃvattati? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkapau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, maggau ayaṃ paṭipadā imaisañ‍ca tiṇṇaṃ samātāputtikānaṃ bhayānaṃ imaisañ‍ca tiṇṇaṃ amātāputtikānaṃ bhayānaṃ pahānāya samatik‍kamāya saṃvattatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. vaināgapurasuttaṃ

64. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina vaināgapuraṃ nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| assausuṃ khau vaināgapurikā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau vaināgapuraṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti bhagavā (sī॰ syā kaṃ॰ pī॰) idaṃ suttavaṇṇanāya aṭṭhakathāya saṃsandaitabbaṃ pārā॰ 1; dī॰ ni॰ 1.255 passitabbaṃ| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau vaināgapurikā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, appaikac‍cai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vaināgapurikau vacchagauttau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāvañ‍cidaṃ bhautau gautamassa vippasan‍nāni indriyāni, parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, sāradaṃ badarapaṇḍuṃ maṇḍaṃ (ka॰) parisuddhaṃ hauti pariyaudātaṃ; aivamaivaṃ bhautau gautamassa vippasan‍nāni indriyāni parisuddhau  chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, tālapak‍kaṃ sampati bandhanā pamuttaṃ muttaṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) parisuddhaṃ hauti pariyaudātaṃ; aivamaivaṃ bhautau gautamassa vippasan‍nāni indriyāni parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, naikkhaṃ nikkhaṃ-itipi (ma॰ ni॰ 3.168) jambaunadaṃ dakkhakammāraputtasuparikammakataṃ uk‍kāmukhai sukusalasampahaṭṭhaṃ paṇḍukambalai nikkhittaṃ bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca; aivamaivaṃ bhautau gautamassa vippasan‍nāni indriyāni parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau| yāni tāni, bhau gautama, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – āsandi pal‍laṅkau gaunakau cittakau paṭikā paṭalikā tūlikā vikatikā uddalaumī aikantalaumī kaṭṭissaṃ kausaiyyaṃ kuttakaṃ hatthattharaṃ assattharaṃ rathattharaṃ ajinappavaiṇī kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ kādalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ (sī॰) sauttaracchadaṃ ubhataulauhitakūpadhānaṃ, aivarūpānaṃ nūna bhavaṃ gautamau uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanānaṃ nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti|

‘‘yāni khau pana tāni, brāhmaṇa, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – āsandi pal‍laṅkau gaunakau cittakau paṭikā paṭalikā tūlikā vikatikā uddalaumī aikantalaumī kaṭṭissaṃ kausaiyyaṃ kuttakaṃ hatthattharaṃ assattharaṃ rathattharaṃ ajinappavaiṇī kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ sauttaracchadaṃ ubhataulauhitakūpadhānaṃ| dul‍labhāni tāni pabbajitānaṃ laddhā ca pana laddhāni ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), laddhā ca (pī॰) na kappanti|

‘‘tīṇi khau, imāni, brāhmaṇa, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni, yaisāhaṃ aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| katamāni tīṇi? dibbaṃ  uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, brahmaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, ariyaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ| imāni khau, brāhmaṇa, tīṇi uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni, yaisāhaṃ aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti|

‘‘katamaṃ  pana taṃ, bhau gautama, dibbaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, yassa bhavaṃ gautamau aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yaṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharāmi, sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisāmi| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vanantañ‍ñaiva pavisāmi pacārayāmi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| sau yadaiva tattha haunti tiṇāni vā paṇṇāni vā tāni aikajjhaṃ saṅgharitvā nisīdāmi pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharāmi satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaimi, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau caṅkamāmi, dibbau mai aisau tasmiṃ samayai caṅkamau hauti| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau tiṭṭhāmi, dibbaṃ mai aitaṃ tasmiṃ samayai ṭhānaṃ hauti| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau nisīdāmi, dibbaṃ mai aitaṃ tasmiṃ samayai  āsanaṃ hauti| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau saiyyaṃ kappaimi, dibbaṃ mai aitaṃ tasmiṃ samayai uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ hauti| idaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, dibbaṃ  uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, yassāhaṃ aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! kau cañ‍ñau aivarūpassa dibbassa uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanassa nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, añ‍ñatra bhautā gautamaina!

‘‘katamaṃ pana taṃ, bhau gautama, brahmaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, yassa bhavaṃ gautamau aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yaṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharāmi, sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ  nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisāmi| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vanantañ‍ñaiva pavisāmi| sau yadaiva tattha haunti tiṇāni vā paṇṇāni vā tāni aikajjhaṃ saṅgharitvā nisīdāmi pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharāmi, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpajjhaina abyāpajjhaina (sabbattha) pharitvā viharāmi| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharāmi, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ catutthiṃ (sī॰), iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya  sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpajjhaina pharitvā viharāmi| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau caṅkamāmi, brahmā mai aisau tasmiṃ samayai caṅkamau hauti| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau tiṭṭhāmi…pai॰… nisīdāmi…pai॰… saiyyaṃ kappaimi, brahmaṃ mai aitaṃ tasmiṃ samayai uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ hauti| idaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, brahmaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, yassāhaṃ aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! kau cañ‍ñau aivarūpassa brahmassa uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanassa nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, añ‍ñatra bhautā gautamaina!

‘‘katamaṃ pana taṃ, bhau gautama, ariyaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, yassa bhavaṃ gautamau aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, yaṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharāmi, sau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tamaiva gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisāmi| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vanantañ‍ñaiva pavisāmi| sau yadaiva tattha haunti tiṇāni vā paṇṇāni vā tāni aikajjhaṃ saṅgharitvā nisīdāmi pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau aivaṃ jānāmi – ‘rāgau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau; dausau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau  tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau; mauhau mai pahīnau ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṅkatau  āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau’| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau caṅkamāmi, ariyau mai aisau tasmiṃ samayai caṅkamau hauti| sau cai ahaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃbhūtau tiṭṭhāmi…pai॰… nisīdāmi…pai॰… saiyyaṃ kappaimi, ariyaṃ mai aitaṃ tasmiṃ samayai uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ hauti| idaṃ khau, brāhmaṇa, ariyaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ, yassāhaṃ aitarahi nikāmalābhī akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’ti|
