‘‘daivatāpissa daivatāpissa amanussā (sī॰ pī॰), daivatāpissa amanussāpi (ka॰), daivatāpissa…pai॰… manussāpissa (?) vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti – ‘amukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā purisau vā buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatau hauti, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, sīlavā hauti kalyāṇadhammau, vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau’ti| idaṃ khau taṃ, ānanda, gandhajātaṃ yassa anuvātampi gandhau gacchati, paṭivātampi gandhau gacchati, anuvātapaṭivātampi gandhau gacchatī’’ti|

‘‘na pupphagandhau paṭivātamaiti,

na candanaṃ tagaramal‍likā taggaramal‍likā (pī॰) vā|

satañ‍ca gandhau paṭivātamaiti,

sabbā disā sappurisau pavāyatī’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. cūḷanikāsuttaṃ

81. atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sammukhāmaitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau  sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘bhagavatau, ānanda, sikhissa abhibhū nāma sāvakau brahmalaukai ṭhitau sahassilaukadhātuṃ sahassīlaukadhātuṃ (pī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.185 vitthārau saraina viñ‍ñāpaisī’ti| bhagavā pana, bhantai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kīvatakaṃ pahauti saraina  viñ‍ñāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘sāvakau sau, ānanda, appamaiyyā tathāgatā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sammukhā maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘bhagavatau, ānanda, sikhissa abhibhū nāma sāvakau brahmalaukai ṭhitau sahassilaukadhātuṃ saraina viñ‍ñāpaisī’ti| bhagavā pana, bhantai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kīvatakaṃ pahauti saraina viñ‍ñāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘sāvakau sau, ānanda, appamaiyyā tathāgatā’’ti|

tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sammukhāmaitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘bhagavatau, ānanda, sikhissa abhibhū nāma sāvakau brahmalaukai ṭhitau sahassilaukadhātuṃ saraina viñ‍ñāpaisī’ti| bhagavā pana, bhantai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kīvatakaṃ pahauti saraina viñ‍ñāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘sutā tai, ānanda, sahassī cūḷanikā laukadhātū’’ti? ‘‘aitassa, bhagavā, kālau; aitassa, sugata, kālau! yaṃ bhagavā bhāsaiyya| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘tainahānanda, suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yāvatā, ānanda, candimasūriyā candimasuriyā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pariharanti, disā bhanti viraucanā, tāva sahassadhā laukau| tasmiṃ sahassadhā laukai sahassaṃ tasmiṃ sahassaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) candānaṃ, sahassaṃ sūriyānaṃ, sahassaṃ sinairupabbatarājānaṃ, sahassaṃ jambudīpānaṃ, sahassaṃ aparagauyānānaṃ, sahassaṃ uttarakurūnaṃ, sahassaṃ pubbavidaihānaṃ, cattāri mahāsamuddasahassāni, cattāri mahārājasahassāni, sahassaṃ cātumahārājikānaṃ, sahassaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ, sahassaṃ yāmānaṃ, sahassaṃ tusitānaṃ, sahassaṃ nimmānaratīnaṃ, sahassaṃ paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ, sahassaṃ brahmalaukānaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, sahassī cūḷanikā laukadhātu|

‘‘yāvatānanda, sahassī cūḷanikā laukadhātu tāva sahassadhā laukau| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, dvisahassī majjhimikā laukadhātu|

‘‘yāvatānanda, dvisahassī majjhimikā laukadhātu tāva sahassadhā laukau| ayaṃ vuc‍catānanda, tisahassī mahāsahassī laukadhātu|

‘‘ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, tathāgatau tisahassimahāsahassilaukadhātuṃ tisahassi mahāsahassiṃ laukadhātuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tisahassīmahāsahassīlaukadhātuṃ (pī॰) saraina viñ‍ñāpaiyya, yāvatā pana ākaṅkhaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā tisahassimahāsahassilaukadhātuṃ saraina viñ‍ñāpaiyya, yāvatā pana ākaṅkhaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘idhānanda, tathāgatau tisahassimahāsahassilaukadhātuṃ aubhāsaina pharaiyya| yadā tai sattā taṃ ālaukaṃ sañ‍jānaiyyuṃ, atha tathāgatau ghausaṃ karaiyya saddamanussāvaiyya| aivaṃ khau, ānanda, tathāgatau tisahassimahāsahassilaukadhātuṃ saraina viñ‍ñāpaiyya, yāvatā pana ākaṅkhaiyyā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau (āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ) (bhagavantaṃ) (sī॰), ( ) natthi syā॰ kaṃ॰ pautthakaisu| aṭṭhakathāya samaiti aitadavauca – ‘‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yassa mai satthā aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ tuyhaittha, āvusau ānanda, yadi tai satthā aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā haivaṃ, udāyi, mā haivaṃ, udāyi| sacai, udāyi, ānandau avītarāgau kālaṃ karaiyya, taina cittappasādaina sattakkhattuṃ daivaisu daivaraj‍jaṃ kāraiyya, sattakkhattuṃ  imasmiṃyaiva jambudīpai mahāraj‍jaṃ kāraiyya| api ca, udāyi, ānandau diṭṭhaiva dhammai parinibbāyissatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

ānandavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

chan‍nau ājīvakau sak‍kau, nigaṇṭhau ca nivaisakau|

duvai bhavā sīlabbataṃ, gandhajātañ‍ca cūḷanīti||


(9) 4. samaṇavaggau

1. samaṇasuttaṃ

82. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, samaṇassa samaṇiyāni samaṇakaraṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi? adhisīlasikkhāsamādānaṃ, adhicittasikkhāsamādānaṃ, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi samaṇassa samaṇiyāni samaṇakaraṇīyāni|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhisīlasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhicittasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānai’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. gadrabhasuttaṃ

83. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gadrabhau gaugaṇaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhau hauti – ‘ahampi dammau, ahampi dammau’ti ahampi gau amhā ahampi gau amhāti (sī॰), ahampi amhā ahampi amhāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), ahampi gau ahampi gauti (?)| tassa na tādisau vaṇṇau hauti saiyyathāpi gun‍naṃ, na tādisau sarau hauti saiyyathāpi gun‍naṃ, na tādisaṃ padaṃ hauti saiyyathāpi gun‍naṃ| sau gaugaṇaṃyaiva piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhau hauti – ‘ahampi dammau, ahampi dammau’’’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu bhikkhusaṅghaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhau hauti – ‘ahampi bhikkhu, ahampi bhikkhū’ti| tassa na tādisau chandau hauti adhisīlasikkhāsamādānai saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, na tādisau chandau hauti adhicittasikkhāsamādānai saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, na tādisau chandau hauti adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānai saiyyathāpi añ‍ñaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ| sau bhikkhusaṅghaṃyaiva piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhau hauti – ‘ahampi bhikkhu, ahampi bhikkhū’’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhisīlasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhicittasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānai’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. khaittasuttaṃ

84. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, kassakassa gahapatissa pubbai karaṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, kassakau gahapati paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰ pī॰) khaittaṃ sukaṭṭhaṃ karauti sumatikataṃ sumattikataṃ (ka॰), aittha matisaddau kaṭṭhakhaittassa samīkaraṇasādhanai dārubhaṇḍai vattatīti sak‍kataabhidhānaisu āgataṃ| taṃ ‘‘matiyā suṭṭhu samīkata’’nti aṭṭhakathāya samaiti| paṭikac‍caiva khaittaṃ sukaṭṭhaṃ karitvā sumatikataṃ kālaina bījāni patiṭṭhāpaiti| kālaina bījāni patiṭṭhāpaitvā samayaina udakaṃ abhinaitipi apanaitipi| imāni  khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi kassakassa gahapatissa pubbai karaṇīyāni|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇimāni bhikkhussa pubbai karaṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi? adhisīlasikkhāsamādānaṃ, adhicittasikkhāsamādānaṃ, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi bhikkhussa pubbai karaṇīyāni|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhisīlasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau  nau chandau bhavissati adhicittasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānai’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. vaj‍jiputtasuttaṃ

85. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau vaj‍jiputtakau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau vaj‍jiputtakau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhikamidaṃ, bhantai, diyaḍḍhasikkhāpadasataṃ diyaḍḍhaṃ sikkhāpadasataṃ (sī॰) anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchati| nāhaṃ, bhantai, aittha sak‍kaumi sikkhitu’’nti| ‘‘sakkhissasi pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhituṃ – adhisīlasikkhāya, adhicittasikkhāya adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāyā’’ti? ‘‘sak‍kaumahaṃ, bhantai, tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhituṃ – adhisīlasikkhāya, adhicittasikkhāya, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāyā’’ti| ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhassu – adhisīlasikkhāya, adhicittasikkhāya, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāya’’|

‘‘yatau khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, adhisīlampi sikkhissasi, adhicittampi sikkhissasi, adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhissasi, tassa tuyhaṃ bhikkhu adhisīlampi sikkhatau adhicittampi sikkhatau adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhatau rāgau pahīyissati, dausau pahīyissati, mauhau pahīyissati| sau tvaṃ rāgassa pahānā dausassa pahānā mauhassa pahānā yaṃ akusalaṃ na taṃ karissasi, yaṃ pāpaṃ na taṃ saivissasī’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu aparaina samayaina adhisīlampi sikkhi, adhicittampi sikkhi, adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhi| tassa adhisīlampi  sikkhatau adhicittampi sikkhatau adhipañ‍ñampi  sikkhatau rāgau pahīyi, dausau pahīyi, mauhau pahīyi| sau rāgassa pahānā dausassa pahānā mauhassa pahānā yaṃ akusalaṃ taṃ nākāsi, yaṃ pāpaṃ taṃ na saivīti| catutthaṃ|

5. saikkhasuttaṃ

86. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘saikhau, saikhau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saikhau hautī’’ti? ‘‘sikkhatīti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā saikhauti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca sikkhati? adhisīlampi sikkhati, adhicittampi sikkhati, adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhati| sikkhatīti khau, bhikkhu, tasmā saikhauti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘saikhassa  sikkhamānassa, ujumaggānusārinau|

khayasmiṃ paṭhamaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tatau añ‍ñā anantarā||

‘‘tatau añ‍ñāvimuttassa añ‍ñāvimuttiyā (ka॰), ñāṇaṃ vai ñāṇañ‍ca (ka॰) hauti tādinau|

akuppā mai vimuttīti, bhavasaṃyaujanakkhayai’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ| ( ) (aṭṭhamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ niṭṭhitaṃ) (ka॰)

6. paṭhamasikkhāsuttaṃ

87. ‘‘sādhikamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, diyaḍḍhasikkhāpadasataṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchati, yattha attakāmā kulaputtā sikkhanti| tissau imā, bhikkhavai, sikkhā yatthaitaṃ sabbaṃ samaudhānaṃ gacchati| katamā tissau? adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sikkhā, yatthaitaṃ sabbaṃ samaudhānaṃ gacchati|

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ mattasau kārī pañ‍ñāya  mattasau kārī| sau yāni tāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni tāni āpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi maittha, bhikkhavai, abhabbatā vuttā| yāni ca khau tāni sikkhāpadāni ādibrahmacariyakāni brahmacariyasāruppāni, tattha dhuvasīlau dhuvasīlī (sī॰) pu॰ pa॰ 127-129 (thaukaṃ visadisaṃ) ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ṭhitasīlī (sī॰) ca, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā  sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ mattasau kārī pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau yāni tāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni tāni āpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi maittha, bhikkhavai, abhabbatā vuttā| yāni ca khau tāni sikkhāpadāni ādibrahmacariyakāni brahmacariyasāruppāni tattha dhuvasīlau ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ca, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī pañ‍ñāya  mattasau kārī| sau yāni tāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni tāni āpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi maittha, bhikkhavai, abhabbatā vuttā| yāni ca khau tāni sikkhāpadāni ādibrahmacariyakāni brahmacariyasāruppāni tattha dhuvasīlau ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ca, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ  saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī  anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī pañ‍ñāya paripūrakārī| sau yāni tāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni tāni āpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi maittha, bhikkhavai, abhabbatā vuttā| yāni ca khau tāni sikkhāpadāni ādibrahmacariyakāni brahmacariyasāruppāni tattha dhuvasīlau ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ca, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, padaisaṃ padaisakārī ārādhaiti paripūraṃ paripūrakārī| avañjhāni tvaivāhaṃ avañ‍cuvanaivāhaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, sikkhāpadāni vadāmī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyasikkhāsuttaṃ

88. ‘‘sādhikamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, diyaḍḍhasikkhāpadasataṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchati yattha attakāmā kulaputtā  sikkhanti| tissau imā, bhikkhavai, sikkhā yatthaitaṃ sabbaṃ samaudhānaṃ gacchati| katamā tissau? adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sikkhā yatthaitaṃ sabbaṃ samaudhānaṃ gacchati|

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ mattasau kārī pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau yāni tāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni tāni āpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi maittha, bhikkhavai, abhabbatā vuttā| yāni ca khau tāni sikkhāpadāni ādibrahmacariyakāni brahmacariyasāruppāni tattha dhuvasīlau ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ca, samādāya sikkhati  sikkhāpadaisu| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sattakkhattuparamau hauti| sattakkhattuparamaṃ daivai ca manussai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā kaulaṃkaulau hauti, dvai vā tīṇi vā kulāni sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| sau tiṇṇaṃ  saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aikabījī hauti, aikaṃyaiva mānusakaṃ bhavaṃ nibbattaitvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau yāni tāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni tāni āpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi maittha, bhikkhavai, abhabbatā vuttā| yāni ca khau tāni sikkhāpadāni ādibrahmacariyakāni brahmacariyasāruppāni tattha dhuvasīlau ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ca, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī  pañ‍ñāya paripūrakārī| sau yāni tāni  dhuvasīlau ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ca, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, padaisaṃ padaisakārī ārādhaiti, paripūraṃ paripūrakārī, avañjhāni tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sikkhāpadāni vadāmī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. tatiyasikkhāsuttaṃ

89. ‘‘sādhikamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, diyaḍḍhasikkhāpadasataṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchati yattha attakāmā kulaputtā sikkhanti| tissau imā, bhikkhavai, sikkhā  yatthaitaṃ sabbaṃ samaudhānaṃ gacchati| katamā tissau? adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sikkhā yatthaitaṃ sabbaṃ samaudhānaṃ gacchati|

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī pañ‍ñāya paripūrakārī| sau yāni tāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni tāni āpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi| taṃ kissa haitu? na hi maittha, bhikkhavai, abhabbatā vuttā| yāni ca khau tāni sikkhāpadāni ādibrahmacariyakāni brahmacariyasāruppāni tattha dhuvasīlau ca hauti ṭhitasīlau ca, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā, rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ  āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aikabījī hauti, aikaṃyaiva mānusakaṃ bhavaṃ nibbattaitvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā kaulaṃkaulau hauti, dvai vā tīṇi vā kulāni sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| taṃ vā pana anabhisambhavaṃ appaṭivijjhaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sattakkhattuparamau hauti, sattakkhattuparamaṃ daivai ca manussai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti|

‘‘iti  khau, bhikkhavai, paripūraṃ paripūrakārī ārādhaiti padaisaṃ padaisakārī| avañjhānitvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sikkhāpadāni vadāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamasikkhattayasuttaṃ

90. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, sikkhā| katamā tissau? adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, adhisīlasikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhisīlasikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, adhicittasikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhicittasikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sikkhā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyasikkhattayasuttaṃ

91. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, sikkhā| katamā tissau? adhisīlasikkhā, adhicittasikkhā, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, adhisīlasikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhisīlasikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, adhicittasikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhicittasikkhā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhipañ‍ñā sikkhā| imā  khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sikkhā’’ti|

‘‘adhisīlaṃ  adhicittaṃ, adhipañ‍ñañ‍ca vīriyavā|

thāmavā dhitimā jhāyī, satau guttindriyau uppattindriyau (ka॰) carai||

‘‘yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai|

yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau||

‘‘yathā  divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā|

abhibhuyya disā sabbā, appamāṇasamādhinā||

‘‘tamāhu saikhaṃ paṭipadaṃ pāṭipadaṃ (?) ma॰ ni॰ 2.27 passitabbaṃ, athau saṃsuddhacāriyaṃ saṃsuddhacāraṇaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), saṃsuddhacārinaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

tamāhu laukai sambuddhaṃ, dhīraṃ paṭipadantaguṃ||

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina, taṇhākkhayavimuttinau|

paj‍jautassaiva nibbānaṃ, vimaukkhau hauti caitasau’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

11. saṅkavāsuttaṃ

92. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina saṅkavā paṅkadhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nāma kausalānaṃ nigamau tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā saṅkavāyaṃ viharati| taina khau pana samayaina kassapagauttau nāma bhikkhu saṅkavāyaṃ āvāsikau hauti| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| atha khau kassapagauttassa bhikkhunau bhagavati sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaintai samādapaintai samuttaijaintai sampahaṃsaintai ahudaiva akkhanti ahu appac‍cayau – ‘‘adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ adhisal‍laikhataivāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) samaṇau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā saṅkavāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ  viharitvā yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| tatra  sudaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati|

atha khau kassapagauttassa bhikkhunau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau ahudaiva kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahu vippaṭisārau – ‘‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yassa mai bhagavati sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaintai samādapaintai samuttaijaintai sampahaṃsaintai ahudaiva akkhanti ahu appac‍cayau – ‘adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau santikai ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau daisaiyya’’nti| atha khau kassapagauttau bhikkhu saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina rājagahaṃ taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina rājagahaṃ yaina gijjhakūṭau pabbatau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kassapagauttau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘aikamidaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ bhagavā saṅkavāyaṃ viharati, saṅkavā nāma kausalānaṃ nigamau| tatra, bhantai, bhagavā sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| tassa mayhaṃ bhagavati sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaintai samādapaintai samuttaijaintai sampahaṃsaintai ahudaiva akkhanti ahu appac‍cayau  – ‘adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti| atha khau bhagavā saṅkavāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| ( ) (anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati| atha khau (ka॰) tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau ahudaiva kuk‍kuc‍caṃ ahu vippaṭisārau – alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yassa mai bhagavati sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaintai samādapaintai samuttaijaintai sampahaṃsaintai ahudaiva akkhanti ahu appac‍cayau – ‘adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau santikai ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau daisaiyyanti| ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ yassa mai bhagavati sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaintai samādapaintai samuttaijaintai sampahaṃsaintai ahudaiva akkhanti ahu appac‍cayau – ‘adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti| tassa  mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu, āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti|

‘‘taggha taṃ taggha tvaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), kassapa, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yassa tai mayi sikkhāpadapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya bhikkhū sandassaintai samādapaintai samuttaijaintai sampahaṃsaintai ahudaiva akkhanti ahu appac‍cayau – ‘adhisal‍likhataivāyaṃ samaṇau’ti| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, kassapa, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuddhihaisā, kassapa, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘thairau caipi, kassapa, bhikkhu hauti na sikkhākāmau na sikkhāsamādānassa vaṇṇavādī, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na sikkhākāmā tai ca na sikkhāya samādapaiti, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū  sikkhākāmā taisañ‍ca na vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, aivarūpassāhaṃ, kassapa, thairassa bhikkhunau na vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? satthā hissa vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇatīti añ‍ñai naṃ taṃ (sī॰ pī॰) bhikkhū bhajaiyyuṃ, yai naṃ bhajaiyyuṃ tyāssa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ, yyāssa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyāti| tasmāhaṃ, kassapa, aivarūpassa thairassa bhikkhunau na vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmi|

‘‘majjhimau caipi, kassapa, bhikkhu hauti…pai॰… navau caipi, kassapa, bhikkhu hauti na sikkhākāmau na sikkhāsamādānassa vaṇṇavādī, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na sikkhākāmā tai ca na sikkhāya samādapaiti, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū sikkhākāmā taisañ‍ca na vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, aivarūpassāhaṃ, kassapa, navassa bhikkhunau na vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? satthā hissa vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇatīti añ‍ñai naṃ bhikkhū bhajaiyyuṃ, yai naṃ bhajaiyyuṃ  tyāssa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ, yyāssa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyāti| tasmāhaṃ, kassapa, aivarūpassa navassa bhikkhunau na vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmi|

‘‘thairau  caipi, kassapa, bhikkhu hauti sikkhākāmau sikkhāsamādānassa vaṇṇavādī, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na sikkhākāmā tai ca sikkhāya samādapaiti, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū sikkhākāmā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati  bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, aivarūpassāhaṃ, kassapa, thairassa bhikkhunau vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? satthā hissa vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇatīti añ‍ñai naṃ bhikkhū bhajaiyyuṃ, yai naṃ bhajaiyyuṃ tyāssa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ, yyāssa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyāti| tasmāhaṃ, kassapa, aivarūpassa thairassa bhikkhunau vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmi|

‘‘majjhimau caipi, kassapa, bhikkhu hauti sikkhākāmau…pai॰… navau caipi, kassapa, bhikkhu hauti sikkhākāmau sikkhāsamādānassa vaṇṇavādī, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū na sikkhākāmā tai ca sikkhāya samādapaiti, yai cañ‍ñai bhikkhū sikkhākāmā taisañ‍ca vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇati bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ kālaina, aivarūpassāhaṃ, kassapa, navassa bhikkhunau vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? satthā hissa vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇatīti añ‍ñai naṃ bhikkhū bhajaiyyuṃ, yai naṃ bhajaiyyuṃ tyāssa  diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ, yyāssa diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyuṃ taisaṃ taṃ assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyāti| tasmāhaṃ, kassapa, aivarūpassa navassa bhikkhunau vaṇṇaṃ bhaṇāmī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

samaṇavaggau navamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

samaṇau gadrabhau khaittaṃ, vaj‍jiputtau ca saikkhakaṃ|

tayau ca sikkhanā vuttā, dvai sikkhā saṅkavāya cāti||


(10) 5. lauṇakapal‍lavaggau

1. ac‍cāyikasuttaṃ

93. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, kassakassa gahapatissa ac‍cāyikāni karaṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, kassakau gahapati sīghaṃ sīghaṃ khaittaṃ sukaṭṭhaṃ karauti sumatikataṃ| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ khaittaṃ sukaṭṭhaṃ karitvā  sumatikataṃ sīghaṃ sīghaṃ bījāni patiṭṭhāpaiti| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ bījāni patiṭṭhāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ udakaṃ abhinaitipi  apanaitipi| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi kassakassa gahapatissa ac‍cāyikāni karaṇīyāni| tassa khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, kassakassa gahapatissa natthi sā iddhi vā ānubhāvau vā – ‘aj‍jaiva mai dhañ‍ñāni jāyantu, svaiva gabbhīni hauntu, uttarasvaiva pac‍cantū’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, hauti sau samayau yaṃ tassa kassakassa gahapatissa tāni dhañ‍ñāni utupariṇāmīni jāyantipi gabbhīnipi haunti pac‍cantipi|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇimāni bhikkhussa ac‍cāyikāni karaṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi? adhisīlasikkhāsamādānaṃ, adhicittasikkhāsamādānaṃ, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi bhikkhussa ac‍cāyikāni karaṇīyāni| tassa khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau natthi sā iddhi vā anubhāvau vā – ‘aj‍jaiva mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍catu svai vā uttarasvai vā’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, hauti sau samayau yaṃ tassa bhikkhunau adhisīlampi sikkhatau adhicittampi sikkhatau adhipañ‍ñampi sikkhatau anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘tibbau nau chandau bhavissati adhisīlasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau chandau bhavissati adhicittasikkhāsamādānai, tibbau chandau bhavissati adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāsamādānai’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pavivaikasuttaṃ

94. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā pavivaikāni pañ‍ñāpainti| katamāni tīṇi? cīvarapavivaikaṃ, piṇḍapātapavivaikaṃ, saināsanapavivaikaṃ|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā cīvarapavivaikasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpainti, sāṇānipi dhārainti, masāṇānipi dhārainti, chavadussānipi dhārainti, paṃsukūlānipi dhārainti, tirīṭānipi dhārainti, ajinampi dhārainti, ajinakkhipampi dhārainti, kusacīrampi dhārainti, vākacīrampi dhārainti, phalakacīrampi dhārainti, kaisakambalampi dhārainti, vālakambalampi dhārainti, ulūkapakkhikampi  dhārainti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā cīvarapavivaikasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpainti|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā piṇḍapātapavivaikasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpainti| sākabhakkhāpi haunti, sāmākabhakkhāpi haunti, nīvārabhakkhāpi haunti, daddulabhakkhāpi haunti, haṭabhakkhāpi haunti, kaṇabhakkhāpi haunti, ācāmabhakkhāpi haunti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhāpi haunti, tiṇabhakkhāpi haunti, gaumayabhakkhāpi haunti, vanamūlaphalāhārā yāpainti pavattaphalabhaujī| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā piṇḍapātapavivaikasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpainti|

‘‘tatridaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā saināsanapavivaikasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpainti arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ susānaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ bhusāgāraṃ susānaṃ (ka॰) vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ bhusāgāraṃ suñ‍ñāgāraṃ (ka॰)| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā saināsanapavivaikasmiṃ pañ‍ñāpainti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi  añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā pavivaikāni pañ‍ñāpainti|

‘‘tīṇi khau panimāni, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai bhikkhunau pavivaikāni| katamāni tīṇi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā ca hauti, dussīlyañ‍cassa pahīnaṃ hauti, taina ca vivittau hauti; sammādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, micchādiṭṭhi cassa pahīnā hauti, tāya ca vivittau hauti; khīṇāsavau ca hauti, āsavā cassa pahīnā haunti, taihi ca vivittau hauti| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, dussīlyañ‍cassa pahīnaṃ hauti, taina ca vivittau hauti; sammādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, micchādiṭṭhi cassa pahīnā hauti, tāya ca vivittau hauti; khīṇāsavau ca hauti, āsavā cassa pahīnā haunti, taihi ca vivittau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu aggappattau sārappattau suddhau sārai patiṭṭhitau’’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kassakassa gahapatissa sampan‍naṃ sālikkhaittaṃ| tamainaṃ kassakau gahapati sīghaṃ sīghaṃ sīghasīghaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) lavāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ lavāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ saṅgharāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ saṅgharāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ  ubbahāpaiyya  ubbāhāpaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ ubbahāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ puñ‍jaṃ kārāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ puñ‍jaṃ kārāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ maddāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ maddāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ palālāni uddharāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ palālāni uddharāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ bhusikaṃ uddharāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ bhusikaṃ uddharāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ aupunāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ aupunāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ atiharāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ atiharāpaitvā sīghaṃ  sīghaṃ kauṭṭāpaiyya| sīghaṃ sīghaṃ kauṭṭāpaitvā sīghaṃ sīghaṃ thusāni uddharāpaiyya| aivamassu aivassu (ka॰) tāni, bhikkhavai, kassakassa gahapatissa dhañ‍ñāni aggappattāni sārappattāni suddhāni sārai patiṭṭhitāni|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yatau bhikkhu sīlavā ca hauti, dussīlyañ‍cassa pahīnaṃ hauti, taina ca vivittau hauti; sammādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, micchādiṭṭhi cassa pahīnā hauti, tāya ca vivittau hauti; khīṇāsavau ca hauti, āsavā cassa pahīnā haunti, taihi ca vivittau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu aggappattau sārappattau suddhau sārai patiṭṭhitau’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. saradasuttaṃ

95. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ādic‍cau nabhaṃ abbhussak‍kamānau abbhussuk‍kamānau (sī॰ pī॰) sabbaṃ ākāsagataṃ tamagataṃ abhivihac‍ca bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucati ca|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yatau ariyasāvakassa virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ uppaj‍jati udapādi (sabbattha), saha  dassanuppādā, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa tīṇi saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti – sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsau|

‘‘athāparaṃ dvīhi dhammaihi niyyāti abhijjhāya ca byāpādaina ca| sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tasmiṃ cai, bhikkhavai, samayai ariyasāvakau kālaṃ karaiyya, natthi taṃ tassa (ka॰) saṃyaujanaṃ yaina  saṃyaujanaina saṃyuttau ariyasāvakau puna imaṃ punayimaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) laukaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. parisāsuttaṃ

96. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, parisā| katamā tissau? aggavatī parisā, vaggā parisā, samaggā parisā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, aggavatī parisā? idha, bhikkhavai, yassaṃ parisāyaṃ thairā bhikkhū na bāhulikā haunti na sāthalikā, auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā, vīriyaṃ ārabhanti appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya, taisaṃ pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati| sāpi hauti na bāhulikā na sāthalikā auk‍kamanai nikkhittadhurā pavivaikai pubbaṅgamā, vīriyaṃ ārabhati appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, aggavatī parisā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, vaggā parisā? idha, bhikkhavai, yassaṃ parisāyaṃ bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vaggā parisā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, samaggā parisā? idha, bhikkhavai, yassaṃ parisāyaṃ bhikkhū samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharanti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, samaggā parisā|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhū samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharanti, bahuṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū tasmiṃ samayai puñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| brahmaṃ, bhikkhavai, vihāraṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhū viharanti, yadidaṃ muditāya caitauvimuttiyā| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ  samādhiyati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uparipabbatai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai taṃ udakaṃ yathānin‍naṃ pavattamānaṃ pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūraiti, pabbatakandarapadarasākhā paripūrā kusaubbhai kussumbhai (sī॰ pī॰), kusumbhai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) paripūrainti, kusaubbhā paripūrā mahāsaubbhai  paripūrainti, mahāsaubbhā paripūrā kun‍nadiyau paripūrainti, kun‍nadiyau paripūrā mahānadiyau paripūrainti, mahānadiyau paripūrā samuddaṃ samuddasāgarai (ka॰) paripūrainti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhū samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi  sampassantā viharanti, bahuṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū tasmiṃ samayai puñ‍ñaṃ pasavanti| brahmaṃ, bhikkhavai, vihāraṃ tasmiṃ samayai bhikkhū viharanti, yadidaṃ muditāya caitauvimuttiyā| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau parisā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamaājānīyasuttaṃ

97. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau bhaddau (ka॰) assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhyaṃ saṅkhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) gacchati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ  laukassa| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘ayaṃ  dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaājānīyasuttaṃ

98. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhayaṃ gacchati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau  hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī  anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyaājānīyasuttaṃ

99. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhayaṃ gacchati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau rañ‍ñau bhadrau assājānīyau rājārahau  hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca hauti balasampan‍nau ca javasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇṇasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu balasampan‍nau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu javasampan‍nau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. pautthakasuttaṃ

100. ‘‘navaupi, bhikkhavai, pautthakau dubbaṇṇau ca hauti dukkhasamphassau ca appagghau ca; majjhimaupi, bhikkhavai, pautthakau dubbaṇṇau ca hauti dukkhasamphassau ca appagghau ca; jiṇṇaupi, bhikkhavai, pautthakau dubbaṇṇau ca hauti dukkhasamphassau ca appagghau ca| jiṇṇampi, bhikkhavai, pautthakaṃ ukkhaliparimaj‍janaṃ vā karaunti saṅkārakūṭai  vā naṃ taṃ (sī॰), ṭhānai (ka॰) chaḍḍainti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, navau caipi bhikkhu hauti dussīlau pāpadhammau| idamassa dubbaṇṇatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi  sau, bhikkhavai, pautthakau dubbaṇṇau tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| yai khau panassa saivanti bhajanti payirupāsanti diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, taisaṃ taṃ hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| idamassa dukkhasamphassatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, pautthakau dukkhasamphassau tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| yaisaṃ khau pana sau yaisaṃ khau pana (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), yaisaṃ sau (ka॰) pu॰ pa॰ 116 passitabbaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, taisaṃ taṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ| idamassa appagghatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, pautthakau appagghau tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| majjhimau caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti…pai॰… thairau caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti dussīlau pāpadhammau, idamassa dubbaṇṇatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, pautthakau dubbaṇṇau tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| yai khau panassa saivanti bhajanti payirupāsanti diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, taisaṃ taṃ hauti dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| idamassa dukkhasamphassatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, pautthakau dukkhasamphassau tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| yaisaṃ khau pana sau paṭiggaṇhāti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, taisaṃ taṃ na mahapphalaṃ hauti na mahānisaṃsaṃ| idamassa appagghatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, pautthakau appagghau  tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘aivarūpau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, thairau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai bhaṇati| tamainaṃ bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃ nu khau tuyhaṃ bālassa abyattassa bhaṇitaina, tvampi nāma bhaṇitabbaṃ mañ‍ñasī’ti! sau kupitau anattamanau tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ nicchāraiti  yathārūpāya vācāya saṅghau taṃ ukkhipati, saṅkārakūṭaiva naṃ pautthakaṃ|

‘‘navampi, bhikkhavai, kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ vaṇṇavantañ‍caiva hauti sukhasamphassañ‍ca mahagghañ‍ca; majjhimampi, bhikkhavai, kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ vaṇṇavantañ‍caiva hauti sukhasamphassañ‍ca mahagghañ‍ca; jiṇṇampi, bhikkhavai, kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ vaṇṇavantañ‍caiva hauti sukhasamphassañ‍ca mahagghañ‍ca| jiṇṇampi, bhikkhavai, kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ ratanapalivaiṭhanaṃ vā karauti gandhakaraṇḍakai vā naṃ pakkhipanti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, navau caipi bhikkhu hauti sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau, idamassa suvaṇṇatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi taṃ, bhikkhavai, kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ vaṇṇavantaṃ tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| yai khau panassa saivanti bhajanti payirupāsanti diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍janti, taisaṃ taṃ hauti dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| idamassa sukhasamphassatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi taṃ, bhikkhavai, kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ sukhasamphassaṃ tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| yaisaṃ khau pana sau paṭiggaṇhāti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, taisaṃ taṃ mahapphalaṃ hauti mahānisaṃsaṃ| idamassa mahagghatāya vadāmi| saiyyathāpi taṃ, bhikkhavai, kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ mahagghaṃ tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi| majjhimau  caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti…pai॰… thairau caipi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu hauti…pai॰… puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘aivarūpau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, thairau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai bhaṇati| tamainaṃ bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘appasaddā āyasmantau hautha, thairau bhikkhu dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca bhaṇatī’ti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kāsikavatthūpamā bhavissāma, na pautthakūpamā’ti kāsikaṃ vatthaṃ tathūpamāhaṃ bhavissāmi, na pautthakūpamāhanti (ka॰)| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabba’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. lauṇakapal‍lasuttaṃ

101. ‘‘yau yau khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yau ca khau (ka॰), bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yathā yathāyaṃ purisau kammaṃ karauti tathā tathā taṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyatī’ti, aivaṃ santaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyavāsau na hauti, aukāsau na pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yathā yathā vaidanīyaṃ ayaṃ purisau kammaṃ karauti tathā tathāssa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidiyatī’ti, aivaṃ santaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyavāsau hauti, aukāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhassa  antakiriyāya| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nā’ṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti? idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhāvitakāyau hauti abhāvitasīlau abhāvitacittau abhāvitapañ‍ñau parittau appātumau appadukkhavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nā’ṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau bhāvitakāyau  hauti bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau aparittau mahattau mahattā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) appamāṇavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau lauṇakapal‍laṃ lauṇaphalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) parittai udakamal‍lakai udakakapal‍lakai (ka॰) pakkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu taṃ parittaṃ udakaṃ udakamal‍lakai udakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) amunā lauṇakapal‍laina lauṇaṃ assa apaiyya’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘aduñhi, bhantai, parittaṃ udakakapal‍lakai udakaṃ, taṃ amunā lauṇakapal‍laina lauṇaṃ assa apaiyya’’nti| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau  lauṇakapal‍lakaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā pakkhipaiyya| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sā gaṅgā nadī amunā lauṇakapal‍laina lauṇaṃ assa apaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘asu hi, bhantai, gaṅgāya nadiyā mahā udakakkhandhau sau amunā lauṇakapal‍laina lauṇau na assa apaiyyau’’ti lauṇaṃ naivassa apaiyyanti (sī॰), na lauṇau assa apaiyyauti (pī॰)|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cassa puggalassa appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhāvitakāyau hauti abhāvitasīlau abhāvitacittau abhāvitapañ‍ñau parittau appātumau appadukkhavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau bhāvitakāyau hauti bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau aparittau mahattau appamāṇavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau aḍḍhakahāpaṇainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇasatainapi  bandhanaṃ nigacchati| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau aḍḍhakahāpaṇainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇainapi na  bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇasatainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati|

‘‘kathaṃrūpau, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhakahāpaṇainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇasatainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau daliddau hauti appassakau appabhaugau| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhakahāpaṇainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇasatainapi bandhanaṃ nigacchati|

‘‘kathaṃrūpau, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhakahāpaṇainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇasatainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau aḍḍhau hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau| aivarūpau, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhakahāpaṇainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati, kahāpaṇasatainapi na bandhanaṃ nigacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cassa puggalassa appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ| tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhāvitakāyau hauti abhāvitasīlau abhāvitacittau abhāvitapañ‍ñau parittau appātumau appadukkhavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau bhāvitakāyau hauti bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau aparittau mahattau appamāṇavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau bhāvitakāyau hauti bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau aparittau mahattau appamāṇavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, aurabbhikau vā urabbhaghātakau vā  appaikac‍caṃ urabbhaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyamānaṃ pahauti hantuṃ vā bandhituṃ vā jāpaituṃ vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā kātuṃ, appaikac‍caṃ urabbhaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyamānaṃ nappahauti hantuṃ vā bandhituṃ vā jāpaituṃ vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā kātuṃ|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, aurabbhikau vā urabbhaghātakau vā urabbhaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyamānaṃ pahauti hantuṃ vā bandhituṃ vā jāpaituṃ vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā kātuṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau daliddau hauti appassakau appabhaugau| aivarūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, aurabbhikau vā urabbhaghātakau vā urabbhaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyamānaṃ pahauti hantuṃ vā bandhituṃ vā jāpaituṃ vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā kātuṃ|

‘‘kathaṃrūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, aurabbhikau vā urabbhaghātakau vā urabbhaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyamānaṃ nappahauti hantuṃ vā bandhituṃ vā jāpaituṃ vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā kātuṃ| idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau aḍḍhau hauti mahaddhanau mahābhaugau rājā vā rājamahāmattau vā| aivarūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, aurabbhikau vā urabbhaghātakau vā urabbhaṃ adin‍naṃ ādiyamānaṃ nappahauti hantuṃ vā bandhituṃ vā jāpaituṃ vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā kātuṃ| añ‍ñadatthu pañ‍jalikauva pañ‍jalikau (ka॰) naṃ paraṃ (ka॰) yācati – ‘daihi mai, mārisa, urabbhaṃ vā urabbhadhanaṃ vā’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cassa puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhāvitakāyau hauti abhāvitasīlau abhāvitacittau abhāvitapañ‍ñau parittau appātumau appadukkhavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa  appamattakampi pāpakammaṃ kataṃ tamainaṃ nirayaṃ upanaiti|

‘‘kathaṃrūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau bhāvitakāyau hauti bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau aparittau mahattau appamāṇavihārī| aivarūpassa, bhikkhavai, puggalassa tādisaṃyaiva appamattakaṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hauti, nāṇupi khāyati, kiṃ bahudaiva|

‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yathā yathāyaṃ purisau kammaṃ karauti tathā tathā taṃ paṭisaṃvaidaitī’ti, aivaṃ santaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyavāsau na hauti, aukāsau na pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘yathā yathā vaidanīyaṃ ayaṃ purisau kammaṃ karauti tathā tathā tassa vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaitī’ti, aivaṃ santaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyavāsau hauti, aukāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. paṃsudhauvakasuttaṃ

102. ‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, jātarūpassa auḷārikā upak‍kilaisā paṃsuvālukā  paṃsuvālikā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) sakkharakaṭhalā| tamainaṃ paṃsudhauvakau vā paṃsudhauvakantaivāsī vā dauṇiyaṃ ākiritvā dhauvati sandhauvati niddhauvati| tasmiṃ pahīnai tasmiṃ byantīkatai santi jātarūpassa majjhimasahagatā upak‍kilaisā sukhumasakkharā thūlavālukā thūlavālikā (sī॰ pī॰), thul‍lavālikā (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tamainaṃ paṃsudhauvakau vā paṃsudhauvakantaivāsī vā dhauvati sandhauvati niddhauvati| tasmiṃ pahīnai tasmiṃ byantīkatai santi jātarūpassa sukhumasahagatā upak‍kilaisā sukhumavālukā kāḷajal‍likā| tamainaṃ paṃsudhauvakau vā paṃsudhauvakantaivāsī vā dhauvati sandhauvati niddhauvati| tasmiṃ pahīnai tasmiṃ byantīkatai athāparaṃ suvaṇṇasikatāvasissanti suvaṇṇajātarūpakāvasissanti (ka॰)| tamainaṃ suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā jātarūpaṃ mūsāyaṃ pakkhipitvā dhamati sandhamati niddhamati| taṃ hauti jātarūpaṃ dhantaṃ sandhantaṃ adhantaṃ asandhantaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) niddhantaṃ aniddhantakasāvaṃ aniddhantaṃ anihitaṃ anin‍nītakasāvaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), na caiva mudu hauti na ca kammaniyaṃ, na ca pabhassaraṃ pabhaṅgu ca, na ca sammā upaiti kammāya| hauti sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ dhamati sandhamati niddhamati| taṃ hauti jātarūpaṃ dhantaṃ sandhantaṃ niddhantaṃ niddhantakasāvaṃ nihitaṃ nin‍nītakasāvaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), mudu ca hauti kammaniyañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca, na ca pabhaṅgu, sammā  upaiti kammāya| yassā yassā ca pilandhanavikatiyā ākaṅkhati – yadi paṭṭikāya muddikāya (a॰ ni॰ 5.23, yadi kuṇḍalāya, yadi gīvaiyyakai gīvaiyyakaina (ka॰), gīvaiyyakāya (?), yadi suvaṇṇamālāya – tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhauti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, santi adhicittamanuyuttassa bhikkhunau auḷārikā upak‍kilaisā kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ, tamainaṃ sacaitasau bhikkhu dabbajātikau pajahati vinaudaiti  byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| tasmiṃ pahīnai tasmiṃ byantīkatai santi adhicittamanuyuttassa bhikkhunau majjhimasahagatā upak‍kilaisā kāmavitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau, tamainaṃ sacaitasau bhikkhu dabbajātikau pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| tasmiṃ  pahīnai tasmiṃ byantīkatai santi adhicittamanuyuttassa bhikkhunau sukhumasahagatā upak‍kilaisā ñātivitak‍kau janapadavitak‍kau anavañ‍ñattipaṭisaṃyuttau vitak‍kau, tamainaṃ sacaitasau bhikkhu dabbajātikau pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| tasmiṃ pahīnai tasmiṃ byantīkatai athāparaṃ dhammavitak‍kāvasissati dhammavitak‍kauvasissati (ka॰)| sau hauti samādhi na caiva santau na ca paṇītau nappaṭippassaddhaladdhau na aikaudibhāvādhigatau sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritavatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sasaṅkhāraniggayhavārivāvatau (ka॰), sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāriyādhigatau (?) a॰ ni॰ 9.37; dī॰ ni॰ 3.355 hauti| sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃyaiva santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti aikaudibhāvaṃ gacchati (sī॰), aikaudibhāvau hauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), aikaudihauti (pī॰) samādhiyati| sau hauti samādhi santau paṇītau paṭippassaddhiladdhau aikaudibhāvādhigatau na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau| yassa yassa ca abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikiriyāya tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyaṃ – aikaupi hutvā bahudhā assaṃ, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau assaṃ; āvibhāvaṃ, tiraubhāvaṃ; tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi ākāsai; pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi udakai; udakaipi abhij‍jamānai abhij‍jamānau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) gacchaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ; ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau; imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasaiyyaṃ parimaj‍jaiyyaṃ; yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai  suṇaiyyaṃ dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai cā’ti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānaiyyaṃ – sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ sadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ vītadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ samauhaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ vītamauhaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ saṃkhittaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ vikkhittaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ mahaggataṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ amahaggataṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ sauttaraṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ anuttaraṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ samāhitaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ asamāhitaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ; vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati  – ‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau  aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti, iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passaiyyaṃ cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyyaṃ – imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā  manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā; imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nāti, iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passaiyyaṃ cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. nimittasuttaṃ

103. ‘‘adhicittamanuyuttaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tīṇi nimittāni kālaina kālaṃ manasi kātabbāni – kālaina kālaṃ samādhinimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ, kālaina kālaṃ paggahanimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ, kālaina kālaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ samādhinimittaṃyaiva manasi karaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ kausaj‍jāya saṃvattaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ paggahanimittaṃyaiva manasi karaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ uddhac‍cāya saṃvattaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃyaiva manasi karaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ na sammā samādhiyaiyya āsavānaṃ khayāya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ samādhinimittaṃ manasi karauti, kālaina kālaṃ paggahanimittaṃ manasi karauti, kālaina kālaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃ manasi karauti, taṃ hauti cittaṃ muduñ‍ca kammaniyañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca, na ca  pabhaṅgu, sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā uk‍kaṃ bandhaiyya bandhati… ālimpati (visuddhi॰ 1.181 taṃṭīkāyaṃ ca) ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.76; ma॰ ni॰ 3.360 taṃaṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu ca passitabbaṃ, uk‍kaṃ  bandhitvā uk‍kāmukhaṃ ālimpaiyya, uk‍kāmukhaṃ ālimpaitvā saṇḍāsaina  jātarūpaṃ gahaitvā uk‍kāmukhai pakkhipaiyya pakkhipati (visuddhi॰ 1.181), uk‍kāmukhai pakkhipitvā kālaina kālaṃ abhidhamati, kālaina kālaṃ udakaina paripphausaiti, kālaina kālaṃ ajjhupaikkhati| sacai, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ aikantaṃ abhidhamaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ jātarūpaṃ ḍahaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ aikantaṃ udakaina paripphausaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ jātarūpaṃ nibbāpaiyya nibbāyaiyya (sī॰)| sacai, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ aikantaṃ ajjhupaikkhaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ jātarūpaṃ na sammā paripākaṃ gacchaiyya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ kālaina kālaṃ abhidhamati, kālaina kālaṃ udakaina paripphausaiti, kālaina kālaṃ ajjhupaikkhati, taṃ hauti jātarūpaṃ muduñ‍ca kammaniyañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca, na ca pabhaṅgu, sammā upaiti kammāya| yassā yassā ca pilandhanavikatiyā ākaṅkhati – yadi paṭṭikāya, yadi kuṇḍalāya, yadi gīvaiyyakai, yadi suvaṇṇamālāya – tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhauti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttaina bhikkhunā tīṇi nimittāni kālaina kālaṃ manasi kātabbāni – kālaina kālaṃ samādhinimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ, kālaina kālaṃ paggahanimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ, kālaina kālaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau  bhikkhu aikantaṃ samādhinimittaṃyaiva manasi karaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ kausaj‍jāya saṃvattaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ paggahanimittaṃyaiva manasi karaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ uddhac‍cāya saṃvattaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃyaiva manasi karaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ na sammā samādhiyaiyya āsavānaṃ khayāya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ samādhinimittaṃ manasi karauti, kālaina kālaṃ paggahanimittaṃ manasi karauti, kālaina kālaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃ manasi karauti, taṃ hauti cittaṃ muduñ‍ca kammaniyañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca, na ca pabhaṅgu, sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya| yassa yassa ca abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyaṃ…pai॰… (cha abhiñ‍ñā vitthāraitabbā)  āsavānaṃ khayā…pai॰… sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

lauṇakapal‍lavaggau lauṇaphalavaggau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ac‍cāyikaṃ pavivaikaṃ, saradau parisā tayau|

ājānīyā pautthakau ca, lauṇaṃ dhauvati nimittānīti||

dutiyau paṇṇāsakau samattau|



3. tatiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(11) 1. sambaudhavaggau

1. pubbaivasambaudhasuttaṃ

104. ‘‘pubbaiva  mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau laukai assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃ khau laukaṃ laukai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ laukai assādau| yaṃ laukau laukai (pī॰ ka॰) anic‍cau dukkhau vipariṇāmadhammau, ayaṃ laukai ādīnavau| yau laukai chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ laukai nissaraṇa’nti laukanissaraṇaṃ (aṭṭha॰) ‘‘laukai nissaraṇa’’nti padaina saṃsanditabbaṃ| yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ laukassa assādañ‍ca assādatau  ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti abhisambuddhau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khvāhaṃ khau ahaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), khauhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, aivaṃ laukassa assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭhamaassādasuttaṃ

105. ‘‘laukassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau laukai assādau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatakau laukai assādau, pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| laukassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau laukai ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatakau laukai ādīnavau, pañ‍ñāya  mai sau sudiṭṭhau| laukassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ laukai nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatakaṃ laukai nissaraṇaṃ, pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ| yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukassa assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukassa assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyaassādasuttaṃ

106. ‘‘nau  caidaṃ nau caitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.28 passitabbaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukai assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā laukai sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi laukai assādau, tasmā sattā laukai sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukai ādīnavau  abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā laukai nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi laukai ādīnavau, tasmā sattā laukai nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukai nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā laukamhā laukai (ka॰) nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi laukai nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā laukamhā nissaranti| yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sattā laukassa assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsuṃ nābbhañ‍ñaṃsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.28, naiva tāva, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visaṃyuttā vippamuttā vippayuttā (ka॰) vimariyādīkataina vimariyādikataina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) caitasā vihariṃsu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, sattā laukassa  assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsuṃ, atha, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visaṃyuttā vippamuttā vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharantī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samaṇabrāhmaṇasuttaṃ

107. ‘‘yai  kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā laukassa assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, na mai tai na tai (ka॰), bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā laukassa assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti, tai khau, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti viharissanti (sī॰ pī॰)| catutthaṃ|

5. ruṇṇasuttaṃ

108. ‘‘ruṇṇamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yadidaṃ gītaṃ| ummattakamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yadidaṃ nac‍caṃ| kaumārakamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yadidaṃ ativailaṃ dantavidaṃsakahasitaṃ dantavidaṃsakaṃ hasitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, saitughātau gītai, saitughātau nac‍cai, alaṃ vau dhammappamauditānaṃ sataṃ sitaṃ sitamattāyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. atittisuttaṃ

109. ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭisaivanāya natthi titti| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ? sauppassa, bhikkhavai, paṭisaivanāya natthi titti| surāmairayapānassa, bhikkhavai, paṭisaivanāya  natthi titti| maithunadhammasamāpattiyā, bhikkhavai, paṭisaivanāya natthi titti| imaisaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ paṭisaivanāya natthi tittī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. arakkhitasuttaṃ

110. atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘cittai, gahapati, arakkhitai kāyakammampi arakkhitaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi arakkhitaṃ hauti, manaukammampi arakkhitaṃ hauti| tassa arakkhitakāyakammantassa arakkhitavacīkammantassa arakkhitamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi avassutaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi avassutaṃ hauti, manaukammampi avassutaṃ hauti| tassa avassutakāyakammantassa avassutavacīkammantassa avassutamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi pūtikaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi pūtikaṃ hauti, manaukammampi pūtikaṃ hauti| tassa pūtikāyakammantassa pūtivacīkammantassa pūtimanaukammantassa na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, na bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, kūṭāgārai ducchan‍nai kūṭampi arakkhitaṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi arakkhitā haunti, bhittipi arakkhitā hauti; kūṭampi avassutaṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi avassutā haunti, bhittipi avassutā hauti; kūṭampi pūtikaṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi pūtikā haunti, bhittipi pūtikā hauti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, gahapati, cittai  arakkhitai kāyakammampi arakkhitaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi arakkhitaṃ hauti, manaukammampi arakkhitaṃ hauti| tassa arakkhitakāyakammantassa arakkhitavacīkammantassa arakkhitamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi avassutaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi avassutaṃ hauti, manaukammampi avassutaṃ hauti| tassa avassutakāyakammantassa avassutavacīkammantassa avassutamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi pūtikaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi pūtikaṃ hauti, manaukammampi pūtikaṃ hauti| tassa pūtikāyakammantassa pūtivacīkammantassa pūtimanaukammantassa na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, na bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyā|

‘‘cittai, gahapati, rakkhitai kāyakammampi rakkhitaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi rakkhitaṃ hauti, manaukammampi rakkhitaṃ hauti| tassa rakkhitakāyakammantassa rakkhitavacīkammantassa rakkhitamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi anavassutaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi anavassutaṃ hauti, manaukammampi anavassutaṃ  hauti| tassa anavassutakāyakammantassa anavassutavacīkammantassa anavassutamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi apūtikaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi apūtikaṃ hauti, manaukammampi apūtikaṃ hauti| tassa apūtikāyakammantassa apūtivacīkammantassa apūtimanaukammantassa bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gahapati, kūṭāgārai succhan‍nai kūṭampi rakkhitaṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi rakkhitā haunti, bhittipi rakkhitā hauti; kūṭampi anavassutaṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi anavassutā haunti, bhittipi anavassutā hauti; kūṭampi apūtikaṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi apūtikā haunti, bhittipi apūtikā hauti|

aivamaivaṃ khau, gahapati, cittai rakkhitai kāyakammampi rakkhitaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi rakkhitaṃ hauti, manaukammampi rakkhitaṃ hauti| tassa rakkhitakāyakammantassa rakkhitavacīkammantassa rakkhitamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi anavassutaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi anavassutaṃ hauti, manaukammampi anavassutaṃ hauti| tassa anavassutakāyakammantassa anavassutavacīkammantassa anavassutamanaukammantassa kāyakammampi apūtikaṃ hauti, vacīkammampi apūtikaṃ hauti, manaukammampi apūtikaṃ hauti| tassa apūtikāyakammantassa apūtivacīkammantassa apūtimanaukammantassa bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. byāpan‍nasuttaṃ

111. aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘cittai, gahapati, byāpan‍nai kāyakammampi byāpan‍naṃ hauti, vacīkammampi byāpan‍naṃ hauti, manaukammampi byāpan‍naṃ hauti| tassa byāpan‍nakāyakammantassa byāpan‍navacīkammantassa byāpan‍namanaukammantassa na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, na bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyā| saiyyathāpi, gahapati, kūṭāgārai ducchan‍nai kūṭampi byāpan‍naṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi byāpan‍nā haunti, bhittipi byāpan‍nā  hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, gahapati, cittai byāpan‍nai kāyakammampi byāpan‍naṃ hauti, vacīkammampi byāpan‍naṃ hauti, manaukammampi byāpan‍naṃ hauti| tassa byāpan‍nakāyakammantassa byāpan‍navacīkammantassa byāpan‍namanaukammantassa na bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, na bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyā|

‘‘cittai, gahapati, abyāpan‍nai kāyakammampi abyāpan‍naṃ hauti, vacīkammampi abyāpan‍naṃ hauti, manaukammampi abyāpan‍naṃ hauti| tassa abyāpan‍nakāyakammantassa abyāpan‍navacīkammantassa abyāpan‍namanaukammantassa bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyā| saiyyathāpi, gahapati, kūṭāgārai succhan‍nai kūṭampi abyāpan‍naṃ hauti, gaupānasiyaupi  abyāpan‍nā haunti, bhittipi abyāpan‍nā hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, gahapati, cittai abyāpan‍nai kāyakammampi abyāpan‍naṃ hauti, vacīkammampi abyāpan‍naṃ hauti, manaukammampi abyāpan‍naṃ hauti| tassa abyāpan‍nakāyakammantassa…pai॰… abyāpan‍namanaukammantassa bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, bhaddikā  kālaṅkiriyā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭhamanidānasuttaṃ

112. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? laubhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, dausau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, mauhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, laubhapakataṃ kammaṃ laubhajaṃ laubhanidānaṃ laubhasamudayaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ akusalaṃ taṃ kammaṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ taṃ kammaṃ dukkhavipākaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ kammasamudayāya saṃvattati, na taṃ kammaṃ kammaniraudhāya saṃvattati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dausapakataṃ kammaṃ dausajaṃ dausanidānaṃ dausasamudayaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ akusalaṃ taṃ kammaṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ taṃ kammaṃ dukkhavipākaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ kammasamudayāya saṃvattati, na taṃ kammaṃ kammaniraudhāya saṃvattati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, mauhapakataṃ kammaṃ mauhajaṃ mauhanidānaṃ mauhasamudayaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ akusalaṃ taṃ kammaṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ taṃ kammaṃ dukkhavipākaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ kammasamudayāya saṃvattati, na taṃ kammaṃ kammaniraudhāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? alaubhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, adausau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya, amauhau nidānaṃ kammānaṃ samudayāya| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, alaubhapakataṃ kammaṃ alaubhajaṃ alaubhanidānaṃ alaubhasamudayaṃ, taṃ  kammaṃ kusalaṃ taṃ  kammaṃ anavaj‍jaṃ taṃ kammaṃ sukhavipākaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ kammaniraudhāya saṃvattati, na taṃ kammaṃ kammasamudayāya saṃvattati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, adausapakataṃ kammaṃ adausajaṃ adausanidānaṃ adausasamudayaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ kusalaṃ taṃ kammaṃ anavaj‍jaṃ taṃ kammaṃ sukhavipākaṃ, taṃ  kammaṃ kammaniraudhāya saṃvattati, na taṃ kammaṃ kammasamudayāya saṃvattati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, amauhapakataṃ kammaṃ amauhajaṃ amauhanidānaṃ amauhasamudayaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ kusalaṃ taṃ kammaṃ anavaj‍jaṃ taṃ kammaṃ sukhavipākaṃ, taṃ kammaṃ kammaniraudhāya saṃvattati, na taṃ kammaṃ kammasamudayāya saṃvattati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāyā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyanidānasuttaṃ

113. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? atītai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati; anāgatai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati; pac‍cuppan‍nai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atītai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati? atītai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti| tassa atītai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha caitasā anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau chandau jāyati| chandajātau taihi dhammaihi saṃyuttau hauti| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanaṃ vadāmi yau caitasau sārāgau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atītai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anāgatai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai  dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati? anāgatai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti| tassa anāgatai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha caitasā anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau chandau jāyati| chandajātau taihi dhammaihi saṃyuttau hauti| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanaṃ vadāmi yau caitasau sārāgau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, anāgatai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍nai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati? pac‍cuppan‍nai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha caitasā anuvitak‍kaiti  anuvicāraiti| tassa pac‍cuppan‍nai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha caitasā anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau chandau jāyati| chandajātau taihi dhammaihi saṃyuttau hauti| aitamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujanaṃ vadāmi yau caitasau  sārāgau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍nai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau jāyati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāya| katamāni tīṇi? atītai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati; anāgatai bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati; pac‍cuppan‍nai, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atītai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati? atītānaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyānaṃ dhammānaṃ āyatiṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti| āyatiṃ vipākaṃ viditvā tadabhinivattaiti| tadabhinivattaitvā tadabhinivaj‍jaiti, tadabhinivaj‍jaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) caitasā abhinivijjhitvā abhivirājaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pañ‍ñāya ativijjha abhinivijjha (ka॰) passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atītai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anāgatai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati? anāgatānaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyānaṃ dhammānaṃ āyatiṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti| āyatiṃ vipākaṃ viditvā tadabhinivattaiti| tadabhinivattaitvā caitasā abhinivijjhitvā pañ‍ñāya ativijjha passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, anāgatai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍nai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati? pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgaṭṭhāniyānaṃ dhammānaṃ āyatiṃ vipākaṃ pajānāti, āyatiṃ vipākaṃ  viditvā tadabhinivattaiti, tadabhinivattaitvā caitasā abhinivijjhitvā pañ‍ñāya ativijjha passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pac‍cuppan‍nai chandarāgaṭṭhāniyai dhammai ārabbha chandau na jāyati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi nidānāni kammānaṃ samudayāyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sambaudhavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

pubbaiva  duvai assādā, samaṇau ruṇṇapañ‍camaṃ|

atitti dvai ca vuttāni, nidānāni aparai duvaiti||


(12) 2. āpāyikavaggau

1. āpāyikasuttaṃ

114. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, āpāyikā nairayikā idamappahāya| katamai tayau? yau  ca abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau, yau ca suddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carantaṃ amūlakaina abhūtaina (ka॰) abrahmacariyaina anuddhaṃsaiti, yau cāyaṃ aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi kāmaisu dausau’ti, sau tāya kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍jati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau āpāyikā nairayikā idamappahāyā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dul‍labhasuttaṃ

115. ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ? tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, tathāgatappavaiditassa dhammavinayassa daisaitā puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ, katañ‍ñū katavaidī puggalau dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ pātubhāvau dul‍labhau laukasmi’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. appamaiyyasuttaṃ

116. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? suppamaiyyau, duppamaiyyau, appamaiyyau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau suppamaiyyau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau uddhatau hauti un‍naḷau capalau mukharau vikiṇṇavācau muṭṭhassati asampajānau asamāhitau vibbhantacittau pākatindriyau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau suppamaiyyau|
