‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau duppamaiyyau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau anuddhatau hauti anun‍naḷau acapalau amukharau avikiṇṇavācau upaṭṭhitassati sampajānau samāhitau aikaggacittau saṃvutindriyau| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau duppamaiyyau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, puggalau appamaiyyau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ hauti khīṇāsavau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, puggalau appamaiyyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. ānaiñ‍jasuttaṃ

117. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti taṃ nikāmaiti taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati, tatra ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ vīsati kappasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti taṃ  nikāmaiti taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati, tatra ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ cattārīsaṃ kappasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha  puthuj‍janau  yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ  sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau tadassādaiti taṃ nikāmaiti taina ca vittiṃ āpaj‍jati, tatra ṭhitau tadadhimuttau tabbahulavihārī aparihīnau kālaṃ kurumānau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ saṭṭhi kappasahassāni āyuppamāṇaṃ| tattha puthuj‍janau yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā nirayampi gacchati tiracchānayaunimpi gacchati paittivisayampi gacchati| bhagavatau pana sāvakau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā yāvatakaṃ taisaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ khaipaitvā tasmiṃyaiva bhavai parinibbāyati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau, ayaṃ adhippayāsau idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavatau ariyasāvakassa assutavatā puthuj‍janaina, yadidaṃ gatiyā upapattiyā| ‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. vipattisampadāsuttaṃ

118. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vipattiyau| katamā tissau? sīlavipatti, cittavipatti, diṭṭhivipatti| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sīlavipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti, adin‍nādāyī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, musāvādī hauti, pisuṇavācau hauti, pharusavācau hauti, samphappalāpī hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīlavipatti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, cittavipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau abhijjhālu hauti byāpan‍nacittau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, cittavipatti|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhivipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ, kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhivipatti| sīlavipattihaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti; cittavipattihaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti; diṭṭhivipattihaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vipattiyauti|

‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā tissau? sīlasampadā, cittasampadā, diṭṭhisampadā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, cittasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti abyāpan‍nacittau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, cittasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti aviparītadassanau – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi ayaṃ laukau, atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā, atthi pitā, atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā| sīlasampadāhaitu  vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti; cittasampadāhaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ  laukaṃ upapaj‍janti; diṭṭhisampadāhaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ  laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sampadā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. apaṇṇakasuttaṃ

119. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vipattiyau| katamā tissau? sīlavipatti, cittavipatti, diṭṭhivipatti| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sīlavipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti…pai॰… samphappalāpī hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīlavipatti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, cittavipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau abhijjhālu hauti byāpan‍nacittau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, cittavipatti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhivipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhivipatti| sīlavipattihaitu vā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… diṭṭhivipattihaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, apaṇṇakau maṇi uddhaṃ khittau yaina yainaiva patiṭṭhāti suppatiṭṭhitaṃyaiva patiṭṭhāti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sīlavipattihaitu vā sattā…pai॰… upapaj‍janti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vipattiyauti|

‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā tissau? sīlasampadā, cittasampadā, diṭṭhisampadā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, cittasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti abyāpan‍nacittau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, cittasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti aviparītadassanau – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā| sīlasampadāhaitu vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| cittasampadāhaitu vā…pai॰… diṭṭhisampadāhaitu  vā, bhikkhavai, sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, apaṇṇakau maṇi uddhaṃ khittau yaina yainaiva patiṭṭhāti suppatiṭṭhitaṃyaiva patiṭṭhāti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sīlasampadāhaitu vā sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti, cittasampadāhaitu vā sattā…pai॰… diṭṭhisampadāhaitu vā sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sampadā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kammantasuttaṃ

120. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vipattiyau| katamā tissau? kammantavipatti, ājīvavipatti, diṭṭhivipatti| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, kammantavipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti…pai॰… samphappalāpī hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammantavipatti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, ājīvavipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchāājīvau hauti, micchāājīvaina jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) kappaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ājīvavipatti|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhivipatti? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau micchādiṭṭhikau hauti viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhivipatti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vipattiyauti|

‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā tissau? kammantasampadā, ājīvasampadā, diṭṭhisampadā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, kammantasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti…pai॰… samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kammantasampadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, ājīvasampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammāājīvau hauti, sammāājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ājīvasampadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sammādiṭṭhikau hauti aviparītadassanau – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca  laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampadā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sampadā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. paṭhamasaucaiyyasuttaṃ

121. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, saucaiyyāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyasaucaiyyaṃ, vacīsaucaiyyaṃ, manausaucaiyyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kāyasaucaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau  hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāyasaucaiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vacīsaucaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati bhikkhavai, vacīsaucaiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, manausaucaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti abyāpan‍nacittau sammādiṭṭhikau| idaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, manausaucaiyyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi saucaiyyānī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyasaucaiyyasuttaṃ

122. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, saucaiyyāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyasaucaiyyaṃ, vacīsaucaiyyaṃ, manausaucaiyyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kāyasaucaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pāṇātipātā  paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, abrahmacariyā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāyasaucaiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vacīsaucaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vacīsaucaiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, manausaucaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandaṃ – ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandau’ti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandaṃ – ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandau’ti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa  uppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca uppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ byāpādaṃ – ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ byāpādau’ti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ byāpādaṃ – ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ byāpādau’ti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nassa byāpādassa uppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca uppan‍nassa byāpādassa pahānaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca pahīnassa byāpādassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ thinamiddhaṃ – ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ thinamiddha’nti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ thinamiddhaṃ – ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ thinamiddha’nti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nassa thinamiddhassa uppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca uppan‍nassa thinamiddhassa pahānaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca pahīnassa thinamiddhassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ – ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍ca’nti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ  uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ – ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍ca’nti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nassa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca uppan‍nassa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa pahānaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca pahīnassa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ – ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ vicikicchā’ti pajānāti; asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ – ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ  vicikicchā’ti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nāya vicikicchāya uppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti; yathā ca uppan‍nāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca  pajānāti; yathā ca pahīnāya vicikicchāya āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti, tañ‍ca pajānāti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, manausaucaiyyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi saucaiyyānīti|

itivu॰ 66 ‘‘kāyasuciṃ vacīsuciṃ, caitausuciṃ anāsavaṃ|

suciṃ saucaiyyasampan‍naṃ, āhu ninhātapāpaka’’nti|| navamaṃ|

10. maunaiyyasuttaṃ

123. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, maunaiyyāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyamaunaiyyaṃ, vacīmaunaiyyaṃ, manaumaunaiyyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, kāyamaunaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, abrahmacariyā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, kāyamaunaiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vacīmaunaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vacīmaunaiyyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, manaumaunaiyyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, manaumaunaiyyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi maunaiyyānī’’ti|

‘‘kāyamuniṃ vacīmuniṃ, caitaumuniṃ anāsavaṃ|

muniṃ maunaiyyasampan‍naṃ, āhu sabbappahāyina’’nti|| dasamaṃ|

āpāyikavaggau dvādasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

āpāyikau  dul‍labhau appamaiyyaṃ, ānaiñ‍javipattisampadā|

apaṇṇakau ca kammantau, dvai saucaiyyāni maunaiyyanti||


(13) 3. kusināravaggau

1. kusinārasuttaṃ

124. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā kusinārāyaṃ viharati baliharaṇai vanasaṇḍai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| tamainaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā upasaṅkamitvā svātanāya bhattaina nimantaiti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu adhivāsaiti| sau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamati; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdati| tamainaṃ sau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaiti sampavāraiti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘sādhu vata myāyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaiti sampavāraitī’ti| aivampissa hauti – ‘ahau vata māyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā āyatimpi aivarūpaina paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaiyya  sampavāraiyyā’ti! sau taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ gathitau gadhitau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) mucchitau ajjhausan‍nau ajjhāpan‍nau (sī॰ ka॰) ajjhaupan‍nau (ṭīkā) anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| sau tattha kāmavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti, byāpādavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti, vihiṃsāvitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti| aivarūpassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau din‍naṃ na mahapphalanti vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? pamattau hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharati|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā upanissāya viharati| tamainaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā upasaṅkamitvā svātanāya bhattaina nimantaiti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu adhivāsaiti| sau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā  pattacīvaramādāya yaina tassa gahapatissa vā gahapatiputtassa vā nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamati; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdati| tamainaṃ sau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaiti sampavāraiti|

‘‘tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘sādhu vata myāyaṃ gahapati vā  gahapatiputtau vā paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaiti sampavāraitī’ti| aivampissa na hauti – ‘ahau vata māyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā āyatimpi aivarūpaina paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaiyya sampavāraiyyā’ti! sau taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ agathitau amucchitau anajjhausan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| sau tattha naikkhammavitak‍kampi  vitak‍kaiti, abyāpādavitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti, avihiṃsāvitak‍kampi vitak‍kaiti| aivarūpassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau din‍naṃ mahapphalanti  vadāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? appamattau hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viharatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. bhaṇḍanasuttaṃ

125. ‘‘yassaṃ, bhikkhavai, disāyaṃ bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti, manasi kātumpi mai aisā, bhikkhavai, disā na phāsu hauti, pagaiva gantuṃ! niṭṭhamaittha gacchāmi – ‘addhā tai āyasmantau tayau dhammai pajahiṃsu, tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsu bahulīmakaṃsu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| katamai tayau dhammai pajahiṃsu? naikkhammavitak‍kaṃ, abyāpādavitak‍kaṃ, avihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ – imai tayau dhammai pajahiṃsu| katamai tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsu? kāmavitak‍kaṃ, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ – imai tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsu’| yassaṃ, bhikkhavai, disāyaṃ bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti, manasi kātumpi mai aisā, bhikkhavai, disā na phāsu hauti, pagaiva gantuṃ! niṭṭhamaittha gacchāmi – ‘addhā tai āyasmantau imai tayau dhammai pajahiṃsu, imai tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsu’’’|

‘‘yassaṃ pana, bhikkhavai, disāyaṃ bhikkhū samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharanti, gantumpi mai aisā, bhikkhavai, disā phāsu hauti, pagaiva manasi kātuṃ! niṭṭhamaittha gacchāmi – ‘addhā tai āyasmantau tayau dhammai  pajahiṃsu, tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsu| katamai tayau dhammai pajahiṃsu? kāmavitak‍kaṃ, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ – imai tayau dhammai pajahiṃsu| katamai tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsu? naikkhammavitak‍kaṃ, abyāpādavitak‍kaṃ, avihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ – imai tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsu’| yassaṃ, bhikkhavai, disāyaṃ bhikkhū samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharanti, gantumpi mai aisā, bhikkhavai, disā phāsu hauti, pagaiva manasi kātuṃ! niṭṭhamaittha gacchāmi – ‘addhā tai āyasmantau imai tayau dhammai pajahiṃsu, imai tayau dhammai bahulamakaṃsū’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. gautamakacaitiyasuttaṃ

126. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati gautamakai caitiyai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘abhiñ‍ñāyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaimi, nau anabhiñ‍ñāya| sanidānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaimi, nau anidānaṃ| sappāṭihāriyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaimi, nau appāṭihāriyaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya dhammaṃ daisayatau nau anabhiñ‍ñāya, sanidānaṃ dhammaṃ daisayatau nau anidānaṃ, sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisayatau nau appāṭihāriyaṃ, karaṇīyau auvādau, karaṇīyā anusāsanī| alañ‍ca pana vau, bhikkhavai, tuṭṭhiyā, alaṃ attamanatāya, alaṃ saumanassāya – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau saṅghau’’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai sahassī laukadhātu akampitthāti| tatiyaṃ|

4. bharaṇḍukālāmasuttaṃ

127. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau yaina kapilavatthu tadavasari| assausi khau mahānāmau sak‍kau – ‘‘bhagavā kira kapilavatthuṃ  anuppattau’’ti| atha khau mahānāmau  sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘gaccha, mahānāma, kapilavatthusmiṃ, tathārūpaṃ āvasathaṃ jāna yatthaj‍ja mayaṃ aikarattiṃ viharaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavatau paṭissutvā kapilavatthuṃ pavisitvā kaivalakappaṃ kapilavatthuṃ anvāhiṇḍantau āhiṇḍantau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nāddasa kapilavatthusmiṃ tathārūpaṃ āvasathaṃ yatthaj‍ja bhagavā aikarattiṃ viharaiyya|

atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘natthi, bhantai, kapilavatthusmiṃ tathārūpau āvasathau  yatthaj‍ja bhagavā aikarattiṃ viharaiyya| ayaṃ, bhantai, bharaṇḍu kālāmau bhagavatau purāṇasabrahmacārī| tassaj‍ja bhagavā assamai aikarattiṃ viharatū’’ti| ‘‘gaccha, mahānāma, santharaṃ pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina bharaṇḍussa kālāmassa assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā santharaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā udakaṃ ṭhapaitvā pādānaṃ dhauvanāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘santhatau, bhantai, santhārau, udakaṃ ṭhapitaṃ pādānaṃ dhauvanāya| yassadāni, bhantai, bhagavā kālaṃ mañ‍ñatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā yaina bharaṇḍussa kālāmassa assamau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā pādai pakkhālaisi| atha khau mahānāmassa sak‍kassa aitadahausi – ‘‘akālau khau aj‍ja bhagavantaṃ payirupāsituṃ| kilantau  bhagavā| svai dānāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ payirupāsissāmī’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘tayau khaumai, mahānāma, satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? idha, mahānāma, aikac‍cau satthā kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti; na rūpānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti, na vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti| idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau satthā kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti, rūpānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti; na vaidanānaṃ  pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti| idha pana, mahānāma, aikac‍cau satthā kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti, rūpānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti, vaidanānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti| imai khau, mahānāma, tayau satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| ‘imaisaṃ, mahānāma, tiṇṇaṃ satthārānaṃ aikā niṭṭhā udāhu puthu niṭṭhā’’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai bharaṇḍu kālāmau mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aikāti, mahānāma, vadaihī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nānāti, mahānāma, vadaihī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bharaṇḍu kālāmau mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aikāti, mahānāma, vadaihī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nānāti, mahānāma, vadaihī’’ti| tatiyampi khau bharaṇḍu kālāmau mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aikāti, mahānāma, vadaihī’’ti| tatiyampi khau bhagavā mahānāmaṃ sak‍kaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘nānāti, mahānāma, vadaihī’’ti|

atha khau bharaṇḍu kālāmassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mahaisakkhassa vatamhi mahānāmassa sak‍kassa sammukhā samaṇaina gautamaina yāvatatiyaṃ apasāditau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kapilavatthumhā pak‍kamaiyya’’nti| atha khau bharaṇḍu kālāmau kapilavatthumhā pak‍kāmi| yaṃ kapilavatthumhā pak‍kāmi tathā pak‍kantauva ahausi na puna pac‍cāgacchīti| catutthaṃ|

5. hatthakasuttaṃ

128. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau hatthakau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā – ‘‘bhagavatau puratau ṭhassāmī’’ti ausīdatimaiva saṃsīdatimaiva ausīdati caiva saṃsīdati ca (sī॰ pī॰), ausīdati saṃsīdati (syā॰ kaṃ॰), na sak‍kauti saṇṭhātuṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma sappi vā tailaṃ vā vālukāya āsittaṃ ausīdatimaiva saṃsīdatimaiva, na saṇṭhāti; aivamaivaṃ hatthakau daivaputtau – ‘‘bhagavatau puratau ṭhassāmī’’ti ausīdatimaiva saṃsīdatimaiva, na sak‍kauti saṇṭhātuṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā hatthakaṃ daivaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘auḷārikaṃ, hatthaka, attabhāvaṃ abhinimmināhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti, khau hatthakau daivaputtau bhagavatau paṭissutvā auḷārikaṃ attabhāvaṃ abhinimminitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau hatthakaṃ daivaputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yai tai, hatthaka, dhammā pubbai manussabhūtassa pavattinau ahaisuṃ, api nu tai tai dhammā aitarahi pavattinau’’ti? ‘‘yai ca mai, bhantai, dhammā pubbai manussabhūtassa pavattinau ahaisuṃ, tai ca mai dhammā aitarahi pavattinau; yai ca mai, bhantai, dhammā pubbai manussabhūtassa  nappavattinau ahaisuṃ, tai ca mai dhammā aitarahi pavattinau| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, bhagavā aitarahi ākiṇṇau viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi upāsikāhi rājūhi  rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, ākiṇṇau viharāmi daivaputtaihi| dūrataupi, bhantai, daivaputtā āgacchanti hatthakassa daivaputtassa santikai ‘dhammaṃ saussāmā’ti| tiṇṇāhaṃ, bhantai, dhammānaṃ atittau appaṭivānau kālaṅkatau| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ? bhagavatau ahaṃ, bhantai, dassanassa atittau appaṭivānau kālaṅkatau; saddhammasavanassāhaṃ, bhantai, atittau appaṭivānau kālaṅkatau; saṅghassāhaṃ, bhantai, upaṭṭhānassa atittau appaṭivānau kālaṅkatau| imaisaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ atittau appaṭivānau kālaṅkatau’’ti|

‘‘nāhaṃ bhagavatau dassanassa, tittimajjhagā titti tittisambhavaṃ (ka॰) kudācanaṃ|

saṅghassa upaṭṭhānassa, saddhammasavanassa ca||

‘‘adhisīlaṃ sikkhamānau, saddhammasavanai ratau|

tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ atittau, hatthakau avihaṃ gatau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kaṭuviyasuttaṃ

129. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya bārāṇasiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā  khau bhagavā gauyaugapilakkhasmiṃ gauyaugamilakkhasmiṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) piṇḍāya caramānau caramānaṃ (ka॰) añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ rittassādaṃ bāhirassādaṃ muṭṭhassatiṃ  asampajānaṃ asamāhitaṃ vibbhantacittaṃ pākatindriyaṃ| disvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca  –

‘‘mā khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, attānaṃ kaṭuviyamakāsi| taṃ vata bhikkhu kaṭuviyakataṃ attānaṃ āmagandhaina āmagandhai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) avassutaṃ makkhikā nānupatissanti nānvāssavissantīti nānubandhissanti (ka॰), naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatā iminā auvādaina auvaditau saṃvaigamāpādi| atha khau bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya bārāṇasiṃ  piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, gauyaugapilakkhasmiṃ piṇḍāya caramānau añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ rittassādaṃ bāhirassādaṃ muṭṭhassatiṃ asampajānaṃ asamāhitaṃ vibbhantacittaṃ pākatindriyaṃ| disvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucaṃ –

‘‘‘mā khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, attānaṃ kaṭuviyamakāsi| taṃ vata bhikkhu kaṭuviyakataṃ attānaṃ āmagandhaina avassutaṃ makkhikā nānupatissanti nānvāssavissantīti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sau bhikkhu mayā iminā auvādaina auvaditau saṃvaigamāpādī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, kaṭuviyaṃ? kau āmagandhau? kā makkhikā’’ti?

‘‘abhijjhā khau, bhikkhu, kaṭuviyaṃ; byāpādau āmagandhau; pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā makkhikā| taṃ vata, bhikkhu, kaṭuviyakataṃ attānaṃ āmagandhaina avassutaṃ makkhikā nānupatissanti nānvāssavissantīti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘aguttaṃ  cakkhusautasmiṃ, indriyaisu asaṃvutaṃ|

makkhikānupatissanti, saṅkappā rāganissitā||

‘‘kaṭuviyakatau  bhikkhu, āmagandhai avassutau|

ārakā hauti nibbānā, vighātassaiva bhāgavā||

‘‘gāmai vā yadi vārañ‍ñai, aladdhā samathamattanau samamattanau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), sammamattanau (pī॰)|

paraiti caraiti (syā॰ ka॰) bālau dummaidhau, makkhikāhi purakkhatau||

‘‘yai ca sīlaina sampan‍nā, pañ‍ñāyūpasamairatā|

upasantā sukhaṃ sainti, nāsayitvāna makkhikā’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamaanuruddhasuttaṃ

130. atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, dibbaina cakkhunā  visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina yaibhuyyaina passāmi mātugāmaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jamānaṃ| katihi nu khau, bhantai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘tīhi khau, anuruddha, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, anuruddha, mātugāmau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, majjhanhikasamayaṃ issāpariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, sāyanhasamayaṃ kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| imaihi khau, anuruddha, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau mātugāmau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaanuruddhasuttaṃ

131. atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā  sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā anuruddhau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sahassaṃ laukaṃ aulaukaimi| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā apammuṭṭhā (sī॰), apamuṭṭhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| atha ca pana mai nānupādāya na anupādāya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ khau tai, āvusau anuruddha, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sahassaṃ laukaṃ vaulaukaimī’ti, idaṃ tai mānasmiṃ| yampi tai, āvusau anuruddha, aivaṃ hauti – ‘āraddhaṃ khau pana mai vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikagga’nti, idaṃ tai uddhac‍casmiṃ| yampi tai, āvusau anuruddha, aivaṃ hauti – ‘atha ca pana mai nānupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍catī’ti, idaṃ  tai kuk‍kuc‍casmiṃ| sādhu vatāyasmā anuruddhau imai tayau dhammai pahāya, imai tayau dhammai amanasikaritvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharatū’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau aparaina  samayaina imai tayau dhammai pahāya, imai tayau dhammai amanasikaritvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃhari upasaṃhāsi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), upasaṃharati (ka॰)| atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā anuruddhau arahataṃ ahausīti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. paṭicchan‍nasuttaṃ

132. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, paṭicchan‍nāni āvahanti vahanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), nau vivaṭāni| katamāni tīṇi? mātugāmau, bhikkhavai, paṭicchan‍nau āvahati, nau vivaṭau; brāhmaṇānaṃ, bhikkhavai, mantā paṭicchan‍nā āvahanti, nau vivaṭā; micchādiṭṭhi, bhikkhavai, paṭicchan‍nā āvahati, nau vivaṭā| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi paṭicchan‍nāni āvahanti, nau vivaṭāni|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, vivaṭāni viraucanti, nau paṭicchan‍nāni| katamāni tīṇi? candamaṇḍalaṃ, bhikkhavai, vivaṭaṃ viraucati, nau paṭicchan‍naṃ; sūriyamaṇḍalaṃ, bhikkhavai, vivaṭaṃ viraucati, nau paṭicchan‍naṃ; tathāgatappavaiditau dhammavinayau, bhikkhavai, vivaṭau viraucati, nau paṭicchan‍nau| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi vivaṭāni viraucanti, nau paṭicchan‍nānī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. laikhasuttaṃ

133. ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai tayau? pāsāṇalaikhūpamau puggalau, pathavilaikhūpamau puggalau, udakalaikhūpamau  puggalau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pāsāṇalaikhūpamau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau  dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pāsāṇai laikhā na khippaṃ luj‍jati vātaina vā udakaina vā, ciraṭṭhitikā hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pāsāṇalaikhūpamau puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, pathavilaikhūpamau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau na dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pathaviyā laikhā khippaṃ luj‍jati vātaina vā udakaina vā, na ciraṭṭhitikā hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau abhiṇhaṃ kujjhati| sau ca khvassa kaudhau na dīgharattaṃ anusaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pathavilaikhūpamau puggalau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, udakalaikhūpamau puggalau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau puggalau āgāḷhainapi vuc‍camānau pharusainapi  vuc‍camānau amanāpainapi vuc‍camānau sandhiyatimaiva … yaiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰) … caiva (pī॰) saṃsandatimaiva … yaiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰) … caiva (pī॰) sammaudatimaiva … yaiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰) … caiva (pī॰)| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakai laikhā khippaṃyaiva paṭivigacchati, na ciraṭṭhitikā hauti; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau puggalau āgāḷhainapi vuc‍camānau pharusainapi vuc‍camānau amanāpainapi vuc‍camānau sandhiyatimaiva saṃsandatimaiva sammaudatimaiva| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, udakalaikhūpamau puggalau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti pu॰ pa॰ 115| dasamaṃ|

kusināravaggau tairasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kusinārabhaṇḍanā  caiva, gautamabharaṇḍuhatthakau|

kaṭuviyaṃ dvai anuruddhā, paṭicchan‍naṃ laikhaina tai dasāti||


(14) 4. yaudhājīvavaggau

1. yaudhājīvasuttaṃ

134. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau yaudhājīvau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, yaudhājīvau dūrai pātī ca hauti akkhaṇavaidhī ca mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā| imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau yaudhājīvau rājārahau hauti rājabhauggau, rañ‍ñau aṅgantaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamaihi tīhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dūrai pātī ca hauti akkhaṇavaidhī ca mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dūrai pātī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattā vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yā dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ vaidanaṃ  sabbā vaidanā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci sañ‍ñā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattā vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yā dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ sabbā sañ‍ñā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yai kaici saṅkhārā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattā vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā hīnā vā paṇītā vā yai dūrai santikai vā, sabbai saṅkhārai – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dūrai pātī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhaṇavaidhī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu akkhaṇavaidhī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahatau kāyassa padālaitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahantaṃ avij‍jākkhandhaṃ padālaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mahatau kāyassa padālaitā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu  āhunaiyyau hauti…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. parisāsuttaṃ

135. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, parisā| katamā tissau? uk‍kācitavinītā parisā, paṭipucchāvinītā parisā, yāvatāvinītā yāvatajjhāvinītā (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) parisā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau parisā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mittasuttaṃ

136. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mittau saivitabbau| katamaihi tīhi? ( ) (idha bhikkhavai bhikkhu) (pī॰ ka॰) duddadaṃ dadāti, duk‍karaṃ karauti, dukkhamaṃ khamati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau mittau saivitabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. uppādāsuttaṃ

137. ‘‘uppādā vā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ anuppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ, ṭhitāva sā dhātu  dhammaṭṭhitatā dhammaniyāmatā| sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā| taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti| abhisambujjhitvā abhisamaitvā ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñāpaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti – ‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’ti| uppādā vā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ anuppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ ṭhitāva sā dhātu dhammaṭṭhitatā dhammaniyāmatā| sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā| taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti| abhisambujjhitvā  abhisamaitvā ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñāpaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti – ‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’ti| uppādā vā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatānaṃ anuppādā vā tathāgatānaṃ ṭhitāva sā dhātu dhammaṭṭhitatā dhammaniyāmatā| sabbai dhammā anattā| taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti| abhisambujjhitvā abhisamaitvā ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñāpaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti – ‘sabbai  dhammā anattā’’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. kaisakambalasuttaṃ

138. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici tantāvutānaṃ vatthānaṃ, kaisakambalau taisaṃ paṭikiṭṭhau akkhāyati| kaisakambalau, bhikkhavai, sītai sītau, uṇhai uṇhau, dubbaṇṇau, duggandhau, dukkhasamphassau| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yāni kānici puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇavādānaṃ samaṇappavādānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) makkhalivādau taisaṃ paṭikiṭṭhau akkhāyati|

‘‘makkhali, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi kammaṃ, natthi kiriyaṃ, natthi vīriya’nti| yaipi  tai, bhikkhavai, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, taipi bhagavantau kammavādā caiva ahaisuṃ kiriyavādā ca vīriyavādā ca| taipi, bhikkhavai, makkhali maughapurisau paṭibāhati – ‘natthi kammaṃ, natthi kiriyaṃ, natthi vīriya’nti| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, taipi bhagavantau kammavādā caiva bhavissanti kiriyavādā ca vīriyavādā ca| taipi, bhikkhavai, makkhali maughapurisau paṭibāhati – ‘natthi kammaṃ, natthi kiriyaṃ, natthi vīriya’nti| ahampi, bhikkhavai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau kammavādau caiva kiriyavādau ca vīriyavādau ca| mampi, bhikkhavai, makkhali maughapurisau paṭibāhati – ‘natthi kammaṃ, natthi kiriyaṃ, natthi vīriya’’’nti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, nadīmukhai khippaṃ uḍḍaiyya auḍḍaiyya (sī॰) bahūnaṃ bahun‍naṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) macchānaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya anayāya byasanāya; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, makkhali maughapurisau manussakhippaṃ mañ‍ñai laukai uppan‍nau bahūnaṃ sattānaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya anayāya  byasanāyā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sampadāsuttaṃ

139. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, sampadā| katamā tissau? saddhāsampadā, sīlasampadā, pañ‍ñāsampadā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau sampadā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vuddhisuttaṃ

140. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vuddhiyau| katamā tissau? saddhāvuddhi, sīlavuddhi, pañ‍ñāvuddhi – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vuddhiyau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. assakhaḷuṅkasuttaṃ

141. ‘‘tayau ca, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkai daisaissāmi tayau ca purisakhaḷuṅkai| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tayau assakhaḷuṅkā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca; na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau assakhaḷuṅkā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tayau purisakhaḷuṅkā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau  javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca; na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau saṃsādaiti  saṃhīraiti (ka॰), nau vissaj‍jaiti| idamassa na vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| na khau pana lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa na ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca; na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| na pana lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa na ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca; na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ  dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| lābhī khau pana hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ  vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau purisakhaḷuṅkā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. assaparassasuttaṃ

142. ‘‘tayau ca, bhikkhavai, assaparassai assasadassai (sī syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) daisaissāmi tayau ca purisaparassai purisasadassai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, tayau assaparassā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assaparassau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assaparassau javasampan‍nau hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca; na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau assaparassā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tayau purisaparassā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisaparassau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca; na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhiviniyai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau saṃsādaiti, nau vissaj‍jaiti| idamassa na vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| na khau pana lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa na ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau javasampan‍nau hauti; na vaṇṇasampan‍nau, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| na khau pana lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa na ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti; vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| lābhī khau pana hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau purisaparassā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. assājānīyasuttaṃ

143. ‘‘tayau ca, bhikkhavai, bhadrai assājānīyai daisaissāmi tayau ca bhadrai purisājānīyai| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, tayau bhadrā assājānīyā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhadrau assājānīyau  …pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau bhadrā assājānīyā|

‘‘katamai ca bhikkhavai, tayau bhadrā purisājānīyā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhadrau purisājānīyau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhadrau purisājānīyau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| lābhī khau pana hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhadrau purisājānīyau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau  ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau bhadrā purisājānīyā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. paṭhamamauranivāpasuttaṃ

144. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati mauranivāpai paribbājakārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ac‍cantaniṭṭhau hauti ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussānaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina, asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ac‍cantaniṭṭhau hauti ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. dutiyamauranivāpasuttaṃ

145. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ac‍cantaniṭṭhau hauti ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī  ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussānaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? iddhipāṭihāriyaina, ādaisanāpāṭihāriyaina, anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi  saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ac‍cantaniṭṭhau hauti ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. tatiyamauranivāpasuttaṃ

146. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ac‍cantaniṭṭhau hauti ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussānaṃ| katamaihi tīhi? sammādiṭṭhiyā, sammāñāṇaina, sammāvimuttiyā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ac‍cantaniṭṭhau  hauti ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti| tairasamaṃ|

yaudhājīvavaggau cuddasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

yaudhau parisamittañ‍ca, uppādā kaisakambalau|

sampadā vuddhi tayau, assā tayau mauranivāpinauti||


(15) 5. maṅgalavaggau

1. akusalasuttaṃ

147. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi tīhi? akusalaina kāyakammaina, akusalaina vacīkammaina, akusalaina manaukammaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi tīhi? kusalaina kāyakammaina, kusalaina vacīkammaina, kusalaina manaukammaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sāvaj‍jasuttaṃ

148. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi tīhi? sāvaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, sāvaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, sāvaj‍jaina manaukammaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi tīhi? anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina – imaihi khau…pai॰… aivaṃ saggai’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. visamasuttaṃ

149. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… visamaina kāyakammaina, visamaina vacīkammaina, visamaina manaukammaina – imaihi khau…pai॰… aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi…pai॰… samaina kāyakammaina, samaina vacīkammaina, samaina manaukammaina – imaihi khau…pai॰… aivaṃ saggai’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. asucisuttaṃ

150. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… asucinā kāyakammaina, asucinā vacīkammaina, asucinā manaukammaina – imaihi khau…pai॰… aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sucinā kāyakammaina, sucinā vacīkammaina, sucinā manaukammaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. paṭhamakhatasuttaṃ

151. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi tīhi? akusalaina kāyakammaina, akusalaina vacīkammaina, akusalaina manaukammaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bālau abyattau asappurisau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| katamaihi tīhi? kusalaina kāyakammaina, kusalaina vacīkammaina, kusalaina manaukammaina…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyakhatasuttaṃ

152. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sāvaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, sāvaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, sāvaj‍jaina manaukammaina…pai॰…|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyakhatasuttaṃ

153. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… visamaina kāyakammaina, visamaina vacīkammaina, visamaina manaukammaina…pai॰…|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… samaina kāyakammaina, samaina vacīkammaina, samaina manaukammaina…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. catutthakhatasuttaṃ

154. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… asucinā kāyakammaina, asucinā vacīkammaina, asucinā manaukammaina…pai॰…|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sucinā kāyakammaina, sucinā vacīkammaina, sucinā manaukammaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau paṇḍitau viyattau sappurisau akkhataṃ anupahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, anavaj‍jau ca hauti ananuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vandanāsuttaṃ

155. ‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, vandanā| katamā tissau? kāyaina, vācāya, manasā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau vandanā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. pubbaṇhasuttaṃ

156. ‘‘yai, bhikkhavai, sattā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ kāyaina sucaritaṃ caranti, vācāya sucaritaṃ caranti, manasā sucaritaṃ caranti, supubbaṇhau, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ sattānaṃ|

‘‘yai, bhikkhavai, sattā majjhanhikasamayaṃ kāyaina sucaritaṃ caranti, vācāya sucaritaṃ caranti, manasā sucaritaṃ caranti, sumajjhanhikau, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ sattānaṃ|

‘‘yai, bhikkhavai, sattā sāyanhasamayaṃ kāyaina sucaritaṃ caranti, vācāya sucaritaṃ caranti, manasā sucaritaṃ caranti, susāyanhau, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ sattāna’’nti|

‘‘sunakkhattaṃ sumaṅgalaṃ, suppabhātaṃ suhuṭṭhitaṃ suvuṭṭhitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)|

sukhaṇau sumuhuttau ca, suyiṭṭhaṃ brahmacārisu||

‘‘padakkhiṇaṃ kāyakammaṃ, vācākammaṃ padakkhiṇaṃ|

padakkhiṇaṃ manaukammaṃ, paṇīdhi tai padakkhiṇai paṇidhiyau padakkhiṇā (sī॰ pī॰), paṇidhi tai padakkhiṇā (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

padakkhiṇāni katvāna, labhantatthai labhatatthai (sī॰ pī॰) padakkhiṇai||

‘‘tai atthaladdhā sukhitā, viruḷhā buddhasāsanai|

araugā sukhitā hautha, saha sabbaihi ñātibhī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

maṅgalavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

akusalañ‍ca sāvaj‍jaṃ, visamāsucinā saha|

caturau khatā vandanā, pubbaṇhaina ca tai dasāti||

tatiyau paṇṇāsakau samattau|



(16) 6. acailakavaggau

157-163. ‘‘tissau  imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā tissau? āgāḷhā paṭipadā, nijjhāmā paṭipadā, majjhimā paṭipadā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, āgāḷhā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau aivaṃvādī hauti aivaṃdiṭṭhi – ‘natthi kāmaisu dausau’ti| sau kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, āgāḷhā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, nijjhāmā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau acailakau hauti muttācārau, hatthāpalaikhanau hatthāvalaikhanau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) dī॰ ni॰ 1.394; ma॰ ni॰ 1.155 passitabbaṃ, na aihibhadantikau, na tiṭṭhabhadantikau, nābhihaṭaṃ na uddissakataṃ na nimantanaṃ sādiyati| sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na kaḷaupimukhā khaḷaupimukhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) paṭiggaṇhāti na aiḷakamantaraṃ na daṇḍamantaraṃ na musalamantaraṃ na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ na gabbhiniyā na pāyamānāya na purisantaragatāya na saṅkittīsu na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī na macchaṃ na maṃsaṃ na suraṃ na mairayaṃ, na thusaudakaṃ pivati| sau aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau, dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau… sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau; aikissāpi dattiyā yāpaiti, dvīhipi dattīhi yāpaiti… sattahipi dattīhi yāpaiti; aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, dvāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti – iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati|

sau sākabhakkhaupi hauti, sāmākabhakkhaupi hauti, nīvārabhakkhaupi hauti, daddulabhakkhaupi hauti, haṭabhakkhaupi hauti, kaṇhabhakkhaupi hauti, ācāmabhakkhaupi hauti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhaupi hauti, tiṇabhakkhaupi hauti, gaumayabhakkhaupi hauti, vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī|

sau sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti, chavadussānipi dhāraiti, paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti, tirīṭānipi dhāraiti, ajinampi dhāraiti, ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti, kusacīrampi dhāraiti, vākacīrampi dhāraiti, phalakacīrampi dhāraiti, kaisakambalampi dhāraiti, vāḷakambalampi  dhāraiti, ulūkapakkhikampi  dhāraiti, kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ubbhaṭṭhakaupi hauti āsanapaṭikkhittau, uk‍kuṭikaupi hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau, kaṇṭakāpassayikaupi hauti kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ kappaiti, sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati – iti aivarūpaṃ anaikavihitaṃ kāyassa ātāpanaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nijjhāmā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau paṭipadā’’ti|

‘‘tissau imā, bhikkhavai, paṭipadā| katamā tissau? āgāḷhā paṭipadā, nijjhāmā paṭipadā, majjhimā paṭipadā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, āgāḷhā paṭipadā…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, āgāḷhā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, nijjhāmā paṭipadā…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, nijjhāmā paṭipadā|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ  janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati…|

‘‘chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰…|

‘‘saddhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti… vīriyindriyaṃ bhāvaiti… satindriyaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhindriyaṃ bhāvaiti… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ bhāvaiti…|

‘‘saddhābalaṃ  bhāvaiti… vīriyabalaṃ bhāvaiti… satibalaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhibalaṃ bhāvaiti… pañ‍ñābalaṃ bhāvaiti…|

‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…|

‘‘sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti… sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāvaiti… sammāvācaṃ bhāvaiti… sammākammantaṃ bhāvaiti … sammāājīvaṃ bhāvaiti… sammāvāyāmaṃ bhāvaiti… sammāsatiṃ bhāvaiti… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti…| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, majjhimā paṭipadā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, tissau paṭipadā’’ti|

acailakavaggau chaṭṭhau|


tassuddānaṃ –

satipaṭṭhānaṃ sammappadhānaṃ, iddhipādindriyaina ca|

balaṃ baujjhaṅgau maggau ca, paṭipadāya yaujayaiti||


(17) 7. kammapathapaiyyālaṃ

164-183. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi tīhi? attanā ca pāṇātipātī hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātai samādapaiti, pāṇātipātai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi tīhi? attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca adin‍nādāyī hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānai samādapaiti, adin‍nādānai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā  ca kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārai  samādapaiti, kāmaisumicchācārai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca musāvādī hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādai samādapaiti, musāvādai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā  ca musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca musāvādā  vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, musāvādā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca pisuṇavācau hauti, parañ‍ca pisuṇāya vācāya samādapaiti, pisuṇāya vācāya ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca pharusavācau hauti, parañ‍ca pharusāya vācāya samādapaiti, pharusāya vācāya ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca samphappalāpī hauti, parañ‍ca samphappalāpai samādapaiti, samphappalāpai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, parañ‍ca samphappalāpā vairamaṇiyā samādapaiti, samphappalāpā vairamaṇiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca abhijjhālu hauti, parañ‍ca abhijjhāya samādapaiti, abhijjhāya ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca anabhijjhālu hauti, parañ‍ca anabhijjhāya samādapaiti, anabhijjhāya ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā  ca byāpan‍nacittau hauti, parañ‍ca byāpādai samādapaiti, byāpādai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, parañ‍ca abyāpādai samādapaiti, abyāpādai ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti…|

‘‘attanā ca micchādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti, micchādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti …|

‘‘attanā ca sammādiṭṭhikau hauti, parañ‍ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapaiti, sammādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñ‍ñau hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti|

kammapathapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

pāṇaṃ adin‍namicchā ca, musāvādī ca pisuṇā|

pharusā samphappalāpau ca, abhijjhā byāpādadiṭṭhi ca|

kammapathaisu paiyyālaṃ, tikakaina niyaujayaiti||


(18) 8. rāgapaiyyālaṃ

184. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? suñ‍ñatau samādhi, animittau samādhi, appaṇihitau samādhi – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| ( ) (rāgassa bhikkhavai abhiñ‍ñāya tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai tayau? savitak‍kasavicārau samādhi, avitak‍kavicāramattau samādhi, avitak‍kaavicārau samādhi| rāgassa bhikkhavai abhiñ‍ñāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā|) aitthantarai pāṭhau katthaci dissati, aṭṭhakathāyaṃ passitabbau

‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñāya…pai॰… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā|

‘‘dausassa… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa… makkhassa… palāsassa… issāya… macchariyassa… māyāya… sāṭhaiyyassa… thambhassa… sārambhassa… mānassa… atimānassa… madassa… pamādassa abhiñ‍ñāya… pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya… cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya imai tayau dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

(idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhau syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰ pautthakaisu na dissati

rāgapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

imā uddānagāthāyau sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu na dissanti rāgaṃ dausañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca, kaudhūpanāhapañ‍camaṃ|

makkhapaḷāsaissā ca, maccharimāyāsāṭhaiyyā||

thambhasārambhamānañ‍ca, atimānamadassa ca|

pamādā sattarasa vuttā, rāgapaiyyālanissitā||

aitai  aupammayuttaina, āpādaina abhiñ‍ñāya|

pariñ‍ñāya parikkhayā, pahānakkhayabbayaina|

virāganiraudhacāgaṃ, paṭinissaggai imai dasa||

suñ‍ñatau animittau ca, appaṇihitau ca tayau|

samādhimūlakā paiyyālaisupi vavatthitā cāti||

tikanipātapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
